- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Jerry
Aliases: Jerry NPC, Jerry The Assistant, Jerry the Assistant
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Jerry, Mayor Election
[Jerry]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Getting Started ([[Quests]]); Crop 'n Profit ([[Quests]])
[Locations]
Description: Introduces the player to SkyBlock, and allows the player to view patch notes and claim deliveries.
Location: [[Private Island]]

Jerry is an [[NPC]] located on the player's [[Private Island]], and acts as a guide when the player begins a new profile. He offers tools for island management, store deliveries and shows the latest patch notes.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Introduction|[NPC] Jerry: Your SkyBlock island is part of a much larger universe. [NPC] Jerry: The SkyBlock universe is full of islands to explore and resources to discover! [NPC] Jerry: Use the Portal to warp to the first of those islands - the SkyBlock Hub!|
|In [[Village]]|[NPC] Jerry: It's the holiday season in SkyBlock and that can only mean one thing... [NPC] Jerry: Jerry's Workshop is under attack! [NPC] Jerry: Save the Jerrys' home and ensure all of SkyBlock gets to receive Gifts!|
|☀ Stranded ([[Stranded]]) Introduction|[NPC] Jerry: Your SkyBlock island is all you have! [NPC] Jerry: Progress as far as you can by killing mobs, gaining collection, and using minions. [NPC] Jerry: There is no escape...you are stranded!|
|☀ Stranded ([[Stranded]]) Tips; Not enough coins|[NPC] Jerry: My expertise doesn't come for free!|
|☀ Stranded ([[Stranded]]) Tips|[NPC] Jerry: Villagers tend to have sweet trades! [NPC] Jerry: Did you know Slimes can spawn near the void? [NPC] Jerry: Cocoa Beans only grow on one type of tree! [NPC] Jerry: Shiny creatures can drop shiny things! [NPC] Jerry: Rabbits can spawn in different biomes! [NPC] Jerry: I've heard that scary monsters can attack your island sometime in Autumn. [NPC] Jerry: The only way to obtain Emeralds is to trade for them! [NPC] Jerry: Pigmen are great at hoarding wealth. [NPC] Jerry: This place desperately needs more Iron Golems! [NPC] Jerry: In my opinion, Magma Cubes are just fiery slimes. [NPC] Jerry: Villagers tend to have cool trades!|

## Trivia
- In a forum post released by Hypixel, Jerry was revealed to be an immortal entity of infinite power. Apparently, the reason that he would sometimes duplicate was that they were struggling to contain his power and the dev team refused to fix it because they feared for their lives. The forum post mysteriously vanished within 5 minutes of it being posted.
- Jerry tended to multiply before update v0.7.4, with many of them appearing at random on the Private Island. As of v0.7.4, all of them have played a "Battle Royale," according to 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] Jayavarmen in the patch notes, leaving only one Jerry on the private island. There is still a bug in which Jerry could occasionally duplicate, but it would be removed when traveling to another server.
- As an April Fool's joke, Jerry could move freely on [[Private Island|The Private Island]], all player skins changed to Jerry, and Move Jerrys had the description of the [[Quality Map]].
- Jerry was added in another Hypixel minigame, Bedwars, where one trades him Emeralds for Lucky Blocks.
- If Jerry falls into the void, he will attempt to respawn in a safe location.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jan 30, 2026|Moved Jerry in the [[Village]] from -3, 70, -47 ([[Coordinates]]) to -33.5, 69, 7.5 ([[Coordinates]]), due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 26, 2019|0.4|Added The Guide.|
|Jul 2, 2019|0.5|Renamed to Jerry.|
|Jul 2, 2019|0.5|Will respawn upon death.; Can be moved using an egg.; Give updates on SkyBlock patches and deliveries.; Change the Private Island biome.|
|Jul 8, 2019|Fixed an issue where Jerry would steal all the items if he spawned on a chest.|
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Jerry now has a Guesting Menu, where you can find info and also access your Settings.|
|Jul 15, 2019|Fixed being able to place Jerry eggs in water on public islands.|
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Added the ability to remove Hub Portal ([[Portals]]) by talking to Jerry.|
|Aug 15, 2019|Fix Jerry's menu opening whenever you shoot it with a projectile.|
|Sep 11, 2019|0.7.1|If you manually broke the Hub Portal ([[Portals]]) with previous methods you may now purchase a Hub Portal from Jerry.|
|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Added Jerry to [[Jerry's Workshop]].|
|Mar 12, 2020|In the [[Dark Auction]], if you put a winning bid and leave, you will win the item and it will be delivered to your Jerry.|
|Mar 12, 2020|If you put a losing bid and leave, you will be refunded in Jerry.|
|Jun 15, 2020|You can view SkyBlock [[Achievements]] more easily in Jerry.|
|Jan 18, 2022|0.12.2|Added tips for ☀ Stranded ([[Stranded]]) gamemode.|
|Mar 24, 2023|Made Jerry's name bold.|
|Jun 21, 2023|Added a message above Jerry pointing out where to claim lost & found items.|
|Aug 29, 2023|Fixed Jerry's name not being capitalized when picking up deliveries before interacting with him.|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Moved Jerry in the [[Village]] from -3, 70, -47 ([[Coordinates]]) to -33.5, 69, 7.5 ([[Coordinates]]), due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|

# Jimmy
Categories: Non-player characters
[Jimmy]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Safety First ([[Quests]])
Description: Tries to steal from the player during [[Brigette]]'s account security quest.
Location: [[Security Hall]]

Jimmy is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Security Hall]]. He appears as a "recruit" while [[Brigette]] talks about account security, but is quickly detained after admitting to fighting the [[Security Sloth]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before the player has to speak to him|[NPC] Jimmy: I'm just checking out the security here. It's top notch!|
|Once the player has to speak to him|[NPC] Jimmy: Hi, the name's Jimmy. Select an option: [Hello, Jimmy.]|
|Selecting [Hello, Jimmy.]|[NPC] Jimmy: This place looks awesome. I'm all about security. Select an option: [Security is indeed very important.]|
|Selecting [Security is indeed very important.]|[NPC] Jimmy: I got these really cool gauntlets. It lets me fight against baddies. Select an option: [Cool.]|
|Selecting [Cool.]|Jimmy glances at the player. [NPC] Jimmy: You don't look quite ready for a security job. Where's your gear, bro? [NPC] Jimmy: Say, would you like to trade for my gauntlets? [NPC] Jimmy: It will allow you to punch well above your weight. [NPC] Jimmy: For the lowly price of 999 coins, they can be yours. [NPC] Jimmy: If you're down for it, we can trade outside the Security Hall. [NPC] Jimmy: Deal? Select an option: [Deal.]|
|Selecting [Deal.]|[NPC] Jimmy: Great, we're now besties! [NPC] Jimmy: Say, how did you end up here? Select an option: [I volunteered to be part of the Safety Squad.]|
|Selecting [I volunteered to be part of the Safety Squad.]|[NPC] Jimmy: That's cool. I got here because I got into a scuffle with the Security Sloth. [NPC] Jimmy: Man, I was so close to defeating the big guy, can't believe his backup came so fast! Select an option: [Wait, what?]|
|Selecting [Wait, what?]|[NPC] Brigette: STOP RIGHT THERE! [NPC] Brigette: That's not a recruit, he's supposed to be a detainee! [NPC] Jimmy: Aw shucks, I was so close! [NPC] Jimmy: I'll try again next time. [NPC] Jimmy: You haven't seen the last of me! [NPC] Brigette: I'm so sorry about putting you in danger like that. [NPC] Brigette: Are you alright? Select an option: [What just happened?]|

Jonesy is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Backwater Bayou]] that shows the [[Chapters]] for the island.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Jonesy: We don't get many new faces around these parts... [NPC] Jonesy: Welcome, sailor, to our lil' Backwater Bayou! [NPC] Jonesy: We've got trash, junk, and everything in between! [NPC] Jonesy: I'm here to guide you through what our corner of SkyBlock has to offer!|

Begin your journey through the Seven Seas.:

# Jotraeline Greatforge
Aliases: Greatforge
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Dwarven Mines
[Jotraeline Greatforge]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Allows the player to upgrade their [[Drills]].
Location: [[Forge Basin]]
Coordinates: -6.5 145 -18.5
Jotraeline Greatforge is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Forge Basin]] of the [[Dwarven Mines]], who tells the player about [[Drills]] and allows them to modify them.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: Sup, [player]? I am Jotraeline Greatforge, your one stop shop for refueling and upgrading Drills. [NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: Drills are like pickaxes, but better! They are powered with Drill Fuel, which comes in many different forms. [NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: Biofuel is one of such fuels, which applies 3,000 fuel to your Drill! [NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: You can also apply Drill Upgrades to Drills, offering various buffs!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: ✆ You have an Abiphone! I've always wanted an Abiphone Basic! [NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: ✆ I could tinker with it while I wait here... [NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: ✆ And if I had one, I would totally add you! [GIVE ABIPHONE]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Trying to give the player's only Abiphone ([[Abiphones]])|[NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: ✆ Hey it's very nice of you, but I can't take your ONLY Abiphone!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Giving Abiphone Basic ([[Abiphones]])|[NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: ✆ Oooh an Abiphone, thank you so much! [NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: ✆ Now I can finally add Cole, and you!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Giving a Different Abiphone ([[Abiphones]])|[NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: ✆ Uh... do you have any cheaper phone? [NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: ✆ Sure I'd like you in my Contacts List, but I don't want this kind of burden on our relationship. [NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: ✆ I only want an Abiphone Basic.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: I heard you have a composter in your barn, it happens to run on the same fuel drills run on! Could I check it out?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: Quite fascinating technology you have there, I'm sure with this machinery we could create even better appliances in the future!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: That's alright! I'll just save up 12 years to be able to afford one myself.|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|Without a Drill ([[Drills]])|[NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: Drills are sweet! Too bad you don't have one. I hear you can create them in the Forge!|
|Without a Drill ([[Drills]])|[NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: Come back to me when you have a Drill in your inventory!|
|Without a Drill ([[Drills]])|[NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: What are you doing talking to me without a Drill? Come back when you have one!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: ✆ Pro Drill Maintenance, Jotraeline speaking. [NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: ✆ Yes of course we can upgrade your drill.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: ✆ Pro Drill Maintenance, Jotraeline speaking. [NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: ✆ Your drill is still under warranty, yes.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: ✆ Pro Drill Maintenance, Jotraeline speaking. [NPC] Jotraeline Greatforge: ✆ I wish I ever had vacations.|
## Trivia
- Jotraeline Greatforge could be related to [[Geonathan Greatforge]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]], due to them sharing the same surname.

# Julian
Categories: Non-player characters
[Julian]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Talks about how they are looking for Precursors.
Location: [[Oubliette]]
Coordinates: 207 81.781 130.281
Julian is an [[NPC]] in the [[Oubliette]]. They talk about how they are looking for Precursors.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Julian: If you've made it this far, you're talented. [NPC] Julian: Me? [NPC] Julian: I'm trying to solve one of the deepest mysteries of our world. [NPC] Julian: I'm looking for traces of the Precursors, but there's none anywhere. [NPC] Julian: Zip! [NPC] Julian: Nada! [NPC] Julian: Not even anywhere in the Rift! [NPC] Julian: If they're trying to return to our world one day, shouldn't they come through here? [NPC] Julian: Oruo isn't even here. [NPC] Julian: But I know for a fact he acquired the Blessing of Time right here, in the Château! [NPC] Julian: So many questions left unanswered...|
## Trivia
- [[Elise (Rift)|Elise]] says the following dialogue about Julian: [NPC] Elise: Have you encountered Julian? [NPC] Elise: He's been spotted in the Rift on several occasions. [NPC] Elise: While he may seem friendly, be cautious. [NPC] Elise: Those in his organization have committed questionable acts. [NPC] Elise: One doesn't reach his rank without crossing a few lines.
- Julian is one of a few NPCs who do not originate from the [[Rift Dimension]], alongside [[Dr. Emmett]], [[Barry]], [[Alatar]] and [[Tel Kar]].
- Based on his appearance, he may come from [[Scarleton]] in the [[Crimson Isle]].
- Julian is mentioned by [[Shen Magnum Opus|Shen Magnum Opus']] in their dialogue, along with [[Maeve]] and Seth.

# Juliette
Aliases: Juliet

Juliette is an [[NPC]] located in [[The Park]], on a separate island located in the jungle portion at the very top of [[The Park]].
Talking to her will start the [[Romero and Juliette]] quest, requiring the player to find [[Romero]] in different locations to give him items.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Completing a step but not holding gift|[NPC] Juliette: You found Romero? [NPC] Juliette: Did he have anything for me? [NPC] Juliette: (Hold Romero's gift to progress)|

|Initiate step|[NPC] Juliette: Nice to meet you! [NPC] Juliette: Could you find my dear Romero? [NPC] Juliette: He's in a cave somewhere under the Savanna Woodland. [NPC] Juliette: ... [NPC] Juliette: Oh! [NPC] Juliette: He wears glasses and blends well in a crowd!|
|Completion|[NPC] Juliette: You found Romero? That's weird, I just saw him! [NPC] Juliette: He wanted me to have this? So sweet! [NPC] Juliette: ... [NPC] Juliette: A yellow rock? What's the point of that? [NPC] Juliette: Here, you keep it! [NPC] Juliette: Since you're here, can you find Romero again? [NPC] Juliette: He said he was getting groceries at the downtown markets.|
[Quest Step 2 (Flower House)]
|Initiate step|[NPC] Juliette: Since you're here, can you find Romero again? [NPC] Juliette: He said he was getting groceries at the downtown markets.|
|Completion|[NPC] Juliette: Romero? He just came over with the groceries. [NPC] Juliette: These flowers are for me? [NPC] Juliette: That's so kind of you! [NPC] Juliette: You know I'm with Romero, right? [NPC] Juliette: Dear, sorry for the inconvenience... [NPC] Juliette: Can you please find Romero again? [NPC] Juliette: He's at some sort of emerald altar... Your Shiny Yellow Rock was upgraded to Yellow Rock of Love!|
[Quest Step 3 (Graveyard)]
|Initiate step|[NPC] Juliette: Dear, sorry for the inconvenience... [NPC] Juliette: Can you please find Romero again? [NPC] Juliette: He's at some sort of emerald altar...|
|Completion|[NPC] Juliette: Oof! Why did you bring this all the way here? [NPC] Juliette: How is that a gift? [NPC] Juliette: Just keep it! I don't want this! [NPC] Juliette: What I want is Romero! [NPC] Juliette: For some reason, he's now looking for... [NPC] Juliette: The largest, most evil patch of fungus ever! Your Yellow Rock of Love was upgraded to Mediocre Ring of Love!|
[Quest Step 4 (Crimson Isle)]
|Initiate step|[NPC] Juliette: What I want is Romero! [NPC] Juliette: For some reason, he's now looking for... [NPC] Juliette: The largest, most evil patch of fungus ever!|
|Completion|[NPC] Juliette: Stew? I love stew! [NPC] Juliette: ... [NPC] Juliette: !!! [NPC] Juliette: Yuck!!! [NPC] Juliette: Yuck!!! [NPC] Juliette: Yuck!!! [NPC] Juliette: Yuck!!! [NPC] Juliette: Yuck!!! [NPC] Juliette: DISGUSTING! [NPC] Juliette: Is that a stew from hell? [NPC] Juliette: Maybe Romero should give you cooking lessons! Your Mediocre Ring of Love was upgraded to Rubbish Ring of Love! [NPC] Juliette: Oh! I love Romero! He has so many skills. [NPC] Juliette: Did you know he likes photography? [NPC] Juliette: Just now, he went to take pictures of the old castle. [NPC] Juliette: Go find him!|
[Quest Step 5 (Mountain)]
|Initiate step|[NPC] Juliette: Oh! I love Romero! He has so many skills. [NPC] Juliette: Did you know he likes photography? [NPC] Juliette: Just now, he went to take pictures of the old castle. [NPC] Juliette: Go find him!|
|Completion|[NPC] Juliette: You really believe that this is from the Moon? [NPC] Juliette: How? Someone flew up there? [NPC] Juliette: Thanks, I guess. [NPC] Juliette: Actually, I heard Romero flew close to the Sun. [NPC] Juliette: Sounds more like a metaphor, like... [NPC] Juliette: He went to where gold is smelted. [NPC] Juliette: Please find him, wherever that is! Your Rubbish Ring of Love was upgraded to Modest Ring of Love!|
[Quest Step 6 (Gold Mine)]
|Initiate step|[NPC] Juliette: Actually, I heard Romero flew close to the Sun. [NPC] Juliette: Sounds more like a metaphor, like... [NPC] Juliette: He went to where gold is smelted. [NPC] Juliette: Please find him, wherever that is!|
|Completion|[NPC] Juliette: This is a wonderful gift! [NPC] Juliette: I will keep this one for myself. [NPC] Juliette: Sometimes, some things have to remain a mystery. [NPC] Juliette: About something not so secret, can you get in touch with Romero? [NPC] Juliette: He told me he's in his home. Your Modest Ring of Love was upgraded to Refined Ring of Love!|
[Quest Step 7 (Wilderness)]
|Initiate step|[NPC] Juliette: About something not so secret, can you get in touch with Romero? [NPC] Juliette: He told me he's in his home.|
|Completion|[NPC] Juliette: Uh? Romero solved his cube? [NPC] Juliette: That's unbelievable. [NPC] Juliette: No, really, I don't believe it at all. |Initiate step|[NPC] Juliette: I don't know why Romero is trying to be so unlike himself lately. [NPC] Juliette: I like him just the way he is! [NPC] Juliette: He says he's getting equipment for a duel. [NPC] Juliette: Can you find Romero before he hurts himself?|
|Completion|[NPC] Juliette: This is a wonderful poem. [NPC] Juliette: But, does he really think there's someone else? [NPC] Juliette: I can't possibly imagine why he'd think that. [NPC] Juliette: Romero went to meditate at his retreat. [NPC] Juliette: It's the smallest house in SkyBlock. [NPC] Juliette: Please find him, I'm worried! Your Classy Ring of Love was upgraded to Exquisite Ring of Love!|
[Quest Step 9 (Mushroom Desert)]
|No condition|[NPC] Juliette: Romero went to meditate at his retreat. [NPC] Juliette: It's the smallest house in SkyBlock. [NPC] Juliette: Please find him, I'm worried!|
[Quest Step 9 (Complete)]
|No condition|[NPC] Juliette: Another gift from Romero? [NPC] Juliette: Thank you for delivering it. [NPC] Juliette: I don't know how Romero has such hard a time understanding that I LOVE HIM. [NPC] Juliette: I like him just the way he is! [NPC] Juliette: I don't need tons of gifts to know that.|
[Quest Step 10 (Jungle Island)]
|Before wearing suit|[NPC] Juliette: It's decided, this is the day! [NPC] Juliette: We're making it official!|
|After wearing suit|[NPC] Juliette: We can't thank you enough for bringing us together! [NPC] Juliette: You're the best, <player>!|
[After Quest]
|No condition (Juliette emits heart particles during this dialogue)|[NPC] Juliette: I love Romero!|
## Trivia
- Both Romero and Juliette are a reference to the main characters in the play Romeo and Juliet by w:William Shakespeare.

# Kalhuiki Door Guardian
Categories: Non-player characters, Crystal Hollows
[Kalhuiki Door Guardian]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Trial of the Jungle ([[Quests]])
Description: Allows the player to access the [[Jungle Temple]] parkour by presenting a [[Jungle Key]], so they can obtain the Amethyst Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]).
Location: [[Jungle Temple]]
Kalhuiki Door Guardians are two [[NPC|NPCs]] found outside the [[Jungle Temple]] in the [[Jungle]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]]. When bringing a [[Jungle Key]] to either Door Guardian, the door to the [[Jungle Temple]] will open, allowing access to the parkour inside, and the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amethyst Crystal]].
## Dialogue
- Grammar and/or punctuation mistakes are intentional, as they appear so in the dialogue in-game.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Without a [[Jungle Key]]|[NPC] Kalhuiki Door Guardian: This temple is locked, you will need to bring me a key open the door!|
|With a [[Jungle Key]]|[NPC] Kalhuiki Door Guardian: A Jungle Key! I will open the door for you.|
|Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]) already obtained|[NPC] Kalhuiki Door Guardian: This temple does not have the treasure you are seeking at this time.|
## Trivia
- This is the only way to claim the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amethyst Crystal]]. It is possible to complete the parkour by breaking into the temple, but the crystal will be locked.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 30, 2021|AHN 0.12 Round 1|Added Kalhuiki Door Guardian.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Kalhuiki Door Guardian.|

# Keepers of Divan
Aliases: Keeper of Diamond, Keeper of Emerald, Keeper of Gold, Keeper of Lapis, Keepers, Keepers Of Divan

[Keepers of Divan]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Divan's Quest ([[Quests]])
Description: Accepts the Scavenged Lapis Sword ([[Scavenged Items]]) from the player so they can obtain the Jade Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]).
Location: [[Mines of Divan]]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Divan's Quest ([[Quests]])
Description: Accepts the Scavenged Golden Hammer ([[Scavenged Items]]) from the player so they can obtain the Jade Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]).
Location: [[Mines of Divan]]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Divan's Quest ([[Quests]])
Description: Accepts the Scavenged Diamond Axe ([[Scavenged Items]]) from the player so they can obtain the Jade Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]).
Location: [[Mines of Divan]]
Type: [[NPC]]

Keepers of Divan are [[NPC|NPCs]] that can be found in the [[Mines of Divan]]. Giving [[Scavenged Items]] to them will allow the player to obtain the [[Gemstone Crystals|Jade Crystal]]. Talking to the first Keeper will give the player a [[Metal Detector]].
|Icon|Item|Associated; Keeper|

|[[Scavenged Items]]|Scavenged Golden Hammer|Keeper of Gold|
|[[Scavenged Items]]|Scavenged Diamond Axe|Keeper of Diamond|
|[[Scavenged Items]]|Scavenged Emerald Hammer|Keeper of Emerald|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction|[NPC] Keeper of <Ore>: Take this Metal Detector to scavenge the ground for lost items! [NPC] Keeper of <Ore>: Return the items to their rightful owners to gain a reward!|
|Before finding items|[NPC] Keeper of <Ore>: Talk to me when you have found a Scavenged <Item>.|
|When returning a Scavenged Item|[NPC] Keeper of <Ore>: Excellent! You have returned the Scavenged <Item> to it's rightful place! [NPC] Keeper of <Ore>: Use your Metal Detector to find the remaining items!|
|After returning the Scavenged Item|[NPC] Keeper of <Ore>: You have already restored this Dwarf's Scavenged <Item>! [NPC] Keeper of <Ore>: Use your Metal Detector to find the rest of the items!|
|When returning the last Scavenged Item|[NPC] Keeper of <Ore>: You found all of the items! Behold... the Jade Crystal!|
|Before placing Jade Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]) in [[Crystal Nucleus]]|[NPC] Keeper of <Ore>: You haven't placed the Jade Crystal yet! [NPC] Keeper of <Ore>: Go to the Crystal Nucleus to place it!|
|After placing Jade Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]) in [[Crystal Nucleus]]|[NPC] Keeper of <Ore>: You have already placed the Jade Crystal! [NPC] Keeper of <Ore>: Come back when you need to collect it again!|

# Kelly

Kelly is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Spruce Woods]]. They award the player with [[Kelly's T-Shirt]] after collecting 128 [[Spruce Log|Spruce Logs]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|

# King
Aliases: Dwarven King, Erren, Thormyr
Categories: Non-player characters, Dwarven Mines
[King]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Introduces the player to [[Commissions]].
Location: [[Royal Palace]]
Coordinates: 129.5 196 196.5
The King is one of a group of seven [[NPC|NPCs]] that are located in the [[Royal Palace]] within the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Purpose
There are seven NPCs that can be King, and at any given time, only one of them is the King. The King changes at the the end of each SkyBlock Day (every 20 minutes real time). Speaking to the King for the first time will allow the player to start [[Commissions]]. Speaking to all seven different Kings during their reigns rewards the player with a [[King Talisman]] and the [[Achievements|"A Royal Meeting" Achievement]]. It is not possible to view which Kings the player has spoken to, but the player is told in chat how many different Kings they have spoken to each time they speak to a King that they haven't already spoken to. The seven NPCs always rotate counter-clockwise in their seats, which allows the order of the next Kings to be determined. The cycle is as follows:
- King Brammor
- King Emkam
- King Redros
- King Erren
- King Thormyr
- King Emmor
- King Grandan
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Speaking to current King First interaction|[NPC] King <name>: Hello Adventurer! [NPC] King <name>: I see you've found yourself in my cavernous kingdom. [NPC] King <name>: All work here is rewarded, complete commissions and I will bestow you with riches!|
|Speaking to another NPC at the table None|[NPC] <name>: Are you looking for the King? [NPC] <name>: I'm not the King at the moment, I am but his humble servant. [NPC] <name>: Today the King is <name>. [NPC] <name>: He's right here at this table!|
## Trivia
- According to [[Tornora]], "There isn't much going on in our caves", so "Most of their time is spent trying to decide who's going to be the next king".
- [[Queen Mismyla]] is the queen regardless of who the King is.
  - Her dialogue implies that she isn't fond of any of them, though she appears to like some more than others.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added King|

# King Yolkar
Aliases: Goblin King, Yolkar
Categories: Non-player characters, Crystal Hollows
[King Yolkar]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Royal Goblins ([[Quests]])
Description: Applies the [[King's Scent]] effect to the player in exchange for 1 [[Goblin Egg]], so they can obtain the Amber Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]).
Location: [[Goblin Holdout]]
King Yolkar is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Goblin Holdout]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. When talking to him and giving him 1 [[Goblin Egg]], he will give the player the [[Potions|King's Scent]] [[Potions|Potion Effect]] for 20 minutes, allowing the player to sneak past the Goblin Queen's guards into the [[Goblin Queen's Den]] to obtain the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amber Crystal]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] King Yolkar: *rumble* *rumble* [NPC] King Yolkar: Did ya hear that, [player]? That's the sound of an empty stomach. [NPC] King Yolkar: I've been so hungry since the Goblin Queen tossed me aside. [NPC] King Yolkar: Bring me back a Goblin Egg of any type and we can teach her a lesson!|
|Without Goblin Eggs ([[Goblin Egg]])|[NPC] King Yolkar: Bring me any type of Goblin Egg and we can show the Goblin Queen what it's like to lose something she loves?! [NPC] King Yolkar: Dark, right?!|
|Incorrect item|[NPC] King Yolkar: That is certainly not the meal I am looking for! Bring me back a Goblin Egg and you will satiate my hunger. [NPC] King Yolkar: Where is that Goblin Egg? My Chef is waiting!|
|Bringing 1 [[Goblin Egg]]|[NPC] King Yolkar: Well done, [player]! [NPC] King Yolkar: These eggs will help me stomach my pain. [NPC] King Yolkar: Now let's hit the Queen where it hurts - by stealing her Amber Crystal! [NPC] King Yolkar: I'm covering you in my foul stench as we speak. It won't last long before it dissipates! [NPC] King Yolkar: Get to the Queen before my stench goes away and you'll be able to sneak past her imbecile Guards!|
|After given King's Scent ([[Potions]])|[NPC] King Yolkar: You must hurry! Get to the Goblin Queen before my stench escapes you! [NPC] King Yolkar: And stay away from water!|
|After your first [[Crystal Nucleus]] run|[NPC] King Yolkar: You need my foul stench again? Bring me a Goblin Egg of any type!|
## Trivia
- King Yolkar's name may be based off of the word yolk, the inner part of an egg, which may explain his constant hunger for Goblin Eggs.
- The [[Potions|King's Scent]] effect can be toggled on and off using [[Booster Cookie]].
- The [[Potions|King's Scent]] effect will disappear when a player with this effect goes into water in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
  - Touching water with the [[Potions|King's Scent]] effect outside the Crystal Hollows will not remove the effect.
- Water in the Crystal Hollows indicates that King Yolkar is nearby, so players can search accordingly.

# Krevius
Categories: Non-player characters, Crimson Isle
[Krevius]
Type: [[NPC]]
[Locations]

Krevius is an [[NPC]] and guard to [[Queen Nyx]], who primarily guards the [[Throne Room]] with [[Grelius]]. They also briefly appear in other places on the [[Crimson Isle]], twice during the [[Factions|Mage Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]] and once at the end of both [[Factions|Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questlines]].
## Quests
Krevius prevents the player from entering the [[Throne Room]] until you present a [[Letter of Recommendation]] during the [[Factions|Mage Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]].
Later in this questline, they appear with [[Grelius]] and [[Captain Ahone]] in the [[Burning Desert]] when you call Captain Ahone about the [[Apprentice (Crimson Isle)|Apprentice]]. They also appear with the two in the [[Mage Council]] when discussing stolen [[Sulphur]].
At the end of both [[Factions|Faction]] quests, they appear in the [[Forgotten Skull]] with [[Grelius]], [[Captain Ahone]], [[Elle]], [[Ugo]], and two [[Piglin Guard|Piglin Guards]].

|Selecting [I have a letter of recommendation]|[NPC] Krevius: FINE, YOU MAY PASS! [NPC] Grelius: FINE, YOU MAY PASS!|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|

|None|[NPC] Krevius: WHAT DO YOU WANT? [NPC] Grelius: WHAT DO YOU WANT?|
|None|[NPC] Krevius: WE HAVE OUR EYES ON YOU. [NPC] Grelius: WE HAVE OUR EYES ON YOU.|
## Trivia
- If the player selects the [I'm here to cause trouble, of course] option, they will be killed, with the death message: ☠ You got pulverised by Krevius and Grelius.

Kutral is an [[NPC]] in the [[Dragontail]] brewery that talks to [[Nall]] about new [[Abiphones]].
## Keypad Code
By standing at [[Coordinates|-655, 107, -741]], the player can overhear Kutral and [[Nall]]'s conversation, which includes a 4-digit code. The code can be entered at the Keypad located at [[Coordinates|-678, 107, -736]], to access the secret room in which [[Nuvian]] sells [[Abiphones]]. The code changes every day.

# Kuudra Archeologist
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Article stubs, Crimson Isle
[Kuudra Archeologist]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Allows the player to obtain the Kuudra Follower Leggings ([[Kuudra Follower Armor]]) under certain conditions.
Location: [[Dragontail]]
Coordinates: -570.5 111 -642.5
Kuudra Archeologist is an [[NPC]] found on [[Dragontail]]. They appear in [[Dragontail]] after the [[Player]] defeats [[Kuudra]] for the first time.
## Purpose
The coordinates for the [[Kuudra Relic]] can be obtained by entering a 2 and a 22, obtained from pulling out a book in the [[Cathedral]], into the equation given by Kuudra Archeologist.
Bringing Kuudra Archeologist a [[Kuudra Relic]] will award the player with [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Kuudra Follower Leggings]].

# Kuudra Believer
Categories: Non-player characters, Article stubs
[Kuudra Believer]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Rewards the player with the Kuudra Follower Helmet ([[Kuudra Follower Armor]]) for donating a specific amount of coins.
Location: [[Plhlegblast Pool]]
Coordinates: -390.5 81 -701.5
The Kuudra Believer is an [[NPC]] found on the [[Crimson Isle]], who asks for players to donate [[Coins]] if they believe in the return of [[Kuudra]]. They allow the player to obtain [[Herring the Fish]] and the [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Kuudra Follower Helmet]], as well as access the [[Weird Sailor]].
## Quest
The Kuudra Believer will appear in the [[Plhlegblast Pool]] after the [[Player]] defeats [[Kuudra]] for the first time. Donated [[Coins]] will not get refunded.
Depending on the amount of donated coins and other conditions, different things will happen:
- If a player donates 4,202,022 [[Coins]] while a [[Plhlegblast]] is within 3 blocks, they will receive [[Herring the Fish]].
- If a player donates at least 1,000,000 [[Coins]] and enters [[Kuudra's Hollow]] within 30 minutes of donating the [[Coins]], the [[Weird Sailor]] will appear at [[Coordinates|-131, 93, -177]].
- If a player donates [[Kuudra Follower Armor|a certain amount of coins]] once they have obtained the [[Wet Napkin]], as well as a [[Plhlegblast]] is within 3 blocks of the NPC, they will receive a [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Kuudra Follower Helmet]].

|Offering 100,000 [[Coins]]|[NPC] Kuudra Believer: What a great offering. Kuudra will be pleased.|
|Offering 1,000,000 [[Coins]]|[NPC] Kuudra Believer: Wow. [NPC] Kuudra Believer: You truly are a believer. [NPC] Kuudra Believer: Kuudra will bless you.|
|Offering either 4,202,022 [[Coins]] or the amount needed ([[Kuudra Follower Armor]]) for Kuudra Follower Helmet ([[Kuudra Follower Armor]]) while a [[Plhlegblast]] is nearby|[NPC] Kuudra Believer: Wow. [NPC] Kuudra Believer: You truly are a believer. [NPC] Kuudra Believer: Kuudra will bless you. [NPC] Kuudra Believer: ADVENTURER [NPC] Kuudra Believer: it seems like Kuudra has shown favour upon you! [NPC] Kuudra Believer: This item surfaced from the lavapool|

# Kuudra Loremaster
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Article stubs, Crimson Isle
Kuudra Loremaster in the Blazing Fortress
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
[Kuudra Loremaster]
Type: [[NPC]]
[Locations]
Description: Hints at the possibility of meeting their past self.
Location: [[Scarleton]]
Coordinates: -106.5 126 -817.5
Description: Rewards the player with Kuudra Follower Boots ([[Kuudra Follower Armor]]) under certain conditions.
Location: [[Deep Caverns]]
Coordinates: -256.5 146 120.5
The Kuudra Loremaster is an [[NPC]] located in [[Scarleton]] and the [[Deep Caverns]]'s distant [[Blazing Fortress]] island. They appear after defeating [[Kuudra]] once, and allow the player to obtain the [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Kuudra Follower Boots]].
## Locations
### Crimson Isle
The Kuudra Loremaster is located at [[Coordinates|-106.5, 126, -817.5]] in [[Scarleton]] and can be found after defeating [[Kuudra]] at least once.
### Blazing Fortress
#### Setup
To make the Kuudra Loremaster appear on the [[Blazing Fortress]], the player must first say one of the following lines to the Kuudra Loremaster on the Crimson Isle:
- THE BRIDGE IS COLLAPSING, GET TO THE CENTER!
- Those orbs strengthen Kuudra! Shoot them down with your cannons!
- A Dropship! Take it down before it's too late!
If done correctly, Kuudra Loremaster will respond with [NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: Wow! That is a classic. It's great to meet a fellow follower of Kuudra. to verify this part of their quest is complete. The Kuudra Loremaster will now be able to appear on the [[Blazing Fortress]], based on the following method of appearance.
#### Appearance
For the entire 24 hours of every real-life Tuesday, based on the UTC-5 time zone (which matches EST but ignores daylight savings), the Kuudra Loremaster can be found in the [[Blazing Fortress]] at [[Coordinates|-256.5, 146, 120.5]]. This can be reached by entering the [[Deep Caverns]] and teleporting across the void.
## Quest
If the player talks to the Kuudra Loremaster in the [[Blazing Fortress]] with 1,024 (16 stacks) [[Enchanted Netherrack]] and 1,024 [[Enchanted Magma Cream]], as well as over 1,000,000 [[Netherrack]] and [[Magma Cream]] [[Collections|Collection]], the Kuudra Loremaster will give them the [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Kuudra Follower Boots]] and disappear right after that.
## Dialogue
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Saying a Trigger Line|[NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: Wow! That is a classic. It's great to meet a fellow follower of Kuudra. [NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: I wish I could give you something to commemorate meeting you, but I don't have that much anymore. [NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: Maybe if we met in the past I would have something for you.|
|On [[Blazing Fortress]]; After Saying a Trigger Line|[NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: Have I met you before? I am getting some dejavu.|
|On [[Blazing Fortress]]; With 1,024 [[Enchanted Netherrack]] and [[Enchanted Magma Cream]]; Without 1,000,000+ [[Netherrack]] and [[Magma Cream]] Collection|[NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: Wow that's a lot of resources! [NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: Too bad you didn't put in the effort to collect them yourselves.|
|On [[Blazing Fortress]]; With 1,024 [[Enchanted Netherrack]] and [[Enchanted Magma Cream]]; With 1,000,000+ [[Netherrack]] and [[Magma Cream]] Collection|[NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: Wow how did you know I liked these items! [NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: I'm lucky to have met you. [NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: Here, take this to commemorate our meeting!|
|After saying "What just happened!? Is this Kuudra's real lair?" and holding Repulsive Kuudra Teeth Plaque ([[Kuudra Teeth Plaque]])|[NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: I don't know, adventurer, is it his real lair??? [NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: Speaking of layers, I see you have Repulsive Kuudra Teeth Plaque! [NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: You can definitely use that to take some layers off Kuudra's teeth! [NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: There, I'll help you get it broken down!|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None|[NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: I know so many things about Kuudra. [NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: They are quite interesting.|
|None|[NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: I've been studying the ancient texts of Kuudra. [NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: They are quite interesting.|
|None|[NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: When I was much younger, I used to collect so much Magma Cream and Netherrack. [NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: Unfortunately, those days are behind me. [NPC] Kuudra Loremaster: I now spend my days learning about Kuudra.|
## Trivia
- The required collection is added up from all the coop members.
- The Kuudra Loremaster appears for only 24 hours each time when the [[Nether Wart]] patches behind their location or on the [[Crimson Isle]] spawn portal are in the full growth stage. The Nether Warts grow at 5:00 AM UTC every day, and will be reset after fully grown.
## Bugs
- The Kuudra Loremaster will occasionally not respond when one of the trigger lines is said. However, the quest should still work as expected.

# Kysha
Categories: Non-player characters
[Kysha]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: David's Hunting Lessons ([[Quests]])
Description: Teaches the player how to syphon and fuse Attribute Shards ([[Attributes]]).
Location: [[Fusion House]]
Coordinates: -601.5 115 -12
Kysha is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Fusion House]]. They teach the player how to syphon and fuse [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before unlocking [[Heart of the Forest]]|[NPC] Kysha: <player>! Have you talked to Hina at the front of the island to get your hands on a Heart of the Forest?|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Kysha: Hi <player>! [NPC] Kysha: David Hunterborough says you've completed your training. [NPC] Kysha: Now, it's time to teach you the true purpose of hunting. [NPC] Kysha: Every Shard you collect goes into your Hunting Box, located in the Hunting Skill Menu. [NPC] Kysha: There are two things you can do with those Shards. [NPC] Kysha: Are you following so far? Select an option: [Of course!]|
|After selecting [Of course!]|[NPC] Kysha: You can either syphon or fuse them. [NPC] Kysha: Syphoning is key - it lets you permanently gain the attribute of the creature it came from. Select an option: [What are attributes?]|
|After selecting [What are attributes?]|[NPC] Kysha: Attributes are very much like Pet abilities in a way. [NPC] Kysha: But hunting lets you harness those powers independently of the creature. [NPC] Kysha: Here, take this Phanpyre Shard. [NPC] Kysha: Open your Hunting Box, inside the Hunting Skill Menu, and syphon it. Kysha gave you 1x Phanpyre Shard!|
|Before opening the Hunting Box ([[Attributes]])|[NPC] Kysha: Go inside your Hunting Box, inside the Hunting Skill Menu, and Syphon it.|
|After syphoning a Shard ([[Attributes]])|[NPC] Kysha: Congratulations! You permanently unlocked the [Shard Name] attribute! [NPC] Kysha: Every time you syphon more of a Shard, that attribute will grow stronger. [NPC] Kysha: You can view it in your Attribute Menu inside the Hunting Skill Menu. Select an option: [Anything else?]|
|After selecting [Anything else?]|[NPC] Kysha: Yes! [NPC] Kysha: Like I said earlier, you can also fuse Shards. [NPC] Kysha: The Precursors built a machine that merges the genetic essence of two creatures. [NPC] Kysha: We call this process fusion. [NPC] Kysha: To fuse, combine 5 Shards of one creature with 5 Shards of another in the Fusion Machine. [NPC] Kysha: The fusion process can uncover creatures that haven't been discovered yet! [NPC] Kysha: I've given you 5 Phanpyre Shards and 5 Cod Shards to fuse in the Fusion Machine. [NPC] Kysha: Try it out and see what you can create! Kysha gave you 5x Phanpyre Shards and 5x Cod Shards!|
|Before fusing Shards in the Fusion Machine|[NPC] Kysha: Go on, try to fuse Shards together in the fusion machine. [NPC] Kysha: You might need to hunt a few more creatures first!|
|After fusing Shards|[NPC] Kysha: You fused a [Shard Name] shard! [NPC] Kysha: Interesting... [NPC] Kysha: Fusions can have very peculiar results, I'm sure you will spend a lot of time around here. [NPC] Kysha: Anyhow, I don't think I have anything left to teach you. [NPC] Kysha: You might want to talk to David Hunterborough next.|
|Idle|[NPC] Kysha: We are still discovering Shards every year! [NPC] Kysha: Fusions are infinite!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Kysha: ✆ In my spare time I like to go swimming down in the Murkwater Loch. [NPC] Kysha: ✆ There are so many beautiful creatures to see down there. [NPC] Kysha: ✆ Verdants, Azures, Shellwise, Joydives, I've seen them all. [NPC] Kysha: ✆ However, there's one that I've never quite been able to find. [NPC] Kysha: ✆ The folk around here call her Nessie. [NPC] Kysha: ✆ She's so seldom seen that many don't even believe she truly exists. [NPC] Kysha: ✆ But I do. I can sense her down there sometimes, while I'm swimming. [NPC] Kysha: ✆ So, how about it? [NPC] Kysha: ✆ Bring me a Nessie Shard and I'll give you my Abiphone Contact. [GIVE SHARD]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Selecting [GIVE SHARD]; without [[Nessie Shard]]|[NPC] Kysha: ✆ Looks like you don't have the Nessie Shard I asked for. [NPC] Kysha: ✆ I've heard that she's quite a rare catch, so I guess you'll just have to keep trying.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Giving [[Nessie Shard]]|[NPC] Kysha: ✆ Wow! You really found her! What was she like? Select an option: [It was an amazing experience.] [NPC] Kysha: ✆ I bet! [NPC] Kysha: ✆ I can't believe she actually exists... I was beginning to lose hope. [NPC] Kysha: ✆ Looks like I need to get down to the Loch as soon as possible. [NPC] Kysha: ✆ Anyways, as promised, here's my contact. [NPC] Kysha: ✆ Give me a call if you ever need to use the Fusion Machine!|

# Lapis Miner
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Mining, Quests, Quest giver, Deep Caverns
[Lapis Miner]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Helpful Miner ([[Quests]])
Description: Tasks the player with bringing them a [[Lapis Pickaxe]], rewarding a [[Compactor]] if done.
Location: [[Lapis Quarry]]
Coordinates: -10.5 120 35.5
The Lapis Miner is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Lapis Quarry]]. They request the player to bring them a [[Lapis Pickaxe]], which will be consumed upon completing of the [[Quests|Helpful Miner]] quest. The player will receive a [[Compactor]] as a reward.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction|[NPC] Lapis Miner: Hey you! I broke my Lapis Pickaxe - do you think you could bring me another? [NPC] Lapis Miner: First you'll need to reach Lapis Lazuli Collection III, and then you can craft it for me.|
|Before reaching Lapis Lazuli Collection III ([[Lapis Lazuli]])|[NPC] Lapis Miner: Some items in SkyBlock can only be crafted when you reach a certain collection requirement. [NPC] Lapis Miner: To craft me a Lapis Pickaxe, you will need to reach Lapis Lazuli Collection III.|
|After reaching Lapis Lazuli Collection III ([[Lapis Lazuli]]), and before bringing a [[Lapis Pickaxe]]|[NPC] Lapis Miner: Oh! You reached Lapis Lazuli Collection III! [NPC] Lapis Miner: Any chance you can craft me a new Lapis Pickaxe now? [NPC] Lapis Miner: Check the Recipe Book in your SkyBlock Menu to learn how to craft it!|
|Bringing a [[Lapis Pickaxe]]|[NPC] Lapis Miner: Thank you! I appreciate your support in these trying times. [NPC] Lapis Miner: For your trouble, here's a Compactor. Place it in a minion upgrade slot and it will automatically turn items into their block form once it's collected enough of them! [NPC] Lapis Miner: I use it in my Lapis Minion, those things fill terribly fast!|
|If you've brought a [[Lapis Pickaxe]]|[NPC] Lapis Miner: My Lapis Pickaxe might not mine the fastest, but it gives me a lot of experience! [NPC] Lapis Miner: I use that experience to enchant my Lapis Pickaxe with efficiency, which makes it mine faster! [NPC] Lapis Miner: How cool is that?|
## Trivia
- The Lapis Miner and [[Redstone Miner]] are siblings.
- The Lapis Miner used to ask for an iron pickaxe instead of a [[Lapis Pickaxe]].
  - There used to be a bug where when clicked multiple times, the Lapis Miner would consume multiple iron pickaxes from player's inventory.

# Lasea
Categories: Non-player characters
[Lasea]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Barbarian Faction Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) Barbarian Training ([[Quests]])
Description: Tasks the player with obtaining a Skeleton Fish ([[Trophy Fish]]) and Flyfish ([[Trophy Fish]]) during the Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]]) Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]).
Location: [[Dragontail]]
Coordinates: -566 108 -657
Lasea is an [[NPC]] in [[Dragontail]]. During the [[Factions|Barbarian Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]], she tasks the player with obtaining a [[Trophy Fish|Skeleton Fish]] and [[Trophy Fish|Flyfish]].
## Quest
After killing the [[Magma Boss]] for [[Ugo]] as part of the [[Factions|Barbarian Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]], Lasea will ask the player to bring her 1 [[Trophy Fish|Skeleton Fish]] and 1 [[Trophy Fish|Flyfish]]. After they are brought these items, the player will be rewarded with [[Factions|100 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]], and then tasked with speaking to [[Gris]] to continue the quest.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Starting Quest|[NPC] Lasea: Hello! [NPC] Lasea: Have you tried lava fishing before? [NPC] Lasea: Us Piglins believe lava fishes are sacred beasts. [NPC] Lasea: However recently something strange has been happening to the fishes. [NPC] Lasea: I am trying to understand the cause of this turmoil. [NPC] Lasea: My beliefs prevent me from fishing, but I cannot investigate without seeing some specimens myself. [NPC] Lasea: Could you please bring me 1 Flyfish and 1 Skeleton Fish. [NPC] Lasea: I don't know myself how to find those, but there is an expert who lives at the top of the volcano. [NPC] Lasea: Their name is Odger.|
|Before bringing the [[Trophy Fish]] Random|[NPC] Lasea: I don't want to point fingers at anyone, but if anyone is messing with the lava, it's certainly not our faction.|
|Before bringing the [[Trophy Fish]] Random|[NPC] Lasea: Odger is a Goblin. I think he eats the fishes, raw...|
|Bringing the [[Trophy Fish]]|[NPC] Lasea: Thank you very much! [NPC] Lasea: I am glad we can count you in our ranks! [NPC] Lasea: I'll be studying those Fishes now. [NPC] Lasea: See you around.|
|Outside Quest|[NPC] Lasea: We Piglins send our deads in the lava of the Volcano for their final journey. [NPC] Lasea: The Volcano is everything to us, its Sulphur made us who we are, so it's only normal that we give everything back to the Volcano. [NPC] Lasea: A long long time ago, when we cremated the corpse of our great Chief Malak, the Volcano erupted. [NPC] Lasea: It was the largest eruption we have ever seen. As if the Volcano was talking to us. [NPC] Lasea: And from that burst, thousands of lava fishes fell from the sky onto Dragontail. [NPC] Lasea: We believe Malak and our ancestors found a new life inside the creatures of the lava.|

# Lazy Miner
Categories: Non-player characters, Quests, Mining, Quest giver
[Lazy Miner]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Lost and Found ([[Quests]])
Description: Tasks the player with finding their lost [[Iron Pickaxe]].
Location: [[Gold Mine]]
Coordinates: -11.5 78 -337.5
Lazy Miner is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Gold Mine]]. They task the player with finding their lost [[Iron Pickaxe]] as part of the [[Quests|Lost and Found]] quest.
## Quest
Interacting with the Lazy Miner for the first time starts the [[Quests|Lost and Found]] quest. They claim they lost their [[Iron Pickaxe]] in the mines. The pickaxe can be found at [[Coordinates|-18, 24, -305]]. Once the player finds the [[Iron Pickaxe]], they get to keep it.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Lazy Miner: Whoops! I lost my good pickaxe again in the mines. That's why I always come prepared with a backup! [NPC] Lazy Miner: It's probably down in the mine somewhere. Can you go find it?|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None|[NPC] Lazy Miner: Maybe one day I'll go back down there.|
|None|[NPC] Lazy Miner: <Current Month> <Current Day> is my resting day!|
|None|[NPC] Lazy Miner: I'd go mining but I'm too lazy.|
|During Objective: Find the Lazy Miner's pickaxe|[NPC] Lazy Miner: Find my pickaxe in the Gold Mine! I'm not going back down there...|
|During Objective: Collect iron and gold ingots|[NPC] Lazy Miner: Smelting Touch is a really useful enchantment. It automatically smelts ores into ingots!|
|During Objective: Collect iron and gold ingots|[NPC] Lazy Miner: Collect iron and gold ingots with that pickaxe you found! [NPC] Lazy Miner: ...Or do it the long way and smelt the ore yourself.|
|Before reaching Mining V (5) ([[Mining]])|[NPC] Lazy Miner: The Deep Caverns are full of strange creatures and expensive treasures. [NPC] Lazy Miner: Reach Mining Level V to gain access!|
|Before visiting the [[Deep Caverns]]|[NPC] Lazy Miner: Have you spelunked the Deep Caverns? [NPC] Lazy Miner: They are full of bountiful treasures but watch out for those Lapis Zombies!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Lazy Miner: It's <real life day of week>, my resting day! I earned myself a little treat, maybe you could bring me some chocolate?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Lazy Miner: Mmh! This is really good! I'll be sure to come back again when I'm no longer busy!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Lazy Miner: That's really unfortunate, I'll just come back again on my next resting day!|
## Trivia
- Lazy Miner's skin, originally titled "Rogue Dwarf", was created by Jumble on Planet Minecraft.

The Lift Operator is an [[NPC]] located on each level of the [[Deep Caverns]]. They allow the player to teleport between the different areas.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Lift Operator: Hey Feller! [NPC] Lift Operator: I control this lift here behind me. [NPC] Lift Operator: Once you've explored an area I can give you a safe ride back there. [NPC] Lift Operator: Be careful not to fall down the shaft though, it's a long way down! [NPC] Lift Operator: Good luck on your adventures!|
|Before reaching the [[Lapis Quarry]]|[NPC] Lift Operator: Venture down into the Lapis Quarry to unlock my Lift Menu!|
|Accessing [[Dwarven Mines]]; Without [[Heart of the Mountain]]|[NPC] Lift Operator: That is too deep, traveler! [NPC] Lift Operator: For that you require Heart of the Mountain. [NPC] Lift Operator: Talk to Rhys, the Dwarven Emissary.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Lift Operator: Operating that lift all day takes a lot outta ya, you know?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Lift Operator: Cheers, that's the stuff!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Lift Operator: Back to the mines I go...|
## Trivia
- The player must first enter a zone before they can teleport to it. The only exception to this is the [[Dwarven Mines]], which requires the player to have completed [[Rhys]]' quest.

# Loras
Categories: Non-player characters
[Loras]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Talks about the Precursors ([[Precursor (disambiguation)]]).
Location: [[Tangleburg Library]]
Coordinates: -593.5 118 14.5
Loras is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Tangleburg Library]]. They tell the player lore about the [[Precursor (disambiguation)|Precursors]] and [[Starborn (disambiguation)|Starborns]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Loras: Greetings, traveler. [NPC] Loras: I'm Loras, a historian dedicated to uncovering the secrets of the Precursors. [NPC] Loras: Sadly, their history is shrouded in mystery, with scarce records surviving. [NPC] Loras: I've had to piece together their story through fieldwork and ancient relics. [NPC] Loras: My dream? To pen a comprehensive tome about them, but the pieces are still falling into place. [NPC] Loras: Interested in what I've uncovered so far? Select an option: [Yes] [Later, maybe]|
|Selecting [Later, maybe]|[NPC] Loras: Understandable. History has a way of capturing us when we least expect it. [NPC] Loras: Feel free to return if curiosity strikes.|
|Selecting [Yes]|[NPC] Loras: Ah, a fellow history enthusiast! Excellent. [NPC] Loras: Through my research, I've deducted that the Precursors were this world's first inhabitants. [NPC] Loras: Remarkably, they lived under the watch of multiple entities called Starborns! [NPC] Loras: I've identified at least eight Starborns, but I suspect there were more. [NPC] Loras: Each Starborn had a unique domain and wielded immense power. Select an option: [What kind of domain?]|
|Selecting [What kind of domain?]|[NPC] Loras: From the Starborn of the Forests or the Sea, even a Starborn of Life! [NPC] Loras: It's unclear whether the Precursors were creations of these Starborns, but they certainly revered them. [NPC] Loras: My studies led me to distinguish two categories: Old and New Starborns. Select an option: [Not very original.]|
|Selecting [Not very original.]|[NPC] Loras: Ah, but the names reflect a theory. [NPC] Loras: The Old Starborns seemen indifferent to mortal affairs, transcending earthly desires. [NPC] Loras: Conversely, the New Starborns engaged actively with the Precursors, reveling in their advancements and guiding their progress. [NPC] Loras: I believe that immortals who maintain worldly interests and desire could be considered 'young' in a divine sense. Select an option: [It's an interesting theory]|
|Selecting [It's an interesting theory]|[NPC] Loras: Much appreciated. [NPC] Loras: The New Starborns were particularly invested, almost as proud mentors observing their wards' milestones. [NPC] Loras: They even intervened directly at times, teaching essential lessons like substainable living and the imporance of nature's balance. Select an option: [So the Starborns were spectating them]|
|Selecting [So the Starborns were spectating them]|[NPC] Loras: Precisely, but also guiding. [NPC] Loras: For instance, they introduced magic to the Precursors, a decision with unforeseen consequences. [NPC] Loras: The Precursors ended up excelling in both magic and science, eventually blending the two to create wonders. Select an option: [I see where this is going]|
|Selecting [I see where this is going]|[NPC] Loras: Their ambition grew boundless. [NPC] Loras: They sought more: longer life, greater strength, boundless energy. [NPC] Loras: Later generations of Precursors, born into a world of accomplishments, yearned for purpose and legacy. [NPC] Loras: For those young generations of Precursors, it became impossible to define their own purpose in the shadow of monumental achievements. [NPC] Loras: Something had to happen, the wheel of progress had to keep turning. [NPC] Loras: So one day, a bold claim arose: the Precursors had surpassed the Starborns. [NPC] Loras: They saw the Starborns' teachings as shackles, hindering their true potential. Select an option: [Those Precursors are very human after all]|
|Selecting [Those Precursors are very human after all]|[NPC] Loras: Indeed... But this hubris marked the beginning of the end. [NPC] Loras: The Precursors harnessed their tools and magic to overpower the Starborns. [NPC] Loras: Adding to this, the Starborns' power was intrinsically tied to the belief of their followers. [NPC] Loras: As the Precursors' faith diminished, so too did the might of the Starborns, weakening them in this crucial confrontation. Select an option: [Can Starborns even die?]|
|Selecting [Can Starborns even die?]|[NPC] Loras: That remains a mystery. But it's clear that their influence faded. [NPC] Loras: As for the Precursors, they vanished without a trace, a puzzling paradox. Select an option: [So how does the story end?]|
|Selecting [So how does the story end?]|[NPC] Loras: Here's my theory: aware of their impending doom, the Starborn of Time, perhaps the most powerful due to universal belief, made a desperate move. [NPC] Loras: He propelled the Precursors far into the future, hoping that by then, others could stand against them. Select an option: [The future? When exactly?]|
|Selecting [The future? When exactly?]|[NPC] Loras: That's the unknown. [NPC] Loras: But it suggests that the Precursors might reemerge one day, a thought both fascinating and daunting. Select an option: [So the Precursors will just appear out of nowhere one day?]|
|Selecting [So the Precursors will just appear out of nowhere one day?]|[NPC] Loras: It's a possibility we cannot ignore. [NPC] Loras: We must be prepared for whatever comes. [NPC] Loras: Thank you for lending an ear to my life's work. [NPC] Loras: Now, I must return to my studies.|
## History

|Jun 5, 2025|Added Loras.|
|Mar 23, 2026|Changed Loras' skin.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added Loras.|
|Mar 31, 2026|0.24.3|Changed Loras' skin.|

# Lumber Jack
Aliases: Lumberjack
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Foraging, Quest giver
[Lumber Jack]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Foraging Tutorial ([[Quests]])
Description: Rewards the player with a [[Promising Axe]] after collecting 20 Oak Logs ([[Oak Log]]).
Location: [[Foraging Camp]]
Coordinates: -123.5 73 -29.5
The Lumber Jack is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Foraging Camp]] in the [[Forest]]. They ask the player to collect 20 [[Oak Log|Oak Logs]]. In return for the logs, they give the player a [[Promising Axe]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before starting the quest|[NPC] Lumber Jack: Timber! [NPC] Lumber Jack: My woodcutting assistant has fallen quite ill! Do you think you could take over for him? Select an option: [Sure] [Nah, I'm good]|
|Selecting [Nah, I'm good]|[NPC] Lumber Jack: Oh okay... maybe come back later!|
|Selecting [Sure]|[NPC] Lumber Jack: Cheers! Let me tell ya a little about what we're going to be doing. [NPC] Lumber Jack: We will be Foraging! As one of SkyBlock's core skills, Foraging involves cutting down lots of different types of trees! [NPC] Lumber Jack: As you do this, you'll unlock various crafts and useful stat boosts that will help in other areas of your adventure. [NPC] Lumber Jack: Here, let me unlock the Foraging skill for you, and then you can get started! [NPC] Lumber Jack: To get you started, please bring me 20 Oak Logs! You can break the trees just off the trail to get 'em. [NPC] Lumber Jack: Use your fists for now - I'll give you my previous assistant's axe if you can prove your worth!|
|Before giving 20 Oak Logs ([[Oak Log]])|[NPC] Lumber Jack: Please collect and then bring to me 20 Oak Logs! You can get 'em from the Forest just off the trail.|
|Giving 20 Oak Logs ([[Oak Log]])|[NPC] Lumber Jack: Nice job! In return for those logs, I'll give ya this Promising Axe. [NPC] Lumber Jack: It's far less painful than using your fists to punch trees, I'll tell ya that much! [NPC] Lumber Jack: If you feel like Foraging is the thing for you, continue down the path and head towards The Park. [NPC] Lumber Jack: My friend Charlie can be found there. I'm sure he'll have some work for you!|
|After completing the quest|[NPC] Lumber Jack: My mate Charlie has a Treecapitator! With it, he can break down entire trees in a single swipe!|
|After completing the quest|[NPC] Lumber Jack: Have you spoken to my mate Charlie over in The Park yet? He could probably use your help.|
|After completing the quest|[NPC] Lumber Jack: Have you enchanted your axe with Efficiency V? It'll help ya break stuff a lot faster.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Lumber Jack: My assistant is feeling off, and I think we could do with a hearty meal. Do you think you can help us out?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Lumber Jack: Thank you! I'm sure this will make him feel better.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Lumber Jack: I thought you'd be more thankful for the sweet axe my assistant gave you.|

# Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Gives the player a [[Mining]]-related quest outside the Rift in exchange for Eleanor's Slippers ([[Eleanor's Armor]]).
Location: [[The Mountaintop]]
Coordinates: -0.5 114 35.5
Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I is an [[NPC]] on [[The Mountaintop]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. They give the player a [[Mining]]-related quest outside the Rift in exchange for [[Eleanor's Armor|Eleanor's Slippers]].
## Purpose
Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I gives the player a [[Mining]] quest that they must complete outside the Rift.
Behind the nearby mirror, a replica of the player is equipped with [[Heat Armor]] and a [Lv1] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Silverfish Pet]] while mining [[Cobblestone]] with a [[Drills|Drill]] in the [[Slimehill]].
To complete the quest, all of the following conditions must be met at the same time:
- Wear a full set of [[Heat Armor]], [[Flamebreaker Armor]], [[Sorrow Armor]], or [[Armor of Divan]].
- Equip an [[Armadillo Pet]], [[Mithril Golem Pet]], [[Scatha Pet]], [[Silverfish Pet]], or [[Snail Pet]] of [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarity or higher.
- Hold a [[Drills|Drill]] of any type.
- Be between [[Coordinates|32, 64, 27]] and [[Coordinates|52, 75, 3]] in the [[Slimehill]].
- Sneak twice within one second.
Once completed, the message "You sense a watchful presence..." will appear in chat. The player can then return to Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I to claim [[Eleanor's Armor|Eleanor's Slippers]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I: I seek a particular miner. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I: Reflected in this mirror... could that be you? Select an option: [They definitely look like me.]|
|Selecting [They definitely look like me.] or before completing the quest|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I: Confirm your identity for me. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I: From beyond the mirror, wear a similar set of mining gear, or stronger. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I: Then, crouch twice.|
|Completed quest|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I: Very well. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I: You've proven your identity. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I: Here, accept this offering.|
|After completing the quest without claiming the reward|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I: Here, accept this offering. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I: Perhaps you are the one my master seeks.|
|After quest completion|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I: Perhaps you are the one my master seeks.|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Added Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I.|

# Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth II

Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth II is an [[NPC]] in the [[Walk of Fame]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. They allow the player to enter the [[Wizardman Bureau]] after obtaining the [[Celestial Timecharm]].
## Purpose
Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth II prevents the player from entering the [[Wizardman Bureau]] until they have obtained the [[Celestial Timecharm]].

# Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III
Aliases: Eleanor Goldsworth, Eleanor Q. Goldsworth, Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III, Madame Eleanor Goldsworth, Madame Goldsworth
Categories: Non-player characters
[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Introduces the player to the [[Museum]], a place where players can store items in exchange for SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]).
Location: [[Museum]]
Coordinates: 33.5 73 11.5
Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III is an [[NPC]] that is found in the [[Museum]]. She can be clicked to open the Museum menu. Once the player has reached Museum Milestone 3, she will also appraise their museum for a one-time fee of 10,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Dialogue
- Grammar and/or punctuation mistakes are intentional, as they appear so in the dialogue in-game.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III: Welcome, [player]! [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III: This world has an incredible amount of interesting gear and rare items, which I would like to showcase. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III: Would you like to help me fill the Museum? I can even reward you for your contributions! [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III: I'll show you the list of items that I'm currently looking for. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III: You can donate any item from the list and you'll still be able to use those items whenever you want! [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III: However, once you donate an item, it will be come Co-op Soulbound to you, meaning you won't be able to trade off your profile, auction, or sell the item anymore. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III: Choose carefully!|
|Museum Disabled|This Museum is temporarily disabled. Check back again later!|
|[[The Garden]] First Interaction|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III: I'm here to put a value on your farm. Bring me your fanciest crop.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III: Truly Magnificent! I value your farm at a minimum of [value of farm] coins!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III: I value your farm at 0 coins. Horrible service.|
|[[The Garden]] Speaking as a Guest|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III: Hmm... I haven't seen you around here before. Do you work here?|
## Appraisal
Once the player has reached Museum Milestone 3 and paid Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III 10M [[Coins]], she will display the total value of the player's museum.
## Trivia
- Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III calculates the value of the player's [[The Garden|Garden]] with the following equation: Value=GardenLevel×10,000,000+100.
- Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III used to have an [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact, unlocked from [[Museum|Museum Milestones]]. However, this feature was swiftly and silently removed.
## Gallery
- Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III as seen in the Museum.

# Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX
Categories: Non-player characters, Confirmations needed, Pages with UIs
[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Gives the player a [[Fishing]]-related quest outside the Rift in exchange for Eleanor's Tunic ([[Eleanor's Armor]]).
Location: [[The Mountaintop]]
Coordinates: 37.5 129 41.5
Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX is an [[NPC]] on [[The Mountaintop]] in the [[Rift Dimension]] who gives the player a [[Fishing]]-related quest outside the Rift in exchange for [[Eleanor's Armor|Eleanor's Tunic]].
## Purpose
Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX gives the player a [[Fishing]] quest that must be completed outside the Rift.
Behind the nearby mirror, a replica of the player is shown fishing in the [[Dwarven Mines]] while equipped with [[Sponge Armor]] and a [Lv1] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Flying Fish Pet]].
To complete the quest, all of the following conditions must be met at the same time:
- Wearing one of the following full Armor Sets:
  - [[Sponge Armor]], [[Shark Scale Armor]], [[Nutcracker Armor]]
  - [[Gold Hunter Armor]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], [[Diamond Hunter Armor]][Confirm],
  - [[Diver's Armor]], [[Abyssal Armor]]
  - [[Thunder Armor|Thunder Armorwith]] [[Thunderbolt Necklace]]
  - [[Magma Lord Armor]][Confirm], with [[Magma Lord Necklace]][Confirm].
- Equipping a [[Flying Fish Pet]] or [[Ammonite Pet]] of [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarity or higher.
- Holding any [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]].
- Being inside the [[Dwarven Mines]] while within the coordinates [[Coordinates|-130, 195, -94]] and [[Coordinates|-172, 214, -49]].
  - The location is in a cave that can be accessed at [[Coordinates|-121, 205, -60]].
- Splashing themselves with any potion.
Once completed, the message "You sense a watchful presence..." will appear in chat. The player can then return to Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX to claim [[Eleanor's Armor|Eleanor's Tunic]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX: I seek a particular fisherman. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX: Reflected in this mirror... could that be you? Select an option: [They definitely look like me.]|
|Selecting [They definitely look like me.] or before completing the quest|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX: Confirm your identity for me. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX: From beyond the mirror, wear a similar set of fishing gear, or stronger. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX: Then, splash yourself with a potion.|
|Completed quest|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX: Very well. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX: You've proven your identity. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX: Here, accept this offering.|
|After completing the quest without claiming the reward|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX: Here, accept this offering. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX: Perhaps you are the one my master seeks.|
|After quest completion|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX: Perhaps you are the one my master seeks.|
## Tips
- Players should quickly swap back to holding their [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] after throwing the Splash [[Potions|Potion]].

|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Added Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX.|

# Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth V
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth V]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Gives the player a [[Farming]]-related quest outside the Rift in exchange for Eleanor's Cap ([[Eleanor's Armor]]).
Location: [[The Mountaintop]]
Coordinates: 5.5 150 22.5
Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth V is an [[NPC]] on [[The Mountaintop]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. They give the player a [[Farming]]-related quest outside the Rift in exchange for [[Eleanor's Armor|Eleanor's Cap]].
## Purpose
Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth V gives the player a [[Farming]] quest that they must complete outside the Rift.
Behind the nearby mirror, a replica of the player is shown farming [[Carrot|Carrots]] with a [[Gauss Carrot Shovel|Gauss Carrot Hoe]] on [[The Barn]] while equipped with [[Cropie Armor]] and a [Lv1] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Elephant Pet]].
To complete the quest, all of the following conditions must be met at the same time:
- Wearing a full Armor set of [[Cropie Armor]], [[Squash Armor]], [[Fermento Armor]] or [[Helianthus Armor]]
  - Though these armor sets are listed as requiring certain [[Farming]] skill levels to use (ex. Cropie Armor requires [[Farming|Farming XXX (30)]]), they can still be worn and used for this quest even if the player's Farming level is below the threshold.
- Equipping an [[Elephant Pet]] or [[Mooshroom Cow Pet]] of [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarity or higher
- Holding one of the following items:
  - [[Euclid's Wheat Sickle]], [[Gauss Carrot Shovel]], [[Turing Sugar Cane Cutter]], [[Newton Nether Wart Cutter|Newton Nether Warts Hoe]], [[Pythagorean Potato Shovel]], or one of their upgrades.
- Being on [[The Barn]] in the carrot field
  - Within the coordinates [[Coordinates|134, 81, -282]] and [[Coordinates|121, 88, -291]]
- Say Eleanor is the best!
Once completed, the message "You sense a watchful presence..." will appear in chat. The player can then return to Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth V to claim [[Eleanor's Armor|Eleanor's Cap]].

Goldsworth V: From beyond the mirror, wear a similar set of farming gear, or stronger. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth V: Then say "Eleanor is the best!"|
|Completed quest|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth V: Very well. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth V: You've proven your identity. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth V: Here, accept this offering.|
|After completing the quest without claiming the reward|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth V: Here, accept this offering. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth V: Perhaps you are the one my master seeks.|
|After quest completion|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth V: Perhaps you are the one my master seeks.|

# Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth VII
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth VII]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Gives the player a [[Foraging]]-related quest outside the Rift in exchange for Eleanor's Trousers ([[Eleanor's Armor]]).
Location: [[The Mountaintop]]

Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth VII is an [[NPC]] on [[The Mountaintop]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. They give the player a [[Foraging]]-related quest outside the Rift in exchange for [[Eleanor's Armor|Eleanor's Trousers]].
## Purpose
Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth VII gives the player a [[Foraging]] quest that they must complete outside the Rift.
Behind the nearby mirror, a replica of the player is equipped with no armor and a [Lv1] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Ocelot Pet]] while mining [[Jungle Log|Jungle Wood]] with a [[Treecapitator]] on the [[Jungle Island]].
To complete the quest, all of the following conditions must be met at the same time:
- Wearing a full Armor set of [[Golden Armor]], [[Gold Hunter Armor]], [[Armor of Divan]] or [[Diver's Armor]]
- Equipping a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Ocelot Pet]]
- Mining the logs within the coordinates [[Coordinates|-463, 127, -83]] and [[Coordinates|-472, 127, -83]] inside the [[Jungle Island]]
Once completed, the message "You sense a watchful presence..." will appear in chat. The player can then return to Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth VII to claim [[Eleanor's Armor|Eleanor's Trousers]].

# Maeve
Categories: Non-player characters, Article stubs
[Maeve]
Type: [[NPC]]
Location: [[The Garden]]
Maeve is an [[NPC]] that only appears in [[The Garden]]. They can appear after the player has reached [[The Garden|Garden XII (12)]]. Completing their offer will reward the player with a [[Harvest Harbinger Potion|Harvest Harbinger V Potion]].
## Dialogue
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None Prelude|[NPC] Maeve: Ah, so the Garden isn't just full of weeds, huh? [NPC] Maeve: Finally, someone worth talking to. You look like you know the difference between a Cropie and a lump of dirt. [NPC] Maeve: I'm in the market for some rare materials. Maybe you're familiar? [NPC] Maeve: In exchange, I might have something 'special' for you.|
|None Prelude|[NPC] Maeve: I must say, this Garden is starting to grow on me. Figuratively, of course.|
|None Prelude|[NPC] Maeve: Miss me? Don't answer that, I know you did.|
|None Prelude|[NPC] Maeve: Ah, The Garden. Where dreams wither and weeds thrive. What do you have for me today?|
|None Request|[NPC] Maeve: Ah, Cropie! Nature's little mystery. Got any?|
|None Request|[NPC] Maeve: Squash? Not the sport, darling. The vegetable. I'll make it worth your while.|
|None Request|[NPC] Maeve: Got any Fermento? Trust me, it's not for a fruit salad.|
|Completing|[NPC] Maeve: Ah, a trade well made. A potion for you, brewed by yours truly.|
|Completing|[NPC] Maeve: Good deal. Here, this potion isn't found on any old store shelf. Don't waste it.|
|Completing|[NPC] Maeve: You're not here on Seth's behalf, are you? I mean, he's cute but he could never afford me.|
|Completing|[NPC] Maeve: Ah, you think this potion is strong? You have no idea what power really tastes like.|
|Completing|[NPC] Maeve: Rare materials are just the tip of the iceberg. But then, you're not ready for what's beneath.|
|Completing|[NPC] Maeve: I've seen things that would make your health bar drop to zero. But don't worry, your little Cropies are safe with me.|
|Completing|[NPC] Maeve: Who needs friends when you have powerful enemies? Oh wait, I have both!|
|Completing|[NPC] Maeve: What's my secret to eternal youth? That's for me to know and for you to find out...maybe.|
|Completing|[NPC] Maeve: These potions are child's play. The real stuff? Well, that's not on the menu for rookies.|
|Completing|[NPC] Maeve: Ah, you want to know my secrets? Even if I told you, you wouldn't believe me.|
|Completing|[NPC] Maeve: Don't bother asking why I need these materials. Let's just say it's...above your clearance level.|
|Completing|[NPC] Maeve: Seth understands the value of my potions. Do you?|
|Completing|[NPC] Maeve: Let's just say these materials aren't the only rare commodities I'm interested in.|
|Denying|[NPC] Maeve: No materials? Huh, I guess your adventures are as empty as your inventory.|
## Trivia
- Maeve was the first [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] visitor added.
- Maeve is mentioned in [[Shen Magnum Opus|Shen Magnum Opus']] dialogue, along with [[Julian]] and [[Mentioned Entities|Seth]].

Marco is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Flower House]]. He asks for the player to pick 5 [[Flowers|Poppy]] and craft them into Rose Red Dye. In exchange, he gives the player a [[Spray Can]] and access to the portal to the [[Canvas Room]].

Step into the portal to the left over there!|

[NPC] Marco: Let me know when you're done with your painting!|

|[[The Garden]] Accepting|[NPC] Marco: This'll do nicely! Ciao!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Marco: If you ever change your mind, you know where to find me!|
