# Marina
Aliases: Candidate Marina, Maria, Marina the Fisherwoman, Mayor Marina, Minister Marina
Categories: Non-player characters, Events, Mayor Election, Mayor, Fishing Festival
[Marina Marina]
Also known as: Mayor Marina
Type: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])
[Locations]
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]), or nearby when elected as Minister ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Community Center]]
Coordinates: 6.5 79 19.5
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]). Also appears on an outer stand when running to become Mayor or Minister ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Election Room]]
Coordinates: -3.5 49 34.5
Tooltip: Year # Candidate; Votes: # (#%); Last elected: #y ago; __________________________ Fishing XP Buff Gain +50☯ Fishing Wisdom on public islands.; Luck of the Sea 2.0; Gain 15α Sea Creature Chance.; Fishing Festival Start a special fishing event the first 3 days of each month!; Fish and fight dangerous sharks and earn unique Shark loot.; Double Trouble For every 1 α Sea Creature Chance, gain +0.1 ⚓ Double Hook Chance.; __________________________; ✯ Minister Perks are also granted if this mayor wins second place!; Click to vote for Marina!
Marina the Fisherwoman is a mayoral candidate that can be elected during the [[Mayor Election]] event. Her perks focus around [[Fishing]].
## Mayoral Perks
Candidate Marina may run for election with up to 4 possible perks.
  [[Marina]] Marina the Fisherwoman
1. Fishing XP Buff: Gain 50 [[Fishing Wisdom]] on public islands.
2. Luck of the Sea 2.0: Gain 15 [[Sea Creature Chance]].
3. [[Fishing Festival]]: Start a special fishing event during the first 3 days of each month! Fish and fight dangerous [[Sharks (disambiguation)|Sharks]] and earn unique Shark loot.
4. Double Trouble: For every 1 [[Sea Creature Chance]], gain +0.1 [[Double Hook Chance]].
### Fishing Festivals Schedules
Regular [[Fishing Festival|Fishing Festivals]] Schedules:
|In-Game Date|Countdown|
|-|-|
|1st of Early Summer|: Countdown:|
|1st of Summer|: Countdown:|
|1st of Late Summer|: Countdown:|
|1st of Early Autumn|: Countdown:|
|1st of Autumn|: Countdown:|
|1st of Late Autumn|: Countdown:|
|1st of Early Winter|: Countdown:|
|1st of Winter|: Countdown:|
|1st of Late Winter|: Countdown:|
|1st of Early Spring|: Countdown:|
|1st of Spring|: Countdown:|
|1st of Late Spring|: Countdown:|
## Dialogue
- The color of the NPC's name changes depending on which color they are in the current [[Mayor Election]]. Yellow has been used as a default color.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Marina: I love diving and fishing. The world is much simpler when you live on the beach. [NPC] Marina: If you don't like the sea, don't vote for me.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Mayor Marina: Caught a big fish today, you got anything to pair it with?|
|[[The Garden]] Accepting|[NPC] Mayor Marina: Perfect, just what I needed!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Mayor Marina: No worries, the sea provides.|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Marina: Be water my friend.|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Marina: I come from an atoll, the sea goes very deep there.|
## Trivia
- Like many of the other Candidates, Marina's name is also of Latin origin. Marina's name can be translated to "Of the Sea", which is fitting considering her perks all relate to fishing and that players can obtain the [[Rod of the Sea]] through her event.
- Her second perk, Luck Of The Sea 2.0, may possibly be a different version of the enchantment of the same name, though this is currently unknown.
- Her left boot is designed after the top half of a [[Pufferfish]].
- Marina and [[Diana]] are the only mayors to give straight stats.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:

# Martin
Categories: Non-player characters
[Martin]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Talks about [[Fishing]] in the [[Tomb Floodway]].
Location: [[Tomb Floodway]]
Coordinates: -579.5 93 31.5
Martin is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Tomb Floodway]].
## Purpose
Martin talks about how [[Fishing]] in the [[Tomb Floodway]] allows the player to avoid catching [[Sea Creatures]]. He also says that rare materials for top-quality [[Fishing Nets]] can be caught there, referring to [[Flexbone|Flexbones]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Interaction|[NPC] Martin: Isn't this place fascinating? [NPC] Martin: Absolutely no sea creatures here, can you believe it? [NPC] Martin: Still, you can catch some rare materials to craft top-quality fishing nets.|
## Trivia
- Martin can be seen [[Fishing]] in the water below him.

|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added Martin.|

# Master Tao
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Crimson Isle
[Master Tao]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Allows the player to start [[Dojo]] challenges.
Location: [[Dojo]]
Coordinates: -234 108 -602
Master Tao is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Dojo]], on the [[Crimson Isle]]. He offers challenges for players, which reward the player points. After a certain challenge is completed, the player will be rewarded the number of points they earned during the challenge.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Master Tao: Ah, who are you? [NPC] Master Tao: Hmmm well, are you powerful? I enjoy watching entities display their power... or die in a pit of fire trying! [NPC] Master Tao: Talk to me again, and I'll give you some challenges to entertain me... I mean, prove yourself. [NPC] Master Tao: In fact, here's a little gift to encourage you to give it a try.|
|After Completing a Challenge F Rank|[NPC] Master Tao: You were not good enough. Come back when you've trained more.|
|After Completing a Challenge D Rank|[NPC] Master Tao: Hardly skilled, but I'll let it pass. Come talk to me for your next challenge, and do better in the future.|
|After Completing a Challenge C Rank|[NPC] Master Tao: Rather average, I'd say. Speak to me again to try a new challenge, or improve on this one!|
|After Completing a Challenge B Rank|[NPC] Master Tao: That was pretty good! Please come by again for a new challenge.|
|After Completing a Challenge A Rank|[NPC] Master Tao: Wow, that was really impressive. Come talk to me to view your next challenge.|
|After Completing a Challenge S Rank|[NPC] Master Tao: That was a remarkable performance! I look forward to giving you your next challenge.|
|Teleporting to Arena|[NPC] Master Tao: Ahhh, here we go! Let's get you into the Arena.|
|Waiting for a Challenge to Start|[NPC] Master Tao: Have patience, I'm getting you in right now!|
|Attempting a Disabled Challenge|[NPC] Master Tao: It is not safe to do that challenge right now. Come back later!|
|With Items|[NPC] Master Tao: I only test people who use their bare skills. No extra help allowed! Come back to me once you've stored your items away.|
|With Only [[Equipment]]|[NPC] Master Tao: Your inventory includes your equipment too! Make sure that is cleared!|
|Whilst Mounted on a Pet ([[Pets]])|[NPC] Master Tao: No pets allowed in the dojo! Get off of your pet.|
|Sent by [[Ugo]] First Interaction|[NPC] Master Tao: Ugo is that you? [NPC] Master Tao: You missed yesterday's lesson. [NPC] Master Tao: And the one before that. [NPC] Master Tao: So today you'll have to do three lessons. [NPC] Master Tao: The first one is a Test of Force. [NPC] Master Tao: Go in the Arena.|
|Sent by [[Ugo]] Completing the Test of Force|[NPC] Master Tao: Humph. This will do for now. [NPC] Master Tao: Onto the next Test, Stamina this time.|
|Sent by [[Ugo]] Completing the Test of Stamina|[NPC] Master Tao: You are not performing that well today, but I'll accept that score for today. [NPC] Master Tao: One more test before I let you go. The Test of Mastery.|
|Sent by [[Ugo]] Completing the Test of Mastery|[NPC] Master Tao: Well done Ugo, you finished your training for today. [NPC] Master Tao: Remember Ugo, I can show you the path but I can not walk it for you. [NPC] Master Tao: If you want to make your father proud you will have to train more than it is expected of you. [NPC] Master Tao: Respect your training and you will respect yourself. [NPC] Master Tao: Go now.|
|Sent by [[Ugo]] None|[NPC] Master Tao: Make sure you empty your inventory entirely before you enter.|
|Sent by [[Ugo]] Attempting to Enter Arena With Items|[NPC] Master Tao: Ugo! You know very well you need to store all your items away before you enter the Arena.|
|Sent by [[Ugo]] Failing a Test|[NPC] Master Tao: This isn't good enough Ugo! Your father would be very annoyed if I told him the score you got! [NPC] Master Tao: Go back inside and do better!|
## Trivia
- The Dojo and Master Tao is likely a reference to real-life Dojo arenas.
- The different coloured [[Belts]] are likely a reference to the different tiers of belts in various martial arts.
- Master Tao's name could be based on Tao, romanization of the Chinese 道, meaning "Way." Tao is an important concept in Chinese Philosophy as well as some of its religions, such as Taoism.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 8, 2022|Added Master Tao.|
|Mar 8, 2022|Disabled Test of control.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Master Tao.|
|Sep 8, 2022|Enabled Test of control.|

# Maths Enjoyer
Aliases: Math Enjoyer, Mathematician
Categories: Non-player characters
[Maths Enjoyer]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Explains part of the formula for [[Accessory Power]].
Location: [[Thaumaturgist]]
Coordinates: -72.5 70 -62.5
The Maths Enjoyer is an [[NPC]] located inside [[Thaumaturgist]]. They tell players the formula for [[Accessory Power]].
## Formula
The formula that they give is Stats Multiplier=29.97×(ln⁡(0.0019×(Accessory Power)+1))1.2.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Maths Enjoyer: Hey if you really want to know… [NPC] Maths Enjoyer: The formula to Accessory Power is… [NPC] Maths Enjoyer: Stats Mult. = 29.97(ln(0.0019AP+1))^1.2 [NPC] Maths Enjoyer: Have fun with that!|
## Trivia
- This NPC is the first Enjoyer NPC which explains a specific mechanic in detail, the second of them is [[Cold Enjoyer]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Apr 15, 2022|Added Mathematician.|
|Jan 30, 2026|Moved Maths Enjoyer from 56.5, 69, -39.5 ([[Coordinates]]) to -72.5, 70, -62.5 ([[Coordinates]]), due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Maths Enjoyer, with some changes.|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Moved Maths Enjoyer from 56.5, 69, -39.5 ([[Coordinates]]) to -72.5, 70, -62.5 ([[Coordinates]]), due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|

Mazakala is an [[NPC]] located in [[Scarleton]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Mazakala: Nonsense... [NPC] Mazakala: The appearance of that strange disturbance on the nearby island might have nothing to do with time travel. [NPC] Mazakala: That doesn't mean we should ignore it, someone needs to... [NPC] Mazakala: Can't you see we are busy?|
|None; (Random)|[NPC] Mazakala: Don't disrespect your elders.|
|None; (Random)|[NPC] Mazakala: Stop eavesdropping!!!|
|None; (Random)|[NPC] Mazakala: Please leave us alone.|
|None; (Random)|[NPC] Mazakala: You are being very rude.|
|During the Talk to 4 Villagers Quest ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) First Interaction (If they're Mage 1/4)|[NPC] Mazakala: Oh, a new face! Welcome to the City of Magic! [NPC] Mazakala: We used to be Pig Farmers centuries ago, but thanks to Sulphur we are now superior beings capable of mastering the magical elements! [NPC] Mazakala: If you are interested in tourist attractions I suggest you visit our Cathedral, which is also our Academy now.|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 2/4); Less than 1,000 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Mazakala: [amount] Intelligence! That's...ok I guess!|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 2/4); 1,000-2,000 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Mazakala: [amount] Intelligence! Wow that's not bad at all!|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 2/4); More than 2,000 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Mazakala: [amount] Intelligence! What, how? Who are you even?|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 3/4)|[NPC] Mazakala: Have you tried the new ability phone? [NPC] Mazakala: The new one is barely better than the one from last year, people still buy them like sheep... [NPC] Mazakala: Wait, you don't even have an Abiphone? [NPC] Mazakala: Yikes...|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 4/4)|[NPC] Mazakala: Ah, the Piglins yes, quite troublesome they are. [NPC] Mazakala: I wouldn't go there if I were you.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Mages are spoken to|[NPC] Mazakala: You seem suspicious.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Mages are spoken to|[NPC] Mazakala: Sorry, I can't talk to you right now.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Mages are spoken to|[NPC] Mazakala: Please stop bothering me.|

# Molbert
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Quests, Quest giver, Park
[Molbert]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Mole Problems ([[Quests]])
[Locations]
Description: Tasks the player with bringing 512 (8 stacks) Jungle Logs ([[Jungle Log]]).
Location: [[Jungle Island]]
Coordinates: -465.5 120 -42.5
Description: Appears here after getting trapped.
Location: [[Jungle Island]]
Coordinates: -448.5 119.24 -64.25
Description: Appears here after being freed from the trap, rewarding the player with the [[Mole Hat]].
Location: [[Jungle Island]]
Coordinates: -447.5 120 -63.5
Molbert is an [[NPC]] located on the [[Jungle Island]]. They require the player to chop down 512 [[Jungle Log]] in exchange for a [[Mole Hat]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Molbert: I watched the comings and goings in this park and it occurred to me that a very common problem plagues these areas. [NPC] Molbert: Moles, you see. They have been burrowing everywhere, their tunnels are disrupting the landscape, they ruined the beauty of this place. It's gotten hard to ignore. [NPC] Molbert: You need an experienced person to handle the situation. [NPC] Molbert: Those troublesome creatures are ruining everything and must be dealt with. Do you understand? [NPC] Molbert: This park deserves better and I want to see it flourish like it used to be. [NPC] Molbert: Let us keep this between us, and I will generously reward you for your efforts. [NPC] Molbert: The moles must be dealt with, and I am sure you will do so quickly and quietly. Select an option: [Sure...] [You look like a mole yourself]|
|After selecting [You look like a mole yourself]|[NPC] Molbert: Ridiculous! How could I be a mole if I am wearing human clothes, huh? [NPC] Molbert: Have you seen moles wearing human clothes before? [NPC] Molbert: I don't think so. Now get back to the issue!|
|Talking after selecting [You look like a mole yourself]|[NPC] Molbert: The moles must be dealt with, and I am sure you will do so quickly and quietly. Select an option: [Sure...] [You look like a mole yourself]|
|After selecting [Sure...]|[NPC] Molbert: Great. First thing we need is to build some traps. [NPC] Molbert: For that however, I need 512 Jungle Logs, should be enough to get things going. [NPC] Molbert: Moles loooove carrots, they are just too good to resist. [NPC] Molbert: Don't worry, I've got a stash ready. [NPC] Molbert: Just thinking about them makes me... Uhm, I mean makes them come out of their hiding space.|
|Not enough resources|[NPC] Molbert: If you can bring me 512 Jungle Logs, we can make some traps!|
|After giving resources|[NPC] Molbert: Fantastic, that's all I needed to build the traps. [NPC] Molbert: It will take me some time to assemble them, so you should come back later.|
|After coming back|[NPC] Molbert: The traps are ready for use; All that remains is to set them up in the right place. Once you find the ideal spots, go ahead and deploy them.|
|After placing traps|[NPC] Molbert: Good job, partner. Now we only need to wait for the right moment for these pests to show up. [NPC] Molbert: This might take some time, so you should come back later.|
|After coming back again|[NPC] Molbert: Hey.. uhm partner! I swear it's not what you think! [NPC] Molbert: Could you lend me a hand here, I can explain everything! [HELP MOLBERT]|
|After selecting [HELP MOLBERT]|[NPC] Molbert: Thank you so much, I was stuck there for at least 3 minutes, I was almost gone for good. [NPC] Molbert: I.. I must have slipped by accident and then fell right into the trap! ... Yes, that's what happened! [NPC] Molbert: ...and then I ate the carrot because I was almost starving in there! Select an option: [I know that you are a mole]|
|After selecting [I know that you are a mole]|[NPC] Molbert: Ok, fine you caught me. But please don't tell the others, they wouldn't want to be my friends anymore if they knew the truth. [NPC] Molbert: Here, take this as compensation for all the trouble I made you go through. [NPC] Molbert: I hope you forgive me after this and we can still be friends.|
|After completing the quest|[NPC] Molbert: I wish I could be in love just like Romero & Juliette.|
|After completing the quest|[NPC] Molbert: I hope you forgive me after this and we can still be friends.|
## Trivia
- Molbert is a reference to Mr. Resetti from the Animal Crossing games.
- The three traps Molbert requires to be placed down are located at [[Coordinates|-465.5, 119, -53.5]], [[Coordinates|-448.5, 120, 64.5]], [[Coordinates|-439.5, 122, -91.5]].

# Mollim
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Crimson Isle, Quests
[Mollim]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Gives the player a Rainbow II ([[Rainbow]]) [[Enchanted Book]] after they splash a [[Titanic Experience Bottle]] on them.
Location: [[Scarleton]]
Coordinates: -132.5 100 -789.5
  Not to be confused with [[Rollim]], an NPC that the player can train with if they're in the Mage [[Factions|Faction]].
Mollim is an [[NPC]] in [[Scarleton]] that can be found next to [[Ulyn]]'s shop.
## Quest
The player can throw a [[Titanic Experience Bottle]] on Mollim, which will allow them to create the [[Rainbow|Rainbow II]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]]. They will then give the player a [[Rainbow|Rainbow II]] [[Enchanted Book]], and the player will gain 20 Mage Reputation.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Splashing a Grand ([[Grand Experience Bottle]]), Titanic ([[Titanic Experience Bottle]]), or [[Colossal Experience Bottle]] before speaking to them|[NPC] Mollim: Ahh, I see you've been reading the wiki! [NPC] Mollim: I read it too from time to time. It gives me valuable insights into my experiments!|
|Not in the Mage Faction ([[Factions]])|[NPC] Mollim: Leave me to my experiments, stranger!|
|In the Mage Faction ([[Factions]]) (Quest not Completed)|[NPC] Mollim: I'm on the verge of inventing a new enchantment!|
|In the Mage Faction ([[Factions]]) (Quest not Completed)|[NPC] Mollim: So close...so close...|
|First Interaction in the Mage Faction ([[Factions]])|[NPC] Mollim: Azax? [NPC] Mollim: Where is Azax? [NPC] Mollim: I need more bottles! More! [NPC] Mollim: This experiment is close to succeeding, it could be my new Scarlet Prize!|
|Throwing a [[Grand Experience Bottle]]|[NPC] Mollim: Yes! The table. it speaks to me! [NPC] Mollim: It's not quite there yet...|
|Throwing a [[Grand Experience Bottle]] Again|[NPC] Mollim: We are on the right track... [NPC] Mollim: But we need something stronger!|
|Throwing a Titanic ([[Titanic Experience Bottle]]) or [[Colossal Experience Bottle]]|[NPC] Mollim: That's it! [NPC] Mollim: Eureka! [NPC] Mollim: I created a new Enchantment! [NPC] Mollim: As you helped greatly with this achievement, let me bestow upon you the first imbued book!|
|Throwing a Titanic ([[Titanic Experience Bottle]]) or [[Colossal Experience Bottle]] Again|[NPC] Mollim: Just because it worked before doesn't mean it will work again. [NPC] Mollim: Please splash your experience onto someone who is less busy!|
|Throwing an Experience Bottle while not in the Mage Faction ([[Factions]])|[NPC] Mollim: Is that how you behave in that other faction? [NPC] Mollim: Simply rude!|
|Quest Completed|[NPC] Mollim: My next work will be even more grandiose!|
|Quest Completed|[NPC] Mollim: Yes, yes...mhm...yes...interesting...|
|During the Talk to 4 Villagers Quest ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) First Interaction (If they're Mage 1/4)|[NPC] Mollim: Oh, a new face! Welcome to the City of Magic! |(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 2/4); 1,000-2,000 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Mollim: [amount] Intelligence! Wow that's not bad at all!|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 2/4); More than 2,000 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Mollim: [amount] Intelligence! What, how? Who are you even?|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 3/4)|[NPC] Mollim: Have you tried the new ability phone? [NPC] Mollim: The new one is barely better than the one from last year, people still buy them like sheep... [NPC] Mollim: Wait, you don't even have an Abiphone? [NPC] Mollim: Yikes...|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 4/4)|[NPC] Mollim: Ah, the Piglins yes, quite troublesome they are. [NPC] Mollim: I wouldn't go there if I were you.|

# Mort
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Mort]
Also known as: Gate Keeper
Type: [[NPC]]
[Locations]
Description: Allows the player to enter the [[Catacombs]].
Location: [[Catacombs Entrance]]
Coordinates: -88.5 55 -128.5
Description: Allows the player to enter the [[Catacombs]].
Location: [[Dungeon Hub]]
Coordinates: -61.5 123 0.5
Mort is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Dungeon Hub]] and the [[Catacombs Entrance]] near the [[Coal Mine]]. He allows the player to queue to a [[Dungeons|Dungeon]].
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Dialogue
- Grammar and/or punctuation mistakes are intentional, as they appear so in the dialogue in-game.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Mort: Hey [player], welcome to The Catacombs! [NPC] Mort: We hope you have a long stay! [NPC] Mort: Preferably, an eternal adventure. [NPC] Mort: Our forces are made of rotting adventurers. [NPC] Mort: A party of 5 players is crucial. [NPC] Mort: Time to make friends.|
|Below Combat XV (15) ([[Combat]])|[NPC] Mort Wanderer! [NPC] Mort: Save your own skin, seek challenge elsewhere first. [NPC] Mort: You need Combat Level XV before leading a dungeon.|
|Starting Dungeon Run|[NPC] Mort: Here, I found this map when I first entered the dungeon. [NPC] Mort: You should find it useful if you get lost. [NPC] Mort: Good luck.|
|Without a [[Dungeon Orb]]; From Entrance ([[Catacombs Entrance]]) to Floor II ([[The Catacombs - Floor II]])|[NPC] Mort: Don't forget your Dungeon Orb! You claimed Dungeon Orb! [NPC] Mort: It gives each class 2 unique abilities inside Dungeons! [NPC] Mort: Right click the Orb for spells, and Left click (or Drop) to use your Ultimate!|
|Holding [[Saul's Recommendation]]|[NPC] Mort: Welcome back. Are you here to enter The Catacombs again? [NPC] Mort: WHAT? You want to go in alone? I simply cannot allow that to happen. Select an option: [Have a look at this letter.]|
|Selecting [Have a look at this letter.]|[NPC] Mort: Hmm, what's this? [NPC] Mort: I see.. you have a recommendation letter from Saul. Well, I guess its OK then, I suppose. [NPC] Mort: I'll allow you to enter The Catacombs alone from now on. [NPC] Mort: However, let me give you an explicit final warning. [NPC] Mort: The Dungeon you will enter will be exactly the same difficulty as if you joined with a party. [NPC] Mort: After all, The Catacombs won't go easy on you just because you're on your own. [NPC] Mort: You have been warned. I wish you the best of luck.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Mort: ✆ You do know I am dead, right? [NPC] Mort: ✆ Why do you want me on your Abiphone? [NPC] Mort: ✆ Anyway, you can have it... for a price. [NPC] Mort: ✆ Could you give me a Saving Grace? No particular reason. [NPC] Mort: ✆ Select an option: [GIVE SAVING GRACE]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Not Meeting Requirements|[NPC] Mort: ✆ You don't even have a Saving Grace...|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Meeting Requirements (giving [[Saving Grace]])|[NPC] Mort: ✆ Yeah that didn't work. [NPC] Mort: ✆ I was a little too optimistic. [NPC] Mort: ✆ Could you maybe give me a Spirit Mask? [NPC] Mort: ✆ Select an option: [GIVE SPIRIT MASK]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None; After giving [[Saving Grace]]|[NPC] Mort: ✆ The Saving Grace didn't work. [NPC] Mort: ✆ I was a little too optimistic. [NPC] Mort: ✆ Could you maybe give me a Spirit Bone? [NPC] Mort: ✆ Select an option: [GIVE SPIRIT BONE]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Not Meeting Requirements|[NPC] Mort: ✆ You don't even have a Spirit Bone...|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Meeting Requirements (giving [[Spirit Bone]])|[NPC] Mort: ✆ Thanks. [NPC] Mort: ✆ I've accepted that I'm probably going to remain undead for quite a while. [NPC] Mort: ✆ So, while I'm here, I might as well try to make some friends. [NPC] Mort: ✆ First stop is using this here Spirit Bone to tame myself a canine companion. [NPC] Mort: ✆ Thought we ourselves are but a blink in the face of eternity, our friendship will be everlasting. [NPC] Mort: ✆ Alright, thanks for your efforts, take my contact.|
## Trivia
- Mort' s name is a reference to the French word for "death". This references the fact that Mort himself appears to be dead.

# Nall
Categories: Non-player characters, Crimson Isle
[Nall]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Talks to [[Kutral]] about a 4 digit code, which can be used to access a room where [[Nuvian]] sells [[Abiphones]].
Location: [[Dragontail]]
Coordinates: -657.5 107 -738.5
Nall is an [[NPC]] in the [[Dragontail]] brewery that talks to [[Kutral]] about new [[Abiphones]].
## Keypad Code
By standing at [[Coordinates|-655, 107, -741]], the player can overhear Nall and Kutral's conversation, which includes a 4-digit code. The code can be entered at the Keypad located at [[Coordinates|-678, 107, -736]], to access the secret room in which [[Nuvian]] sells [[Abiphones]]. The code changes every day.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Nall: What? I didn't say anything! [NPC] Nall: Stop spying on us... [NPC] Nall: You are intruding.|
|While Eavesdropping|[NPC] Nall: So do you know if they got the new Abiphones? [NPC] Kutral: Ya, they got all of them. It's a bit expensive though. [NPC] Nall: Incredible and do you know the code..? [NPC] Kutral: Yep, but keep it to yourself. [NPC] Nall: Of course. [NPC] Kutral: Today's code is <####>. [NPC] Nall: Ok, got it.|
|Moving While Eavesdropping|[NPC] Nall: Shhhh! [NPC] Nall: Someone is listening to us!|
|During the Talk to 4 Villagers Quest ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 1/4)|[NPC] Nall: Are you a Wizard? [NPC] Nall: Well you don't look like one, and you don't smell like one either. [NPC] Nall: In that case, I have nothing against you. I wish you a sulphuric day sir.|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 2/4)|[NPC] Nall: Hello stranger. Are you looking to join our faction? [NPC] Nall: What a fantastic choice! [NPC] Nall: We have a range of motives far deeper and more interesting than those terrible fire mages. [NPC] Nall: Joining our faction is quite hard but I'm sure an incredible individual like you will manage!|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 3/4)|[NPC] Nall: Sul-phur [NPC] Nall: Sulphur? [NPC] Nall: Me Sulphur.|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 4/4)|[NPC] Nall: Are you a Hero? [NPC] Nall: Did you come to free us from the everlasting threat of the wizards? [NPC] Nall: The mighty Volcano is protecting us, but for how long?|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Barbarians are spoken to|[NPC] Nall: You seem suspicious.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Barbarians are spoken to|[NPC] Nall: Sorry, I can't talk to you right now.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Barbarians are spoken to|[NPC] Nall: Please stop bothering me.|

# Nancy
Aliases: Mayor Nancy, Minister Nancy
Categories: Non-player characters, Mayor, Events, Mayor Election
[Nancy]
Type: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])
Location: [[Community Center]]
Mayor Nancy is a [[Mayor Election|Mayor]] that appears if no mayor has been elected [[Mayor Election|Mayor]]. She has no perks. She can appear for unknown reasons in certain lobbies that haven't updated or on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]] if there is no elected [[Mayor Election|Mayor]].
She also appears as the Minister if a Special Mayor is elected.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
[Current]
|None|[NPC] Mayor Nancy: Hi/ [NPC] Mayor Nancy: I'm just a placeholder because apparently no one voted on the previous election.|
[Minister of a Special Mayor]
|None|[NPC] Minister Nancy: Hi! [NPC] Minister Nancy: I'm just a placeholder because there is no Minister this election.|
[Original]
|None|[NPC] Mayor Nancy: Hello!|
## Trivia
- Nancy formerly used the same skin as [[Clerk Seraphine]].
- Nancy used to only appear on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network|Alpha Server]] until the Better Mayors update.

# Odexar
Categories: Non-player characters, Crimson Isle
Odexar no talent. No style. Fish smart. Odexar dumb.
— [[Odger]]
[Odexar]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Mage Faction Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) Help Dean ([[Quests]])
Description: Tasks the player with fishing up a Volcanic Stonefish ([[Trophy Fish]]) during the Mage Faction ([[Factions]]) Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]).
Location: [[Scarleton]]
Coordinates: -112.5 99 -844.5
Odexar is a trophy fisher in [[Scarleton]]. He is involved in the [[Factions|Mage Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]].
## Quest
Odexar asks the player to take a [[Trophy Fish|Volcanic Stonefish]] to [[Odger]]. Odger will ask the player to fish up a new [[Trophy Fish|Volcanic Stonefish]], as the one Odexar fished up was damaged. The player must give Odger their [[Trophy Fish|Volcanic Stonefish]], and in return they will get a [[Framed Volcanic Stonefish]] to return to Odexar. After returning the Framed Volcanic Stonefish to Odexar, the player will receive [[Factions|100 ቾ Mage Reputation]] and 80 [[Coins]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Odexar: Lava fishing is the best, it is my favorite pastime. [NPC] Odexar: Do you like my collection of Trophies? [NPC] Odexar: Some fishes can only be caught under specific conditions, and it requires great focus.|
|Quest Active (First Interaction)|[NPC] Odexar: My name is Odexar! I have quite the reputation around here. [NPC] Odexar: You see, I'm the best lava fisher in the world. [NPC] Odexar: Recently I fished a magnificent Volcanic Stonefish from a secret spot I won't reveal. [NPC] Odexar: I would like to show it to Odger but I hurt my feet badly. [NPC] Odexar: Would you mind taking my Volcanic Stonefish to Odger's house on top of the Volcano?|
|After claiming the Volcanic Stonefish ([[Trophy Fish]])|[NPC] Odexar: Thanks, I'm sure Odger will be proud, that is the best Trophy Fish I got in a long time!|
|Before giving the [[Framed Volcanic Stonefish]]|[NPC] Odexar: Odger lives in a house inside the Volcano at the very top, you can't miss it.|
|(same as above)|[NPC] Odexar: Trophy Fishing is my passion, and Odger is the local specialist.|
|After giving the [[Framed Volcanic Stonefish]]|[NPC] Odexar: Marvelous! The Fish looks even better than I remembered! [NPC] Odexar: Odger has delicate hands, this is really top quality work! [NPC] Odexar: What do you say? Odger said I was a bad fisherman? [NPC] Odexar: Hahaha!! What a jokester this Odger, love that goblin. [NPC] Odexar: Thanks a lot friend, here take those 80 coins. Don't spend them all in one go!|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None|[NPC] Odexar: My fishing rod is quite amazing but I'm still saving up for a better one, I can't wait!|
|None|[NPC] Odexar: The largest fish in my collection is almost a meter long, and weighs close to 100 lb!|
|After being shown a [[Loudmouth Bass]] with mass of 101 lbs or more|[NPC] Odexar: The largest fish in my collection is almost a meter long, and weighs close to 100 lb. [NPC] Odexar: Wait, that Loudmouth Bass of yours is <mass> lbs... [NPC] Odexar: HOW??? [NPC] Odexar: I really thought I was finally good at something for a while, you know? [NPC] Odexar: And then you came on over here and trampled on my dreams. [NPC] Odexar: Hope you feel real good about yourself, loudmouth.|

# Oen

Oen is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. They talk about how the world is too well designed and might be a simulation.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Oen: I've been observing you, just a bit, nothing creepy. [NPC] Oen: But nonetheless, I can't help but feel like you and I are different. [NPC] Oen: You move around so fast, doing all sorts of crazy things. [NPC] Oen: And I'm just... here. You know? Select an option: [Well...]|
|Selecting [Well...]|[NPC] Oen: Sometimes I feel like I don't belong, like I'm just a background character. [NPC] Oen: I don't even have any ambition. But strangely... I'm happy to exist. [NPC] Oen: I can't help but feel like I'm in some kind of simulation. Not a real world. [NPC] Oen: Well... maybe a real world, but one created as a test. For some other beings. Select an option: [Can't help you with that, sorry]|
|Selecting [Can't help you with that, sorry]|[NPC] Oen: Have you noticed how the world is made up with simple rules? [NPC] Oen: Sometimes, I glimpse something. In the corner of my eye, like threads of code holding the world together. [NPC] Oen: It's like a simple language that creates everything, just a few sets of rules... Select an option: [Sounds like a you problem.]|
|Selecting [Sounds like a you problem.]|[NPC] Oen: Are you sure? [NPC] Oen: The way I see it your world is also quite simple. [NPC] Oen: Quarks, Leptons, Gauge Bosons, just a few building blocks. [NPC] Oen: Only 4 four fundamental interactions, fields and spacetime. Select an option: [Hold up, how do you know that?]|
|Selecting [Hold up, how do you know that?]|[NPC] Oen: Think about it, doesn't it feel strangely well designed? [NPC] Oen: With just a few ingredients you get a hundred elements, millions of molecules... [NPC] Oen: And from that, oceans, storms, thought, emotion, life itself. [NPC] Oen: Doesn't that seem... too elegant? Too well designed to be random? [NPC] Oen: Are you really sure you are not in some kind of giant experiment, a game, yourself? Select an option: [I've had enough, this isn't fun anymore]|
|Selecting [I've had enough, this isn't fun anymore]|[NPC] Oen: If I can see the matrix sometimes... maybe you can too. [NPC] Oen: Think about it...|
## Trivia
- Oen references The Matrix movie, both in dialogue and appearance. His name is "Neo" backwards, which is a character in The Matrix.
- Oen is one of 5 [[NPC]]'s to mention the "simulation" or the simulated state of the world of SkyBlock. Others include the [[Royal Resident]], the [[Cult of the Fallen Star]] members in meetings #4 - #6, [[Yoshua]], and [[Skylark]].

[SkyBlock Prototype]

# Old Man Garry
Aliases: Garry
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Dwarven Mines, Events
[Old Man Garry]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Introduces the player to Events ([[Dwarven Mines]]) in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
Location: [[Dwarven Village]]
Coordinates: 5.375 200 -109.218
Old Man Garry is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Dwarven Village]]. He manages [[Dwarven Mines|Events]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Old Man Garry: Mining Events on the Dwarven Mines happen regularly! [NPC] Old Man Garry: They are a good way to earn Heart of the Mountain Exp, complete Commissions, and more! [NPC] Old Man Garry: If you ever need to know more about Mining Events, come talk to me!|
## Mechanics
When there are 20s before the next event starts, a message appears in chat announcing what the event is. Clicking the message will warp the player to Old Man Garry. During an active event, Old Man Garry will offer to warp the player to the location where the event is happening.
- Active Events
- [[Goblin Raid]]
- [[Raffle]]
- [[Mithril Gourmand]]
- Passive Events
- [[Gone with the Wind]]
- [[Better Together]]
- [[2x Powder]]
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Old Man Garry: People have no manners these days. They come up to me, and disappear instantly! They don't even greet me! Back in my day, we were raised better than that.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Old Man Garry: It turns out, manners haven't all been lost yet.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Old Man Garry: Of course. What else did I expect from this generation.|
## Trivia
- Although Old Man Garry does not appear in [[Crystal Hollows]], passive events can still occur in there.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added Old Man Garry.|

# Old Timer

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction (Without having obtained [[Timite]])|[NPC] Old Timer: Ever heard of Timite? Select an option: [Can't say I have.]|
|Selecting [Can't say I have.]|[NPC] Old Timer: Ah, a fresh face! Timite is an ore in these parts! [NPC] Old Timer: Timite is a bit of a shape-shifter. [NPC] Old Timer: When it's young and spry, we call it Youngite. Full of energy but doesn't know a thing! [NPC] Old Timer: Then Youngite ages into Timite, wise and valuable! [NPC] Old Timer: And with a little extra push, it becomes Obsolite, a grumpy old thing, complains about the 'good old days'. Select an option: [And how do I give it that 'little push'?]|
|Selecting [And how do I give it that 'little push'?]|[NPC] Old Timer: Glad you asked! You need a Time Gun. [NPC] Old Timer: Craft one using enough Youngite. Point it at an ore, and bam! You've aged it faster than cheese left in the sun. Select an option: [So I shoot rocks with a Time Gun to make them older?]|
|Selecting [So I shoot rocks with a Time Gun to make them older?]|[NPC] Old Timer: Precisely! And the older the ore, the more valuable it gets. Just like me! [NPC] Old Timer: Now go one, make those ores feel their age. Time's a-wastin'!|
|Idle Dialogue|[NPC] Old Timer: These bones ain't what they used to be. [NPC] Old Timer: Maybe I should give myself a zap with the Time Gun... or maybe not.|
|Idle Dialogue|[NPC] Old Timer: I tried using the Time Gun on my tea once. [NPC] Old Timer: Ended up with a cup of dust!|

Ozanne is is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Thaumaturgist]]. She offers a refund for [[Reforging|Reforges]] on [[Accessories]] that were Reforged with the [[Blacksmith]].
## Refunds
Ozanne will refund the player [[Coins]], depending on the rarity of their Accessories and the reforges applied to the Accessories. The maximum amount of coins she will refund is 5,000,000 [[Coins]] before she stops refunding.

|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|5,000 [[Coins]]|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|10,000 [[Coins]]|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|20,000 [[Coins]]|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|50,000 [[Coins]]|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|100,000 [[Coins]]|
Reforge:
|Reforge|[[Coins]]|
|-|-|
|Sweet ([[Reforging]])|40,000 [[Coins]]|
|Silky ([[Reforging]])|50,000 [[Coins]]|
|Bloody ([[Reforging]])|250,000 [[Coins]]|
|Shaded ([[Reforging]])|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|

# Paul
Aliases: Candidate Paul, Mayor Paul, Minister Paul, Paul the Dungeon Explorer
Categories: Non-player characters, Mayor, Events, Mayor Election
[Paul Paul]
Also known as: Mayor Paul
Type: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])
[Locations]
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]), or nearby when elected as Minister ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Community Center]]
Coordinates: 6.5 79 19.5
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]). Also appears on an outer stand when running to become Mayor or Minister ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Election Room]]
Coordinates: -3.5 49 34.5
Tooltip: Year # Candidate; Votes: # (#%); Last elected: #y ago; __________________________ Marauder Dungeon reward chests are 20% cheaper.; EZPZ Gain 10 bonus score on dungeon runs.; Benediction Blessings are 25% stronger.; __________________________; ✯ Minister Perks are also granted if this mayor wins second place!; Click to vote for Paul!
Paul the Dungeon Explorer is a mayoral candidate that can be elected during the [[Mayor Election]] event. His perks focus around the [[Catacombs]].
## Mayoral Perks
Candidate Paul may run for election with up to 3 possible perks.
  [[Paul]] Paul the Dungeon Explorer
1. Marauder: [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon reward chests]] are 20% cheaper.
2. EZPZ: Gain 10 extra bonus score in [[Dungeons]].
3. Benediction: [[Dungeon Blessing|Blessings]] are 25% stronger.
## Dialogue
- The color of the NPC's name changes depending on which color they are in the current [[Mayor Election]]. Yellow has been used as a default color.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Paul: Hey, I know a few dungeon tricks. [NPC] Paul: If you vote for me I'll teach you!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Mayor Paul: All this paperwork is making me hungry.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Mayor Paul: I yearn for the dungeons, [player].|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Mayor Paul: Being a mayor is downtime, anyways.|
|Condition|Dialogue|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Paul: You merely adopted the Dungeons, I was born in them, molded by them.|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Paul: I used to be a dungeon adventurer, until I took an arrow to the elbow.|
## Trivia
- Despite Paul stating that he "took an arrow to the elbow", there's no bandage or wound there. This is a reference to The Elder Scrolls V: Skyrim.
- Paul's "I yearn for the dungeons" dialogue could potentially be a reference to A Minecraft Movie, in which Steve states that "As a child, [he] yearned for the mines".
- When the EZPZ perk is active, an S+ [[Catacombs]] [[The Catacombs - Floor VI|F6]] or [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|F7]] run may have a maximum of 9 deaths (10 if the player who dies first has a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Spirit Pet]]).
- Paul's line "You merely adopted the Dungeons, I was born in them, molded by them." is spoken in parallel with [[Necron]]'s line "YOU MERELY ADOPTED THE CATACOMBS, I WAS MOLDED BY THEM!", both of which are a reference to a line in The Dark Knight Rises spoken by Bane "Oh, you think darkness is your ally. You merely adopted the dark; I was born in it, moulded by it. I didn't see the light until I was already a man, by then it was nothing to me but BLINDING!".
- On June 3, 2024, when what would become 0.20.3 — Better Mayors update first made it on [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], the following has been said: "You didn't hear this from me but apparently Aatrox and Paul failed to hand in their new campaign plans in enough time. (...), and Paul had no Abiphone Cell Service while running M4 (which sounds like the least of his problems)."
  - No news about their 4th perks have been revealed since.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Paul as a Garden Visitor ([[The Garden]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Sep 8, 2020|0.9|Added Paul.|
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Paul as a Garden Visitor ([[The Garden]]).|

Piglin Guards are [[NPC|NPCs]] that appear throughout the [[Crimson Isle]], either in the [[Chief's Hut]] during the [[Factions|Barbarian Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]], [[The Bastion]] during the [[Factions|Mage Faction]] questline, or in [[The Wasteland]] at the end of both questlines.
## Quests
During the [[Factions|Barbarian Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]], two Piglin Guards briefly appear in the [[Chief's Hut]] on three separate occasions: once when [[Chief Scorn]] kicks out [[Ugo]] and [[Elle]] after the player joins the faction, again when they also get kicked out after discussing stolen research, and at the end when just [[Ugo]] is kicked out.
During the [[Factions|Mage Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]], two Piglin Guards appear in [[The Bastion]], when the player is instructed to steal research from there. After the research has been stolen, they disappear, along with the other [[Guard|Guards]].
Two Piglin Guards also appear in [[The Wasteland]] when the two [[Factions]] meet each other at the end of either [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Trying to enter a restricted area in [[The Bastion]]|[NPC] Piglin Guard: Hey, this is a restricted area. Leave now!|

# Plate
Categories: Non-player characters
[Plate]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Tells the player how to start the Temporal Race ([[Wizardman]]).
Location: [[Trial Grounds]]
Coordinates: 46.5 132 24.5
Plate is an [[NPC]] found within the [[Trial Grounds]] of [[The Mountaintop]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It only appears while competing with [[Wizardman]] in his [[Wizardman|Temporal Race]] trial.
## Purpose
Plate serves to tell the player to press the READY hologram to start [[Wizardman]]'s [[Wizardman|Temporal Race]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Step; (Before starting the race)|[NPC] Plate: Ow! That's my face you're stepping on! [NPC] Plate: Show some respect and punch the "READY" hologram, please.|
|Future Step-Ons; (Before starting the race)|[NPC] Plate: Ah, the sweet sensation of being stepped on again. If only there was a way to avoid this!|
|Future Step-Ons; (Before starting the race)|[NPC] Plate: Ouch! Did you miss the READY sign or do you just enjoy causing pain?|
|Future Step-Ons; (Before starting the race)|[NPC] Plate: Ever considered punching something? Say, a hologram?|
|Future Step-Ons; (Before starting the race)|[NPC] Plate: Fantastic, you've found the floor.|
|Future Step-Ons; (Before starting the race)|[NPC] Plate: Here we go again...|
|Future Step-Ons; (Before starting the race)|[NPC] Plate: Marvelous, another footprint for my collection!|

# Porhtal
Aliases: Porthal, The 7th Sin, The Baba Yaga, The Bankster, The Gill-Man, The Gooey, The Intruder, The Prince
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Porhtal]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Unlocks the ability for players to teleport to certain locations in the [[Rift Dimension]].
Location: [[Broken Cage]]
Coordinates: -157.5 70.5 162
Porhtal is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Broken Cage]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. They give access to their eyes, which allow the player to teleport to various locations.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Porhtal: I'm looking for my Liege, but my Eyes went rogue. [NPC] Porhtal: If you calm one of them down, I will be able to teleport you to it.|
|After calming all the Eyes|[NPC] Porhtal: Before you venture into The Rift again, I must thank you. [NPC] Porhtal: You have calmed all of my eyes. [NPC] Porhtal: I now comprehend my purpose here. [NPC] Porhtal: I am but a creation, a link through time, a messenger, a catalyst. [NPC] Porhtal: Soon, our destinies will intertwine once more. [NPC] Porhtal: You must repair the breach, for I am to receive and deliver a vision from it.|
|After annoying one of their Eyes|[NPC] [Eye]: Quit it!|
## Porhtal's Eyes
### Locations
|Name|Location|Coordinates|Reward|Beam Damage|DPS|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|The Intruder|Wizard Tower ([[Wizard Tower (Rift)]])|-51, 105, 71 ([[Coordinates]])|10 [[Rift Time]]|2.5 [[Rift Time]]|5 [[Rift Time]]|
|The Gill-Man|[[Lagoon Hut]]|-206, 66, 50 ([[Coordinates]])|20 [[Rift Time]]|5 [[Rift Time]]|10 [[Rift Time]]|
|The Baba Yaga|[[Dreadfarm]]|-15, 72, -174 ([[Coordinates]])|30 [[Rift Time]]|5 [[Rift Time]]|10 [[Rift Time]]|
|The Bankster|[[Village Plaza]]|31, 70, -37 ([[Coordinates]])|40 [[Rift Time]]|7.5 [[Rift Time]]|15 [[Rift Time]]|
|The Gooey|Colosseum ([[Colosseum (Rift)]])|-166, 78, -54 ([[Coordinates]])|50 [[Rift Time]]|10 [[Rift Time]]|20 [[Rift Time]]|
|The Prince|[[Stillgore Château]]|158, 75, 79 ([[Coordinates]])|60 [[Rift Time]]|12.5 [[Rift Time]]|25 [[Rift Time]]|
|The 7th Sin|[[The Mountaintop]]|48, 170, 40 ([[Coordinates]])|70 [[Rift Time]]|60 [[Rift Time]]|30 [[Rift Time]]|
### Usage
Each eye has 300 [[Health]]. After being calmed by dealing enough damage to it, it grants a certain permanent amount of [[Rift Time]] and is unlocked. Once unlocked, it takes 5s [[Rift Time]] per warp. If the player hasn't paid the time to unlock the zone and any previous zones, the time necessary to access them would be added to the travel time cost, but is reduced by 10%. For example traveling to [[Village Plaza]] requires 4m00s from [[Black Lagoon]], 3m00s from [[West Village]], and 8m00s from [[Village Plaza]], which is in total 15m00s [[Rift Time]] or 900s but it only requires 13m30s [[Rift Time]] or 810s .
Areas that the player does not need to pass through to visit any other area ([[Mirrorverse]], and [[Living Cave]]) are never included.
Destinations that are not required to enter another destination are only included when traveled to. So [[Barrier Street]] is only included when traveling to [[Stillgore Château]], [[Colosseum (Rift)|Colosseum]] only when traveling to Colosseum, [[The Mountaintop]] only when traveling to Mountaintop.
## Trivia
- By clicking and rapidly closing the interface on the Eyes, and then rapidly attacking them, anvil sounds will play and the respective Eye will yell "Quit it!" before launching the player back. This can be used to quickly reach the [[Rift Dimension|Rift Transfer Chest]] in the [[Wyld Woods]] by boosting the player from the [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]] with enough velocity to be able to reach the treetops with two [[Aspect of the Leech]] teleports.
- All of Porhtal's eyes are various references.
  - The Intruder is a reference to the 2019 psychological thriller film of the same name.
  - The Gill-Man is a reference to the antagonist of the same name of the film Creature from the Black Lagoon and its two sequels.
  - The Baba Yaga is a reference to the supernatural creature from Slavic folklore.
  - The Bankster is a reference to the thriller novel Bankster.
  - The Prince is likely a reference to the 2014 action thriller film The Prince.
  - The 7th Sin is possibly a reference to the 1917 silent drama film The Seventh Sin.
- The reverse of Porhtal is Lathrop, which is the human name of [[The Watcher]].
  - Porhtal could be the Rift version of [[The Watcher]], but apart from the name and association with eyes, there is no proof of this.
## History

|May 4, 2023|Added Porhtal.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Porhtal.|

|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Added the eye The 7th Sin.|
|Dec 13, 2024|Fixed The 7th Sin respawning while being fought.|

# Professor Robot
Aliases: Automaton Part, Automaton Parts
Categories: Non-player characters, Crystal Hollows
[Professor Robot]

Description: Allows the player to access the Sapphire Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]) after giving them robot parts.
Location: [[Lost Precursor City]]
Professor Robot is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Lost Precursor City]]. He grants the player access to the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]].
## Quest
In order to obtain the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]], Professor Robot must be given the following items:
- [[Electron Transmitter]]
- [[FTX 3070]]
- [[Robotron Reflector]]
- [[Superlite Motor]]
- [[Control Switch]]
- [[Synthetic Heart]]
Or
- [[Precursor Apparatus]]
They cost a total of or respectively.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Professor Robot: This citadel used to be the epicenter of fructuous engineering. [NPC] Professor Robot: The inhabitants, Precursors, as they were once called, laid the foundations for the future. [NPC] Professor Robot: However, they have been extinct for eons - only leaving the remnants of the Automatons behind. [NPC] Professor Robot: One rather peculiar discovery is the Automaton Prime - the giant robot towering above me. The exact purpose of it is unknown, although it appears to be guarding something. [NPC] Professor Robot: There seems to be 6 key components to the giant that are missing. [NPC] Professor Robot: Each part - the Electron Transmitter, FTX 3070, Robotron Reflector, Superlite Motor, Control Switch, and a Synthetic Heart plays a distinctive role in the creation of this behemoth. [NPC] Professor Robot: I think the Automatons nearby are built with similar components. Find them and bring them to me!|
|Without a Component; This dialogue varies depending on items already given.|[NPC] Professor Robot: That's not one of the components I need! Bring me one of the missing components: Electron Transmitter FTX 3070 Robotron Reflector Superlite Motor Control Switch Synthetic Heart|
|Giving a Component|[NPC] Professor Robot: Thanks for bringing me the <Component>! Bring me <missing count> more components to fix the giant!|
|Giving a Component that was already given|[NPC] Professor Robot: You have already delivered that part to me! Bring me a unique robot part!|
|Given All 6 Components|[NPC] Professor Robot: You've brought me all of the components! [NPC] Professor Robot: I have uploaded them to the giant below...it appears it has opened its storage compartment! [NPC] Professor Robot: Perhaps the long-lost Sapphire Crystal is in there!|
|Giving a [[Precursor Apparatus]]|[NPC] Professor Robot: That's not one of the compon- [NPC] Professor Robot: Wait a minute. This will work just fine. [NPC] Professor Robot: I have uploaded this to the giant below...it appears it has opened its storage compartment! [NPC] Professor Robot: Perhaps the long-lost Sapphire Crystal is in there!|
|Given 5 Components with [[Mole Pet]]'s perk activating|[NPC] Professor Robot: You've brought me all of the components... I think? To be honest, I kind of lost count... [NPC] Professor Robot: I have uploaded them to the giant below...it appears it has opened its storage compartment! [NPC] Professor Robot: Perhaps the long-lost Sapphire Crystal is in there!|
|After Obtaining Sapphire Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]])|[NPC] Professor Robot: You haven't placed the Sapphire Crystal yet! [NPC] Professor Robot: Go to the Crystal Nucleus to place it!|

# Puzzler
Aliases: The Puzzler
Categories: Non-player characters, Dwarven Mines, Quest giver
[Puzzler]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Allows the player to earn 2,500 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] by completing a puzzle.
Location: [[Dwarven Mines]]
Coordinates: 181.5 196 135.5
The Puzzler is an [[NPC]] located in a room next to the [[Grand Library]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Mechanics
Every day, the Puzzler will give the player a simple puzzle composing of directions. Upon completing the puzzle, the player will be rewarded with 2500 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]].
Players can solve its puzzle once every 24 hours, with the countdown starting when the players finish the last puzzle, rather than a set time.
### Puzzle
Clicking on the Puzzler will generate a set of random arrows. The player must begin by standing on the [[Glowstone]] block, and face the entrance of the room. Each triangle represents one block, and the player should turn when indicated. The block the player ends up on can then be mined to grant the aforementioned ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]. The glowstone that the puzzler stands on, as well as the surrounding stairs, both count as blocks that can be broken.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Puzzle|Random arrows.|
|Solving Puzzle|[NPC] Puzzler: ✌✓ [NPC] Puzzler: ▶▶Nice! ▲Here, ◀have ▼some◀ ▶Mithril Powder!▲ Puzzler gave you 2,500 Mithril Powder for solving the puzzle!|
|Incorrect|[NPC] Puzzler: ╳ (╯°□°）╯︵ ┻━┻ [NPC] Puzzler: ▶▲Wrong▲ ◀block◀ ▼▶try again▶▶|
|Already Solved|[NPC] Puzzler: ▶▲Come▲ ◀back◀ ▼▶tomorrow▶▶|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Puzzler: ▲▼triangle▼▶▲food◀▲|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Puzzler: (⌐■_■)✌|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Puzzler: ╳ (ノಠ益ಠ)ノ彡┻━┻|
## Trivia
- Like the [[Powder Ghast]], [[Golden Goblin]], or commission pick-up, the 2500 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] reward is affected by the [[2x Powder]] event.
- The floor of the Puzzler's room used to be made of [[Mithril|Mithril Ore]], [[Stone]] and [[Ores|Iron Ore]], but it was changed as people used it to farm [[Golden Goblin|Golden Goblins]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added Puzzler.|
|Jan 20, 2021|Changed the floor of puzzler's room to wood.|
|Apr 24, 2024|0.20.1|Fixed the solve message not accounting for the Powder Buff ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) perk.|
|Oct 15, 2025|0.23.5|Increased the amount of ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] Puzzler gives from 1,000 to 2,500.|

# Rabbit Bro
Categories: Non-player characters
[Rabbit Bro]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Acts as an employee ([[Chocolate Factory]]) for the [[Chocolate Factory]].
Location: [[Rabbit House]]
Coordinates: 57.5 70 5.5
Rabbit Bro is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Rabbit House]] that generates chocolate through the [[Chocolate Factory]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before the opening of the [[Chocolate Factory]]|[NPC] Rabbit Bro: The Chocolate Factory is opening soon! [NPC] Rabbit Bro: I sure hope I get hired when it opens!|
|Unemployed|[NPC] Rabbit Bro: Get me a job at your factory, bro!|
|Unemployed|[NPC] Rabbit Bro: Hire me, bro!|
|Unemployed|[NPC] Rabbit Bro: You should hire me! I can help you boost your Chocolate Factory production tenfold by next quarter!|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Bro: Hoppity's living the sweet life now, but who's the one who taught him to dodge those garden gnomes? Bro knows best.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Bro: Hoppity thinks he's all that with his chocolate empire. [NPC] Rabbit Bro: But ask him this...who can hop the highest?|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Bro: My morning routine? A quick hop around the fields, a bit of carrot juice, and then straight to work on the next big chocolate innovation. [NPC] Rabbit Bro: Sleep is for the weak!|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Bro: Working out before dawn has its perks; you get to see Hoppity's 'inspirational' morning pep talks. [NPC] Rabbit Bro: It's like a shot of espresso, but with more hopping and less coffee.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Bro: Rise and grind, as I say. The early rabbit gets the cocoa beans. [NPC] Rabbit Bro: Hoppity may run the factory, but who do you think inspires the work ethic?|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Bro: What represents a sigma grindset for me? It's about being powered by my own ambitions, not the expectations laid out by others. [NPC] Rabbit Bro: Each day I commit to the hard work, the relentless pursuit of my personal goals. [NPC] Rabbit Bro: It's not about what the world thinks I should achieve; it's about what I know I can accomplish with dedication and effort.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Bro: My grindset is my commitment to never settling for less than what I know I can achieve. [NPC] Rabbit Bro: It's not about being the best among others, but being the best version of myself.|
|[[Rabbit Uncle]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Bro: If you need a rundown of Uncle's high school football stats, just stick around for five minutes. [NPC] Rabbit Bro: He'll bring it up somehow, even if we are talking about chocolate molds.|
|[[Rabbit Dog]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Bro: That dog works harder than any of us, I swear. It's that dogged determination, you know? [NPC] Rabbit Bro: Just built differently, always fetching, always carrying. Must be nice not needing a coffee break.|
|[[Rabbit Dog]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Bro: Let's be honest, Rabbit Dog outpaces us all, but hey, he's a dog and we're just rabbits. [NPC] Rabbit Bro: It's in his nature to run circles around us, literally. While we strategize, he's already on his third lap. Just born for the hustle, I guess!|
|Chocolate Factory IV ([[Chocolate Factory]]) or higher|[NPC] Rabbit Bro: Coach is the only rabbit I know whose biceps have biceps.|
## Trivia
- Rabbit Bro's name's color in chat will match his Rarity in [[Chocolate Factory]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Apr 9, 2024|Added Rabbit Bro.|
|Jan 30, 2026|Moved Rabbit Bro from 8.5, 71, 19.5 ([[Coordinates]]) to 65.5, 81, -59.5 ([[Coordinates]]), due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 24, 2024|0.20.1|Added Rabbit Bro.|
|Apr 28, 2024|Rabbit Bro can now be hired.|

# Rabbit Cousin
Categories: Non-player characters
[Rabbit Cousin]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Acts as an employee ([[Chocolate Factory]]) for the [[Chocolate Factory]].
Location: [[Rabbit House]]
Coordinates: 69.59375 68 4.5
Rabbit Cousin is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Rabbit House]] that generates chocolate in the [[Chocolate Factory]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Unemployed|[NPC] Rabbit Cousin: I need a job soon, or my parents will kick me out of the warren.|
|Unemployed|[NPC] Rabbit Cousin: My parents have been hounding me to get a job! Please hire me!|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Cousin: I suggested chocolate-covered carrots to Hoppity once. He laughed until he tried it. Now, who's laughing? [NPC] Rabbit Cousin: Still him, because it was a terrible idea.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Cousin: I tell ya, family gatherings got a lot more interesting when Hoppity started bringing those experimental chocolates. [NPC] Rabbit Cousin: Remember the carrot crunch debacle?|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Cousin: Working hard or hardly working? With chocolate, it's both. I might drift in past noon, but when I'm on, I'm on fire. [NPC] Rabbit Cousin: Hoppity knows the score.|
|[[Rabbit Granny]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Cousin: They say genius looks like laziness. Guess I'm living proof. [NPC] Rabbit Cousin: When I finally get to it, even Granny's impressed with the flavors I whip up.|
|[[Rabbit Uncle]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Cousin: Uncle is an inspiration, really. [NPC] Rabbit Cousin: He inspires me to care less about the details, because nothing ever works out in the end.|
|[[Rabbit Dog]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Cousin: Rabbit Dog is so youthful and full of energy. [NPC] Rabbit Cousin: He stinks pretty badly, though.|
|Chocolate Factory IV ([[Chocolate Factory]]) or higher|[NPC] Rabbit Cousin: Coach wants us to push to our limits. I asked if that includes pushing the snooze button. He wasn't amused.|
## Trivia
- Rabbit Cousin's name's color in chat will match their Rarity in [[Chocolate Factory]].

# Rabbit Daddy
Categories: Non-player characters
[Rabbit Daddy]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Acts as an employee ([[Chocolate Factory]]) for the [[Chocolate Factory]].
Location: [[Rabbit House]]
Coordinates: 76.5 65 13.5
Rabbit Daddy is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Rabbit House]] that generates chocolate in the [[Chocolate Factory]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Unemployed|[NPC] Rabbit Daddy: I am the heir to the chocolate throne. [NPC] Rabbit Daddy: The only thing in my way? You. Please hire me, boss.|
|Unemployed|[NPC] Rabbit Daddy: In life there are sharks and there are sheep. [NPC] Rabbit Daddy: I'm a shark. [NPC] Rabbit Daddy: Well, I'm a rabbit. But you get the idea.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Daddy: Every day, it's a new challenge. If it's not the market, it's Sis with her picket signs. [NPC] Rabbit Daddy: Still, we're making the world sweeter, one chocolate bar at a time.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Daddy: Every family has its ups and downs, but ours? [NPC] Rabbit Daddy: We've got a whole soap opera thanks to Sis.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Daddy: Heir to the chocolate throne, they say. [NPC] Rabbit Daddy: More like heir to a bunch of headaches, courtesy of my own daughter's protests.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Daddy: Hoppity wants us to 'think outside the box.' Last time I did that, we ended up with chocolate-covered grass. [NPC] Rabbit Daddy: Sold out in a week. Shows what I know.|
|[[Rabbit Granny]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Daddy: Between Bro's grindset, Cousin's relaxed approach, and Granny's wisdom, we've got all the ingredients for success. [NPC] Rabbit Daddy: Now, if only Sis would see that.|
|[[Rabbit Granny]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Daddy: Bro's dedication is what this company needs more of. And Cousin, well, he brings...creativity. [NPC] Rabbit Daddy: Granny? She's the glue holding us all together.|
|[[Rabbit Uncle]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Daddy: Uncle's advice for any problem: 'Think like it's the final game, down to the last second.' [NPC] Rabbit Daddy: Useful? Sometimes. Relevant? Rarely.|
|[[Rabbit Uncle]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Daddy: Trying to get through a meal without Uncle mentioning the semifinals is like trying to find a needle in a haystack. [NPC] Rabbit Daddy: Impossible, but you never stop hoping.|
|[[Rabbit Dog]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Daddy: He might just be fetching tools or delivering messages, but Rabbit Dog's got a work ethic we could all learn from. [NPC] Rabbit Daddy: Pure, unthinking, relentless energy. Guess that's just how dogs are wired.|
|Chocolate Factory IV ([[Chocolate Factory]]) or higher|[NPC] Rabbit Daddy: Coach could motivate a snail to enter a sprint. It's exhausting, but you've got to admire that kind of energy.|
## Trivia
- Rabbit Daddy's name's color in chat will match his Rarity in [[Chocolate Factory]].

Rabbit Dog is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Rabbit House]] that generates chocolate in the [[Chocolate Factory]].
## Dialogue
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.

|Condition|Random Dialogue|Sound|
|-|-|-|
|Always|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: Woof!|Wolf idle|
|Always|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: Grr...|Wolf growls|
|Always|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: Arf!|Wolf idle|
|Always|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: *pants*|Wolf pants|
|Always|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: *whines*|Wolf whine|
|Always|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: Bork.|Wolf idle|
|Always|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: Bark!|Wolf idle|
|Always|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: Ah-woo!|Wolf howl|
|Employed|||
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: Ah, Rabbit Bro, tirelessly toiling from the break of dawn, clinging to his routines with the desperation of a shipwreck survivor to a life raft. [NPC] Rabbit Dog: One wonders if he runs from failure or merely jogs alongside it.|Villager agrees (x2)|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: Observe the tranquil Rabbit Cuz, philosopher of sloth, whose profound punctuality issues suggest a man deeply at peace with life's ephemeral nature, or perhaps just deeply asleep.|Villager agrees|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: And there strides Rabbit Sis, torchbearer of tumult, whose fervent protests might one day change the world, if they don't first incite her to single-handedly dismantle it, piece by bureaucratic piece.|Villager agrees|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: Ever observant Rabbit Daddy, guardian of the gold, manages finances with a paranoia that would make a conspiracy theorist blush. [NPC] Rabbit Dog: His ledger is tighter than a drum, his brow perpetually furrowed in fiscal fear.|Villager agrees (x2)|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: Granny, with anecdotes as endless as eternity, dispenses wisdom like a vending machine stuck on dispense, whether you requested it or not. [NPC] Rabbit Dog: Each story a subtle reminder that history repeats itself, especially at family gatherings.|Villager agrees (x2)|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: Rabbit Uncle, our nostalgic knight, battles valiantly against the inexorable march of time, jousting with memories of glory days that the rest of us suspect were rather less glorious than recounted.|Villager agrees|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: Rabbit Coach, the relentless fountain of fervor, whose motivational speeches soar so high they often escape the grasp of practical application. [NPC] Rabbit Dog: One imagines his dreams fly similarly, unburdened by the gravity of reality.|Villager agrees (x2)|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: In the pursuit of excellence, one must remember that the journey is as significant as the destination. [NPC] Rabbit Dog: True success lies not in the accolades, but in the lessons learned along the way.|Villager agrees (x2)|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: Empathy is the silent language of the soul, spoken through actions rather than words. [NPC] Rabbit Dog: To truly understand another, one must listen not only with ears but with the heart.|Villager agrees (x2)|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: In every moment of decision, we stand at the crossroads of countless possibilities. [NPC] Rabbit Dog: The paths we choose not only define our destiny but also reflect the essence of our being.|Villager agrees (x2)|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: Wisdom often comes to us in whispers, through the leaves of the trees or the ripple of the waters. [NPC] Rabbit Dog: It teaches us that every voice, no matter how small, has something of value to say.|Villager agrees (x2)|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: Solitude is not the absence of company, but the moment when our soul is free to speak to us, unencumbered by the chaos of the world.|Villager agrees|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: The greatest paradox of life is that in seeking control, we often surrender it. [NPC] Rabbit Dog: True power comes not from dominion over others, but from mastery over oneself.|Villager agrees (x2)|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Dog: Consider the possibility that limitations are merely figments of our imagination, crafted by our fears and insecurities. [NPC] Rabbit Dog: To break free, one must first believe in the boundlessness of their own potential.|Villager agrees (x2)|
## Trivia
- Rabbit Dog's name color in chat will match their Rarity in the [[Chocolate Factory]].

- Rabbit Dog's dialogue plays dog sounds.

# Rabbit Granny
Categories: Non-player characters
e
[Rabbit Granny]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Acts as an employee ([[Chocolate Factory]]) for the [[Chocolate Factory]].
Location: [[Rabbit House]]
Coordinates: 58.5 68 10.5
Rabbit Granny is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Rabbit House]] that generates chocolate in the [[Chocolate Factory]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Unemployed|[NPC] Rabbit Granny: I'm looking to come out of retirement for one last job. [NPC] Rabbit Granny: Plus, I decided I don't think I could ever give up chocolate.|
|Unemployed|[NPC] Rabbit Granny: The Chocolate Factory used to be different before Hoppity arrived. [NPC] Rabbit Granny: You never know that you're in the glory days until it's too late.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Granny: Every time Hoppity talks about 'expanding the business', I remind him: 'Don't forget to expand your heart too.' [NPC] Rabbit Granny: He's a good boy, he listens.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Granny: Hoppity wants to automate the chocolate wrapping. I told him, 'Nothing beats the personal touch.' [NPC] Rabbit Granny: He gave me a computerized knitting machine.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Granny: In my days, we settled disputes over a hot cup of cocoa. [NPC] Rabbit Granny: Maybe that's what Sis and my son need - a reminder of what binds us.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Granny: In my day, we made sweets from whatever was in the garden. Hoppity just added cocoa. [NPC] Rabbit Granny: Kids these days think they've invented sugar.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Granny: I've watched this family business grow from a single carrot patch to rows of cocoa trees. [NPC] Rabbit Granny: Each bar we produce carries that legacy. Makes an old bunny proud.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Granny: They say you can't choose your family. But if I could, I'd choose this chocolate-crazed bunch every time. [NPC] Rabbit Granny: From Dust Bowl to chocolate empire, we've come a long way.|
|[[Rabbit Uncle]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Granny: Uncle insists on playing the high school fight song during our morning stretches. [NPC] Rabbit Granny: He says that it 'sets the tone'. It's charming until it's you tenth listen for the week.|
|[[Rabbit Dog]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Granny: That dog of ours, always bustling about. You'd think he understands the stakes. [NPC] Rabbit Granny: But it's just his nature, always wanting to please, always ready to help. Bless his loyal heart.|
|Chocolate Factory IV ([[Chocolate Factory]]) or higher|[NPC] Rabbit Granny: That coach could talk a carrot into growing faster. It's a talent, but sometimes I just want him to turn the volume down.|
## Trivia
- Rabbit Granny's name's color in chat will match her Rarity in [[Chocolate Factory]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jan 30, 2026|Moved Rabbit Granny from 9.5, 69, 23.5 ([[Coordinates]]) to 58.5, 68, 10.5 ([[Coordinates]]), due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|

|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Moved Rabbit Granny from 9.5, 69, 23.5 ([[Coordinates]]) to 58.5, 68, 10.5 ([[Coordinates]]), due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|

# Rabbit Sis
Categories: Non-player characters
[Rabbit Sis]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Acts as an employee ([[Chocolate Factory]]) for the [[Chocolate Factory]].
Location: [[Rabbit House]]
Coordinates: 68.5 68 12.5
Rabbit Sis is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Rabbit House]] that generates chocolate in the [[Chocolate Factory]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Unemployed|[NPC] Rabbit Sis: Down with Big Chocolate! [NPC] Rabbit Sis: Regulate! Regulate! Regulate!|
|Unemployed|[NPC] Rabbit Sis: Hire me, player! [NPC] Rabbit Sis: Together we can abolish the patriarchy, and ensure clean chocolate for all rabbits!|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Sis: Bro's always talking about his 'sigma grindset'. Tried to get me to read a book on it. [NPC] Rabbit Sis: I told him I'd start my own movement: the 'chocolate mindfulness mindset'.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Sis: Chocolate is love, chocolate is life. But at what cost? [NPC] Rabbit Sis: It's time this family faces the music and listens to the cocoa beans' side of the story!|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Sis: Cousin might be the laziest bunny I know, but he's onto something. [NPC] Rabbit Sis: Why protest when you can just 'be the change'? By napping, apparently.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Sis: Hoppity actually listens to my protests. [NPC] Rabbit Sis: Well, more like he can't avoid them since I do it in the lobby. But it's a start, right?|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Sis: I told Hoppity we should have a line of eco-friendly chocolates. [NPC] Rabbit Sis: He asked if green food coloring counted. [NPC] Rabbit Sis: We're...working on it.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Sis: They call me a troublemaker, a rebel. I say, I'm the only one talking sense! [NPC] Rabbit Sis: Wake up and smell the exploitation, family!|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Sis: They say I'm disrupting the peace, but I'm just trying to sprinkle a little truth on our chocolate-covered lies. [NPC] Rabbit Sis: The factory needs a new recipe...for justice.|
|[[Rabbit Daddy]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Sis: A protest a day keeps the unethical practices away. [NPC] Rabbit Sis: Dad might not see it now, but I'm doing this for the future of all chocolate bunnies.|
|[[Rabbit Uncle]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Sis: Uncle treats every little decision around here like it's the state championship game. [NPC] Rabbit Sis: 'What would coach have done?' He mutters to himself. [NPC] Rabbit Sis: Close the yearbook, pal.|
|[[Rabbit Uncle]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Sis: Uncle has this ritual of watching old high school games every weekend. [NPC] Rabbit Sis: It's like sacred sports hour, but it's just him yelling at the players on screen to 'watch the defense!'|
|[[Rabbit Uncle]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Sis: I asked Uncle for some life advice, but all I got were football X's and O's. [NPC] Rabbit Sis: Apparently 'anticipating your opponent's moves' applies to everything from dating to business deals.|
|[[Rabbit Dog]] employed|[NPC] Rabbit Sis: Rabbit Dog is my best friend. [NPC] Rabbit Sis: He is as loyal as they come, and doesn't look at me funny when I am speaking like everyone else.|
|Chocolate Factory IV ([[Chocolate Factory]]) or higher|[NPC] Rabbit Sis: I think Coach is all brawn and no brains.|
## Trivia
- Rabbit Sis' name's color in chat will match her Rarity in [[Chocolate Factory]].

# Rabbit Uncle
Categories: Non-player characters, Confirmations needed
[Rabbit Uncle]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Acts as an employee ([[Chocolate Factory]]) for the [[Chocolate Factory]].
Location: [[Rabbit House]]
Coordinates: 77.5 65 9.5
Rabbit Uncle is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Rabbit House]] that generates chocolate in the [[Chocolate Factory]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Unemployed|[NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Grandma always said I could be somebody if I put my mind to it. [NPC] Rabbit Uncle: She was wrong.|
|Unemployed|[NPC] Rabbit Uncle: I should be in the Hall of Fame by now. [NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Instead, I am here. Please, hire me.|
|Unemployed|[NPC] Rabbit Uncle: <player>...you have to hire me. Please.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Back in high school, I was just one play away from that state championship. [NPC] Rabbit Uncle: If only I had zigged instead of zagged...|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Every time I see a football, I can't help but wonder 'What if?' [NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Was so close to grabbing that championship ring.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Sometimes, late at night, I replay that final drive in my head. [NPC] Rabbit Uncle: I could have been a legend, you know? [NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Man.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Uncle: I used to chuck that ball like nobody's business. Those were the days, eh? [NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Still got some of those moves, you know.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Uncle: I've got the entire high school trophy case memorized. [NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Sometimes, I give tours. You know, just to keep the legacy going.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Hoppity's like the star quarterback of this chocolate game. Always looking for that next big play. [NPC] Rabbit Uncle: He's got that championship mindset, just like I had.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Sure, Rabbit Bro's up at dawn doing push-ups and planning his day. [NPC] Rabbit Uncle: I used to be like him, then I tore my ACL. [NPC] Rabbit Uncle: My knee has never been the same since, man.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Cuz might not have the typical athlete's discipline, but he's clutch. [NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Just like my old teammate who didn't show up to every practice, but could score tuddies when it mattered most.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Cuz showing up to work is like a trick play. [NPC] Rabbit Uncle: You never see it coming, and when it happens, you can't help but wonder if it was a fluke.|
|Employed|[NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Sis has that fire, like the team captains back in my day. [NPC] Rabbit Uncle: She doesn't just play - she changes the game. [NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Always rallying the troops for her cause, much like a good quarterback does in the fourth quarter.|
|Rabbit Uncle is at least level 20 [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]|[NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Even the Dog's got team spirit. [NPC] Rabbit Uncle: He reminds me of our mascot back in the day. Always on the sidelines, ready to cheer us on. [NPC] Rabbit Uncle: Every team needs a loyal supporter like that.|
|[[Chocolate Factory]] is at least prestige IV [Confirm]|[NPC] Rabbit Uncle: I like Coach's style. My family could learn a thing or two from him.|
## Trivia
- Rabbit Uncle's name color in chat will match their Rarity in the [[Chocolate Factory]].
- Rabbit Uncle appears in the [[Mountain]] when [[Rabbit Granny]] is level 20 or above.
