# Ramero
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Ramero]
Type: [[NPC]]
[Locations]
Description: Appears at the end of [[Detective Amog]]'s first reverse-murder quest.
Location: [[Murder House]]
Coordinates: -48.5 68 -38.312
Description: Appears during [[Detective Amog]]'s second reverse-murder quest.
Location: [[Taylor's]]
Coordinates: -31.5 64.5 -42.5
Description: Appears at the end of [[Detective Amog]]'s second reverse-murder quest before being saved.
Location: [[Murder House]]
Coordinates: -48.5 68 -38.312
Description: Appears here at the end of [[Detective Amog]]'s second reverse-murder quest after being saved.
Location: [[Murder House]]
Coordinates: -50.5 68 -37.5
Ramero is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Murder House]] towards the end of [[Detective Amog]]'s first reverse-murder quest. He also appears in [[Taylor's]] and the [[Murder House]] during the second reverse-murder quest.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|[[Murder House]] Speaking before the barricaded wall has been destroyed|You are a bad person for entering the barricaded house without consent! And please try to follow the story, a lot of effort went into it.|
|[[Murder House]] During [[Detective Amog]]'s first reverse-murder quest|[NPC] Ramero: Oh misery, is this my end? [NPC] Ramero: Is mine own death truly inevitable? Select an option: [I'm afraid so, but we found you right on time.]|
|[[Murder House]] Selecting [I'm afraid so, but we found you right on time.]; (Activates Once)|[NPC] Ramero: I beseech thee, I am an honest man. Please do not take my life. [NPC] Ramero: For I am to wed fair Joliet, and begin a family. Amog knows what to do...|
|[[Murder House]] Hitting Ramero with [[Tactician's Murder Weapon]]|[NPC] Ramero: Ow! That doth smart!|
|[[Murder House]] (same as above)|[NPC] Ramero: Fie! That pain doth gnaw at my very soul!|
|[[Murder House]] (same as above)|[NPC] Ramero: Zounds! That doth truly hurt!|
|[[Murder House]] (same as above)|[NPC] Ramero: By heaven! That wound doth ache!|
|[[Murder House]] (same as above)|[NPC] Ramero: Prithee, the pain is almost unbearable!|
|[[Murder House]] Upon being killed|[NPC] Ramero: I beg of you... convey to Joliet that my love for her shall never die... [NPC] Ramero: Not even in death. I should talk to the detective now...|
|[[Taylor's]] During [[Detective Amog]]'s second reverse-murder quest|[NPC] Ramero: Upon my home's floor, grim bloodstains I found. [NPC] Ramero: An awful sign that death's shadow grows near. [NPC] Ramero: Overtaken by dread, I thought to flee, yet how to save myself I know not. [NPC] Ramero: I am compelled to live, for should I fall, Joliet would wander this world alone until death's embrace reunites us. Select an option: [I don't think this place is safe for you then.]|
|[[Taylor's]] Selecting [I don't think this place is safe for you then.]|[NPC] Ramero: I must then take flight. [NPC] Ramero: I resolve to barricade my abode with materials at hand. [NPC] Ramero: Yet, without aid, I fear my preparations will fall short of time's cruel demand. Select an option: [I will help you, let's get to your house.]|
|[[Taylor's]] Selecting [I will help you, let's get to your house.]|[NPC] Ramero: Thanks unto thee, [player]! [NPC] Ramero: May our united efforts grant me victory against the dire fate that shadows my path.|
|[[Taylor's]] Before going back to the [[Murder House]]|[NPC] Ramero: Let us assemble at mine own house.|
|[[Murder House]] Before barricading the wall|[NPC] Ramero: I have collected yon materials upon the floor. [NPC] Ramero: Let us employ these planks to fortify the entrance. [NPC] Ramero: Make haste, for the villain may arrive forthwith.|
|[[Murder House]] After barricading the wall|[NPC] Ramero: See how this barricade holds firm; it is a fortress in its own right. [NPC] Ramero: Truly, they instill a newfound sense of safety. [NPC] Ramero: My gratitude for thy aid is boundless, for thy labors were crucial, and alone I stood no chance.|
|[[Murder House]] After being saved from death|[NPC] Ramero: Thou hast delivered me from death's cold grasp, when all seemed lost to cruel fate's design. [NPC] Ramero: My heart, once frail, now beats anew, by thy noble hand restored. [NPC] Ramero: This ring, once forged in love's sweet name, now lies as broken as the vow it bore. [NPC] Ramero: Yet methinks it holds a greater meaning still - one of survival, of hope reborn. [NPC] Ramero: I can no longer bear its weight, for it is both wound and memory. But thou, dear savior, shouldst take it. [NPC] Ramero: Not as token of despair, but as a sign that even the broken may yet be saved, as I have been by thee.|

# Ravenous Rhino
Aliases: Rhino
Categories: Non-player characters
[Ravenous Rhino]
Type: [[NPC]]
Location: [[The Garden]]
The Ravenous Rhino is an [[NPC]] that only appears in [[The Garden]]. They require 50 unique [[The Garden|Visitors]] to unlock and only appear while [[Finnegan]]'s Blooming Business Perk is active. Completing their offer requires 32 [[Enchanted Cake]] or 2,048 (32 stacks) [[Enchanted Bread]] chosen at random. Accepting the offer awards the player with an [[Enchanted Book]] ([[Dedication|Dedication IV]]).
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Ravenous Rhino: I'm very hungry. Give me the Enchanted Cake!|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Ravenous Rhino: You know, that wasn't too bad.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Ravenous Rhino: No! I don't want that!|
## Trivia
- The Ravenous Rhino's dialogue appears to be a reference to the Hungry Pumpkin.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 3, 2024|Added Ravenous Rhino.|
|Jun 13, 2024|Ravenous Rhino requires 50 unique visitors to unlock.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 2, 2024|0.20.3|Added Ravenous Rhino.|

# Redstone Miner
Categories: Non-player characters
[Redstone Miner]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: The Redstone Maniac ([[Quests]])
Description: Rewards the player with [[Accessory Bag]] slot upgrades after bringing increasing amounts of Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]]).
Location: [[Pigmen's Den]]
Coordinates: 25 104 16.5
The Redstone Miner is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Pigmen's Den]]. They grant up to four [[Accessory Bag]] slots in exchange for increasing amounts of Redstone.
## Quest
Interacting with the Redstone Miner for the first time starts the [[Quests|The Redstone Maniac]] quest. Provided the player has reached 250 [[Redstone Dust]] collection, the NPC asks them for 128 (2 stacks) [[Redstone Dust]]. The player will receive an additional [[Accessory Bag]] slot, upon giving the items to the Redstone Miner. This can be repeated three more times, with 256, 512 and 1024 redstone respectively for a total of 1,920 (30 stacks) [[Redstone Dust]] or 30 stacks.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction|[NPC] Redstone Miner: Redstone is such an amazing resource that can be used for so many different purposes. [NPC] Redstone Miner: For example, by leveling up your Redstone Collection, you can unlock more slots in your Accessory Bag! [NPC] Redstone Miner: Reach Redstone Collection II to unlock the Small Accessory Bag upgrade, then talk to me again!|
|Player already has Redstone II (2) ([[Redstone Dust]]) collection|[NPC] Redstone Miner: Oh wait, you already have Redstone Collection II! [NPC] Redstone Miner: In that case, would you like to help me out with something? In return, I can give you some more slots for your Accessory Bag. Select an option: [YES] [NO]|
|Selecting [NO] Initial|[NPC] Redstone Miner: Understandable, have a nice day. Please speak to me again if you ever change your mind.|
|Selecting [NO] Talking to [[Redstone Miner]] Redstone Miner Again|[NPC] Redstone Miner: Filling your Accessory Bag grants you Accessory Power. If you speak to my friend Maxwell in the Village, he can channel that power into giving you bonus stats! [NPC] Redstone Miner: For now, would you like to help me out with something? In return, I can give you some more slots for your Accessory Bag. Select an option: [YES] [NO]|
|Selecting [YES]|[NPC] Redstone Miner: Thank you, it's much appreciated! [NPC] Redstone Miner: If you can bring me 128 Redstone, I'll reward you with an extra slot for your Accessory Bag. [NPC] Redstone Miner: You can find Redstone all around the Pigmen's Den, but the Pigmen don't really appreciate you stealing it. [NPC] Redstone Miner: They roughed me up real good earlier, so I'm a bit worried about mining any myself. But, you look strong enough to go for it!|
|Before giving the materials|[NPC] Redstone Miner: If you can bring me 128 Redstone, I'll reward you with an extra slot for your Accessory Bag. Select an option: [Give materials] [No thanks!]|
|Selecting [No thanks!]|[NPC] Redstone Miner: Alright, come back later when you want to give the items!|
|Selecting [Give materials] Not enough materials|[NPC] Redstone Miner: Come back with the right amount of items!|
|Selecting [Give materials] Enough materials|[NPC] Redstone Miner: Amazing! Thanks for the Redstone! [NPC] Redstone Miner: Just as I promised, here's an extra Accessory Bag slot! [NPC] Redstone Miner: You can view your Accessory Bag within the SkyBlock Menu. [NPC] Redstone Miner: If you can bring me 256 Redstone, I'll reward you with another slot for your Accessory Bag. You got an extra Accessory Bag slot from helping the Redstone Miner!|
|Talking to [[Redstone Miner]] Redstone Miner|[NPC] Redstone Miner: If you can bring me 256 Redstone, I'll reward you with another slot for your Accessory Bag. Select an option: [Give materials] [No thanks!]|
|Selecting [No thanks!]|[NPC] Redstone Miner: Alright, come back later when you want to give the items!|
|Selecting [Give materials] Not enough materials|[NPC] Redstone Miner: Come back with the right amount of items!|
|Selecting [Give materials] Enough materials|[NPC] Redstone Miner: Thank you very much! [NPC] Redstone Miner: Now, as I promised, take another slot! [NPC] Redstone Miner: We're not done yet though; This time I'll need 512 Redstone - that's 8 stacks! It's quite a lot, but I know you can do it. You got an extra Accessory Bag slot from helping the Redstone Miner!|
|Talking to [[Redstone Miner]] Redstone Miner|[NPC] Redstone Miner: This time I'll need 512 Redstone - that's 8 stacks! It's quite a lot, but I know you can do it. Select an option: [Give materials] [No thanks!]|
|Selecting [No thanks!]|[NPC] Redstone Miner: Alright, come back later when you want to give the items!|
|Selecting [Give materials] Not enough materials|[NPC] Redstone Miner: Come back with the right amount of items!|
|Selecting [Give materials] Enough materials|[NPC] Redstone Miner: That's the most Redstone I've ever seen in one place! And it's all for me... [NPC] Redstone Miner: Please, take another Accessory Bag slot! You deserve it! [NPC] Redstone Miner: If you have any energy left, I have just one more task for you. Finally, I will need 1,024 Redstone. That's 16 stacks, if you didn't know. [NPC] Redstone Miner: I know it's a lot, so feel free to come back later if you're busy. You got an extra Accessory Bag slot from helping the Redstone Miner!|
|Talking to [[Redstone Miner]] Redstone Miner|[NPC] Redstone Miner: This time, I will need 1,024 Redstone. That's 16 stacks, if you didn't know! Select an option: [Give materials] [No thanks!]|
|Selecting [No thanks!]|[NPC] Redstone Miner: Alright, come back later when you want to give the items!|

[NPC] Redstone Miner: That's all I can do for you. [NPC] Redstone Miner: If you still want more Accessory Bag slots, talk to Jacobus in the Village, or simply level up your Redstone Collection!|
## Trivia
- The Redstone Miner is the brother of the [[Lapis Miner]].
## Bugs
- If the player reaches [[Redstone Dust|Redstone Dust II (2)]] before first talking to the Redstone Miner, they must swap lobbies after the first interaction for it to recognize that they already have the collection.

# Rescue Recruiter
Aliases: Rescue Recruiter (Mage)
Categories: Non-player characters
[Rescue Recruiter]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Informs the player to talk to the [[Undercover Agent]] by [[The Bastion]] to complete the Rescue Mission.
Location: Community Center ([[Community Center (Scarleton)]])
Coordinates: -120.5 92 -751.5
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Informs the player to talk to the [[Undercover Agent]] by the [[Cathedral]] to complete the Rescue Mission.
Location: [[Dragontail]]
Coordinates: -581.5 100 -690.5
The Rescue Recruiter is an NPC that appears outside either in [[Community Center (Scarleton)|Community Center]] or [[Chief's Hut]], depending on the player's [[Factions|Faction]], after accepting a Rescue Mission. The player must then talk to the [[Undercover Agent]] to begin the quest.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Starting Rescue Barbarian|[NPC] Rescue Recruiter: You want to help us rescue a hostage from those filthy brutes over there? [NPC] Rescue Recruiter: Perfect! Go see our Undercover Agent at the Cathedral area in Mage territory to get started.|
|Starting Rescue Mage|[NPC] Rescue Recruiter: You want to help us rescue a hostage from those filthy brutes over there? [NPC] Rescue Recruiter: Perfect! Go see our Undercover Agent at the The Bastion area in Barbarian territory to get started.|
|Already Started|[NPC] Rescue Recruiter: Thank you for your help! Go see the Undercover Agent for details.|
|If you're on the opposite Faction ([[Factions]])|[NPC] Rescue Recruiter: You look suspicious. I have my eyes on you...|
|If you've on the right Faction ([[Factions]]) but have started a rescue mission on the opposite Faction ([[Factions]])|[NPC] Rescue Recruiter: You have a rescue with the enemy? I KNEW they were spying on us!|

# Researcher Betty
Aliases: Betty
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Researcher Betty]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Shows the [[Chapters]] for the [[Lotus Atoll]].
Location: [[Lotus Atoll]]
Coordinates: -13.5 71 3.5
Researcher Betty is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Lotus Atoll]] that allows the player to access the [[Chapters]] for the island.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Researcher Betty: Hi! I assume you are here to help with our Trophy Frog research. [NPC] Researcher Betty: I've got your list of tasks to complete while on the Lotus Atoll. [NPC] Researcher Betty: Complete them all and you'll be rewarded. Here, take a look!|
## Chapters
Begin your journey as Batrachologist.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Talk to [[Researcher Ribery]]|[[Researcher Ribery]] is the head of the whole operation. View his Research Journal to see all species of Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]]) you can catch!|+250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a BRONZE Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|[[Trophy Frogs]] can be exchanged with [[Researcher Ribery]] in return for [[Lotus]].|+250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch an [[Atoll Croaker]]|This is a common Lotus Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the Lotus Atoll.|+250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
[Chapter I Competition Rewards]
[Unlock Chapter II; 40 Lotus; +5 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]
Get to know the Lotus Atoll a little better.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Talk to [[Researcher Lily]]|[[Researcher Lily]] is investigating a strange phenomenon on the island, and knows how to catch a rare species of Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]]) known as the Reality Hopper ([[Trophy Frogs]]).|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Talk to [[Researcher Tina]]|As per [[Researcher Tina]]'s findings, the [[Trophy Chance]] stat increases your chance of catching Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]]).|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a SILVER Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|[[Trophy Frogs]] can be exchanged with [[Researcher Ribery]] in return for [[Lotus]].|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a Bullfrog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|Bullfrogs ([[Trophy Frogs]]) will only bite if you're wearing a [[Red Sweater]].|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Hunt a [[Lotusfish]]|[[Lotusfish]] can be hunted using a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]).; You can learn more about Attribute Shards ([[Attributes]]) by talking to [[David Hunterborough]] over in the [[Moonglade Marsh]].|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Hunt a [[Lotum]]|Lotums ([[Lotum]]) like to jump between lotus. They'll drop a [[Lotum Shard]] if you manage to chase them down.; You can learn more about Attribute Shards ([[Attributes]]) by talking to [[David Hunterborough]] over in the [[Moonglade Marsh]].|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a [[Lotus Guardian]]|This is an UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) Lotus Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the [[Lotus Atoll]].|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a [[GorF]]|This is a RARE ([[Rarity]]) Lotus Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the [[Lotus Atoll]].|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Craft [[Froggles]]|[[Froggles]] can be crafted after reaching [[Lotus]] Collection I. They allow you to see Wormholes ([[Wormhole]])!|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Reach [[Lotus]] Collection II|Progress through your [[Lotus]] Collection by exchanging [[Trophy Frogs]] with [[Researcher Ribery]]!|+500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
[Chapter II Competition Rewards]
[Unlock Chapter III; 1 Travel Scroll to the Lotus Atoll; 5 Silver Lotus; +10 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]
Keep progressing through your Lotus Collection and catching new types of Trophy Frogs.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|

|Catch a Reality Hopper ([[Trophy Frogs]])|If you need help finding one, [[Researcher Lily]] is an expert on the matter!|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Explode a Lily Pad|You can push Lily Pads together to combine them while on the [[Lotus Atoll]]. When they get large enough, they'll EXPLODE, giving you an Exploding Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]]) and some other Rewards!|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a [[Drowned Captain]]|This is an EPIC ([[Rarity]]) Lotus Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the [[Lotus Atoll]].|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Hunt a [[Tewtil]]|Tewtils ([[Tewtil]]) can be fed until the explode to obtain a [[Tewtil Shard]].; You can learn more about Attribute Shards ([[Attributes]]) by talking to [[David Hunterborough]] over in the [[Moonglade Marsh]].|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Reach [[Lotus]] Collection IV|Progress through your [[Lotus]] Collection by exchanging [[Trophy Frogs]] with [[Researcher Ribery]]!|+1,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|

[Unlock Chapter IV; 10 Silver Lotus; +15 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]

|-|-|-|

|Catch a Sea Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|Sea Frogs ([[Trophy Frogs]]) can only be found underwater.|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a Tree Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|Tree Frogs ([[Trophy Frogs]]) are caught while standing on leaves.|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a Cave Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|Cave Frogs ([[Trophy Frogs]]) can only be caught in the [[Lotus Eater's Cave]].|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a Highlands Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|Highlands Frogs ([[Trophy Frogs]]) can only be caught in the [[Lotus Highlands]].|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Hunt a [[Flipflopper]]|Flipfloppers ([[Flipflopper]]) sometimes spawn from Exploding Lily Pad ([[Lotus Atoll]]), and can be hunted using a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]).; You can obtain your first Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]) by talking to [[David Hunterborough]] in the [[Moonglade Marsh]].|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch all 12 unique BRONZE [[Trophy Frogs]]|Remember to claim your rewards from [[Researcher Ribery]]!|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a [[Puddle Jumper]]|This is a LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) Lotus Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the [[Lotus Atoll]]. They require a bit more effort than usual to catch.|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Donate a [[Frogcoin]]|Legends say that those generous enough to make a donation into the Waters of the [[Lotus Eater's Cave]] will have their fortunes blessed.; You can catch Blessed Frogs ([[Trophy Frogs]]) while you have an active Blessing.|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Reach [[Lotus]] Collection VI|Progress through your [[Lotus]] Collection by exchanging [[Trophy Frogs]] with [[Researcher Ribery]]!|+2,500 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|

[Unlock Chapter V; 20 Silver Lotus; +20 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]
Complete your collection of SILVER Trophy Frogs.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Craft Gold Froggles ([[Golden Froggles]])|[[Golden Froggles]] are a direct upgrade to [[Silver Froggles]] and grant better stats. Crafting them requires [[Lotus]] Collection VII.|+5,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a GOLD Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|[[Trophy Frogs]] can be exchanged with [[Researcher Ribery]] in return for [[Lotus]].|+5,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch all 12 unique SILVER [[Trophy Frogs]]|Remember to claim your rewards from [[Researcher Ribery]]!|+5,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Hunt a [[Seashine]]|Seashines ([[Seashine]]) rarely spawn from Exploding Lily Pad ([[Lotus Atoll]]), and can be hunted using a [[Medium Fishing Net]].; You can obtain your first Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]) by talking to [[David Hunterborough]] in the [[Moonglade Marsh]].|+5,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Reach [[Lotus]] Collection VII|Progress through your [[Lotus]] Collection by exchanging [[Trophy Frogs]] with [[Researcher Ribery]]!|+5,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
[Chapter V Competition Rewards]
[Unlock Chapter VI; 40 Silver Lotus; +25 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels)]
Complete your collection of GOLD Trophy Frogs.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Apply a [[Puddle Jumper Hook]] to Your Rod|A [[Puddle Jumper Hook]] can be crafted after reaching [[Lotus]] Collection VIII. Applying it to your rod increases the chance of catching Puddle Jumpers ([[Puddle Jumper]]) while fishing.|+10,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Apply a [[Trophy Line]] to Your Rod|A [[Trophy Line]] will come in handy while on the [[Lotus Atoll]] and any other islands with Trophies ([[Trophy (disambiguation)]]). Talk to [[Roddy]] in the [[Backwater Bayou]] to apply it to your rod!|+10,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a DIAMOND Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|[[Trophy Frogs]] can be exchanged with [[Researcher Ribery]] in return for [[Lotus]].|+10,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch a [[Frog Prince]]|This is a MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) Lotus Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the [[Lotus Atoll]].|+10,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch all 12 unique GOLD [[Trophy Frogs]]|Remember to claim your rewards from [[Researcher Ribery]]!|+10,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Reach [[Lotus]] Collection IX|Progress through your [[Lotus]] Collection by exchanging [[Trophy Frogs]] with [[Researcher Ribery]]!|+10,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|

Finish up with the Lotus Atoll and claim the Pearl of the Lotus!:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|

|Craft [[Diamond Froggles]]|This high-tech piece increases your chance of catching GOLD and DIAMOND tier [[Trophy Frogs]] while worn. Crafting them requires [[Lotus]] Collection XI.|+25,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Obtain a Lily Pad Minion XI Upgrade Stone ([[Lily Pad Minion XII Upgrade Stone]])|Someone on the [[Lotus Atoll]] will sell this to you, but you'll have to find them first. Perhaps it is related to the [[Lotus Eater's Cave]]?|+25,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch all 12 unique DIAMOND [[Trophy Frogs]]|Remember to claim your rewards from [[Researcher Ribery]]!|+25,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Reach [[Lotus]] Collection XI|Progress through your [[Lotus]] Collection by exchanging [[Trophy Frogs]] with [[Researcher Ribery]]!|+25,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|

|First Interaction|[NPC] Researcher Gilbert: Hello! [NPC] Researcher Gilbert: I'm a researcher who focuses upon the study of Frogs! [NPC] Researcher Gilbert: Well, I was a researcher, I suppose. |Selecting [It is indeed!]|[NPC] Researcher Gilbert: Hmm, might I be so bold as to ask if I could join you on your adventures? [NPC] Researcher Gilbert: I don't have a lot going on now, with retirement and all. Select an option: [Sure!]|

[NPC] Researcher Gilbert: As much of a dumping ground as this place is, it really feels like MY dumping ground, you know..? [GIVE ITEM]|

|Selecting [GIVE ITEM] without the Belt|[NPC] Researcher Gilbert: It appears you don't have my souvenir yet! It would be such a shame to leave without it... [NPC] Researcher Gilbert: Please could you get me a Busted Belt Buckle from Junker Joel?|
|Selecting [GIVE ITEM] with the Belt|[NPC] Researcher Gilbert: Amazing, thank you so much! [NPC] Researcher Gilbert: I'll go introduce myself to the rest of the crew! [NPC] Researcher Gilbert: Anchors away! ▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬ NEW ISLAND UNLOCKED! |Before heading to the ship|[NPC] Researcher Gilbert: I'll come and join you on the Ship shortly!|
|On the [[Ship]]|[NPC] Researcher Gilbert: I love the feel of the wind in my hair!|
|On the [[Ship]]|[NPC] Researcher Gilbert: I hope you brought your fishing rod!|

# Researcher Lily
Categories: Non-player characters
[Researcher Lily]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Introduces the player to Wormholes ([[Wormhole]]), [[Froggles]], and the Reality Hopper ([[Trophy Frogs]]).
Location: [[Lotus Atoll]]
Coordinates: 23.5 66.093 14.5
Researcher Lily is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Lotus Atoll]] who talks about [[Wormhole|Wormholes]], [[Froggles]], and the [[Trophy Frogs|Reality Hopper]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Researcher Lily: One moment please - I've run into quite a severe conundrum. Select an option: [What's wrong?]|
|Selecting [What's wrong?]|[NPC] Researcher Lily: Okay, I'll tell you. But promise me you won't freak out. Select an option: [I promise.] [Depends what we're dealing with here...]|
|Selecting [I promise.]|[NPC] Researcher Lily: There appear to be several Wormholes on this island. [NPC] Researcher Lily: It started with just one, we began conducting research on it, and then more started appearing... Select an option: [I don't see any holes, or worms.]|
|Selecting [Depends what we're dealing with here...]|[NPC] Researcher Lily: C'mon dude, don't you deal with stuff like this all the time? [NPC] Researcher Lily: There appear to be several Wormholes on this island. [NPC] Researcher Lily: It started with just one, we began conducting research on it, and then more started appearing... Select an option: [I don't see any holes, or worms.]|
|Selecting [I don't see any holes, or worms.]|[NPC] Researcher Lily: Ah, right! I probably sound crazy right now! [NPC] Researcher Lily: You can only see Wormholes while wearing a pair of Froggles! [NPC] Researcher Lily: You can craft your own pair after unlocking the Lotus Collection! [NPC] Researcher Lily: After that, you can help us out! Select an option: [How am I supposed to help?]|
|Selecting [How am I supposed to help?]|[NPC] Researcher Lily: Look, bro. The term Wormhole doesn't sound very reassuring, right? [NPC] Researcher Lily: Destabilization, fragmentation, displacement - all very possible results of these anomalies. [NPC] Researcher Lily: Bottom line is, we need to catch the Frogs that are responsible for creating these holes! Select an option: [Frogs? But you called them Wormholes...]|
|Selecting [Frogs? But you called them Wormholes...]|[NPC] Researcher Lily: Yeah, I know it sounds weird. But trust me, these aren't ordinary frogs. [NPC] Researcher Lily: These are Trophy Frogs! If you help us catch them, we can study them and figure out how to close the Wormholes! [NPC] Researcher Lily: Equip your Froggles, find the Wormholes, and cast your rod! [NPC] Researcher Lily: With some luck, you'll be able to catch the elusive Reality Hopper! [NPC] Researcher Lily: After a bit of time, the hole will stabilize and disappear. [NPC] Researcher Lily: However, another one will open up somewhere else on the island... Select an option: [That's a lot of yap.]|
|Selecting [That's a lot of yap.]|[NPC] Researcher Lily: I know, it's an arduous process. [NPC] Researcher Lily: I am happy to answer any questions you might have! Best of luck!|
|Future Interactions|[NPC] Researcher Lily: Hey, what's up? Select an option: ➜ [Where do I get Froggles?] ➜ [How do I catch Reality Hoppers?] ➜ [What are Wormholes?]|
|Selecting [Where do I get Froggles?]|[NPC] Researcher Lily: You can craft your own pair of Froggles after reaching Lotus Collection I.|
|Selecting [How do I catch Reality Hoppers?]|[NPC] Researcher Lily: Reality Hoppers are a type of Trophy Frog that somehow create Wormholes! [NPC] Researcher Lily: Equip your Froggles and look around for otherworldly particles in the water. [NPC] Researcher Lily: When you find one, cast your rod into and wait for a bite! [NPC] Researcher Lily: You'll either pull a Reality Hopper out of the hole, or some loot that it's gathered. [NPC] Researcher Lily: Holes will close once you fish in them a few times, but a new one will open up elsewhere on the island.|
|Selecting [What are Wormholes?]|[NPC] Researcher Lily: Wormholes have begun to open up all around the Lotus Atoll! [NPC] Researcher Lily: They seem to be directly related to the Reality Hopper frog! [NPC] Researcher Lily: At least, that's our current hypothesis!|

# Rezzus
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Rezzus]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Assigns players to Raffle ([[Raffle of the Century]]) teams and gives out information.
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: -12 76 3
Rezzus was an [[NPC]] located in the [[Village]] during the [[Raffle of the Century]] event.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction during [[Raffle of the Century]]|[NPC] Rezzus: Hey there! Happy SkyBlock Year [year]! [NPC] Rezzus: To celebrate this EPIC milestone, we are organizing a set of CRAZY raffles! [NPC] Rezzus: But first, you need a TEAM!|
|Closing interface before joining team|[NPC] Rezzus: Oh... did you mean to close that? [NPC] Rezzus: It's fine, click me again!|
|Joining a team|[NPC] Rezzus: Sweet, the [COLOR] team. [NPC] Rezzus: I hear it might be the best one! [NPC] Rezzus: Anyway... there are 3 kinds of raffles going on. [NPC] Rezzus: Speed Raffles are drawn every 2 hours. [NPC] Rezzus: Daily Raffles, they happen every 24 hours. [NPC] Rezzus: The Big One, which is going to be drawn at the end of Year [year]! [NPC] Rezzus: To participate, collect Raffle Tickets and slices of cake by simply playing SkyBlock during the event. [NPC] Rezzus: All tickets you gain are automatically entered into the next raffle of each type. [NPC] Rezzus: When you receive a slice of cake, it will be a certain color, which corresponds to a certain team. [NPC] Rezzus: There are slices of Red Velvet, Blueberry, Lemon Cheesecake, Matcha, and Strawberry Shortcake! [NPC] Rezzus: If you take your slice of cake and feed it to a member of that team, you'll both get some rewards, in addition to bonus Raffle Tickets! [NPC] Rezzus: Here, I'll give you 3 Raffle Tickets to get you started. [NPC] Rezzus: You can get more tickets by eating cake and completing Raffle Tasks. Check out the Raffle Box for more info on those! REZZUS GIFT! You gained +3 Raffle Tickets! [NPC] Rezzus: Tada! [NPC] Rezzus: You can click on the Raffle Box next to me or in your SkyBlock Menu to view your event progress. [NPC] Rezzus: Oh, one last thing! The team that enters the most tickets into The Big One will receive a special memento at the end of the event! [NPC] Rezzus: Have fun! You can come speak to me again if you have any questions!|
|Subsequent interactions|[NPC] Rezzus: Hello again! Happy Century Celebration! [NPC] Rezzus: What would you like to know about the event? Select an option: ➜ [How do I enter the raffles?] ➜ [Which team am I on?]|
|Selecting [How do I enter the raffles?]|[NPC] Rezzus: To enter a raffle, you need to collect Raffle Tickets. [NPC] Rezzus: You will gain tickets just for being online during the event. [NPC] Rezzus: However, you will gain a bit more if you're actively playing, especially from doing Raffle Tasks. [NPC] Rezzus: Tickets are not physical items, so don't worry about losing them! [NPC] Rezzus: Each time you find a ticket, it will be entered into EVERY upcoming raffle.|
|Selecting [Which team am I on?]|[NPC] Rezzus: For this event, you are on the [COLOR] team! [NPC] Rezzus: You can tell what team someone is by looking at the color of the cake emblem next to their name in chat. [NPC] Rezzus: Or, by looking at the color of the cake emblem next to their name in the TAB list. [NPC] Rezzus: If they don't have a cake emblem, then they haven't gotten involved in the event yet... [NPC] Rezzus: Which means more cake for the rest of us!|
|Missing the Raffle of the Century|[NPC] Rezzus: Hi! [NPC] Rezzus: There was a big event in celebration of the SkyBlock Year [year]. [NPC] Rezzus: It looks like you missed it, but maybe you'll catch the next event!|
|Before SkyBlock Year 300|[NPC] Rezzus: Hey there! Happy (upcoming) SkyBlock Year 300! [NPC] Rezzus: We're organizing a huge raffle! [NPC] Rezzus: Come talk to me once the event starts!|
|After the Century Celebrations ([[Raffle of the Century]]) of SkyBlock Year 300, 400 and 500|[NPC] Rezzus: Hi! [NPC] Rezzus: The Century Celebration event is over, but you can still interact with the Raffle Box to claim rewards you might have missed. [NPC] Rezzus: See you next time!|
|Before SkyBlock Year 500|[NPC] Rezzus: Hey there! Happy (upcoming) SkyBlock Year 500! [NPC] Rezzus: As part of the Century Celebration, we're organizing a huge raffle! [NPC] Rezzus: Come talk to me once the event starts!|
|During SkyBlock Year 500 [[Raffle of the Century]], on a Ⓑ Bingo ([[Bingo]]) Profile|[NPC] Rezzus: Hey there! [NPC] Rezzus: You cannot claim prizes or even view the raffle box on bingo, but you can earn tickets! [NPC] Rezzus: Unsurprisingly, you've been assigned to the [COLOR] team! You will now be able to earn tickets on this bingo profile.|
## Trivia
- Rezzus is a reference to one of the co-owners of the [[Hypixel]] network, 55[[Ranks|[ዞ]]] Rezzus.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Aug 31, 2023|Added Rezzus.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Sep 2, 2023|Added Rezzus.|

# Rhanora
Aliases: Rhanora Cat

Rhanora is an [[NPC]] in [[Scarleton]]. They own several cats.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Rhanora: Do you like my cats? [NPC] Rhanora: My favorite cat? I love them all. [NPC] Rhanora: I like to study cats, and I think they sometimes study me. [NPC] Rhanora: I think all cats teleport to the same spiritual world when they dream. [NPC] Rhanora: Cats are the only known animals that are gifted with magic, except they are too lazy to use their powers. [NPC] Rhanora: My dear Leo is on his 9th life, I give him all the care in the world.|
|During the Talk to 4 Villagers Quest ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) First Interaction (If they're Mage 1/4)|[NPC] Rhanora: Oh, a new face! Welcome to the City of Magic! [NPC] Rhanora: We used to be Pig Farmers centuries ago, but thanks to Sulphur we are now superior beings capable of mastering the magical elements! [NPC] Rhanora: If you are interested in tourist attractions I suggest you visit our Cathedral, which is also our Academy now.|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 2/4); Less than 1,000 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Rhanora: [amount] Intelligence! That's...ok I guess!|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 2/4); 1,000-2,000 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Rhanora: [amount] Intelligence! Wow that's not bad at all!|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 2/4); More than 2,000 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Rhanora: [amount] Intelligence! What, how? Who are you even?|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 3/4)|[NPC] Rhanora: Have you tried the new ability phone? [NPC] Rhanora: The new one is barely better than the one from last year, people still buy them like sheep... [NPC] Rhanora: Wait, you don't even have an Abiphone? [NPC] Rhanora: Yikes...|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 4/4)|[NPC] Rhanora: Ah, the Piglins yes, quite troublesome they are. [NPC] Rhanora: I wouldn't go there if I were you.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Mages are spoken to|[NPC] Rhanora: You seem suspicious.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Mages are spoken to|[NPC] Rhanora: Sorry, I can't talk to you right now.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Mages are spoken to|[NPC] Rhanora: Please stop bothering me.|
### Cats
Rhanora has 5 cats: Citrine, Leo, Marmalade, Pax, and Plume.

# Rhys
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Dwarven Mines, Deep Caverns
[Rhys]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: There are Dwarves? ([[Quests]])
[Locations]
Description: Unlocks access to the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Heart of the Mountain]], after the player has reached Mining XV (15) ([[Mining]]) and brought 15 enchanted ore items.
Location: [[Obsidian Sanctuary]]
Coordinates: 31.5 12 14.5
Description: Introduces the player to the [[Dwarven Mines]].
Location: [[Dwarven Village]]
Coordinates: -38 200 -119
Rhys is an [[NPC]] in the [[Obsidian Sanctuary]], near the [[Lift Operator]]. They give the player access to the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Heart of the Mountain]] after they reach [[Mining|Mining XII (12)]] and in exchange for 15 enchanted versions of any type of [[Ores]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction; Before Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]])|[NPC] Rhys: So you want to visit the Dwarven Mines? [NPC] Rhys: You must prove yourself worthy of Heart of the Mountain... [NPC] Rhys: You are no match for the Dwarves of the Deeper Caverns. [NPC] Rhys: Come back when you are Mining Level XII!|
|Before Giving [[Ores]]|[NPC] Rhys: So you want to visit the Dwarven Mines? [NPC] Rhys: You must prove yourself worthy of Heart of the Mountain... [NPC] Rhys: You must bring me some valuable materials that peak my interest. [NPC] Rhys: I would like a total of 15 items. Any from this list will do: [NPC] Rhys: - Enchanted Redstone Dust [NPC] Rhys: - Enchanted Iron Ingot [NPC] Rhys: - Enchanted Coal [NPC] Rhys: - Enchanted Emerald [NPC] Rhys: - Enchanted Lapis Lazuli [NPC] Rhys: - Enchanted Diamond [NPC] Rhys: - Enchanted Gold Ingot|
|Incorrect Items|[NPC] Rhys: That's not an item I'm looking for! [NPC] Rhys: I would like a total of 15 items. Any from the list will do: [NPC] Rhys: - Enchanted Redstone Dust [NPC] Rhys: - Enchanted Iron Ingot [NPC] Rhys: - Enchanted Coal [NPC] Rhys: - Enchanted Emerald [NPC] Rhys: - Enchanted Lapis Lazuli [NPC] Rhys: - Enchanted Diamond [NPC] Rhys: - Enchanted Gold Ingot|
|Some Correct Items|[NPC] Rhys: Thank you for the items!|
|Giving All 15 Any Enchanted [[Ores]]|[NPC] Rhys: Perhaps you are someone we can get along with after all. [NPC] Rhys: I'll get your Heart of the Mountain. It's time for a visit! [NPC] Rhys: Let's go together now. For future trips, remember the Lift Operator.|
|First Interaction; [[Dwarven Mines]]|[NPC] Rhys: Now that you have your Heart of the Mountain, you have access to all of the deeper areas of the mines! [NPC] Rhys: You can access the Heart of the Mountain from the Mining Skill menu, and unlock loads of powerful upgrades as you continue your journey! [NPC] Rhys: We Dwarves inhabit this area. We love it! It is rich in Mithril and even some Titanium. [NPC] Rhys: You will need a good pickaxe to mine ores here. For Mithril, at least something with a Breaking Power of 4. Bubu just down the street could help you with that! [NPC] Rhys: If you're looking for better hardware, maybe try checking out the Forge. You could also visit the Royal Palace to talk to the King about certain tasks! [NPC] Rhys: Enjoy our little slice of mining heaven!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Rhys: I found an unexplored cave while mining for titanium. But it's too dark to see in there, even for me! Can you spare any glowing pumpkins?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Rhys: Thank you! Discovery awaits.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Rhys: Well then, I will even mine in total darkness if I must!|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|After Giving [[Ores]]|[NPC] Rhys: I had a sunburn once, I have stayed down there ever since.|
|After Giving [[Ores]]|[NPC] Rhys: You can ask the Lift Operator for a ride to the Dwarven Mines!|
## Tips
- The best ore to mine for Rhys's quest to unlock the [[Dwarven Mines]] is [[Ores|Redstone]], since 640 can be bought from the [[Mad Redstone Engineer]] and it has a high base drop rate.
  - If the player wants to avoid being attacked by [[Redstone Pigman|Redstone Pigmen]], [[Ores|Lapis Lazuli]] is a close second.
## Trivia
- Completing Rhys's quest grants the [[Achievements|"Resourceful" Achievement]].
- When the [[Dwarven Mines]] update first launched, Rhys wouldn't take the items to complete his quest. This turned out to be a bug with the SkyBlock Addons mod, one of the most popular mods for SkyBlock. Before this was fixed in SkyBlock Addons, there were rumors that the Dwarven Mines had been disabled by the admins to fix bugs, which was untrue.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 15, 2026|Updated [[Crystal Hollows]] description in the Rhys's menu.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added Rhys.|
|Unknown Date|Fixed being able to complete Rhys's quest with 30 of the same item by switching lobbies.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Updated [[Crystal Hollows]] description in the Rhys's menu.|

# Rick
Categories: Non-player characters, Quests
[Rick]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: The Flint Bros ([[Quests]])
Description: Gives the player a [[Promising Shovel]] after bringing them 2 Iron Ingots ([[Iron Ingot]]).
Location: [[Spider's Den]]
Coordinates: -264.5 71 -323.5
Rick is an [[NPC]] located in [[Spider's Den]], inside the [[Gravel Mines]]. They give out the [[Quests|The Flint Bros]] quest.
## Quest
Rick asks for 2 [[Iron Ingot|Iron Ingots]] in exchange for a [[Promising Shovel]], as part of the [[Quests|The Flint Bros]] recipe.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Rick: Hey, I could use some help! My Promising Shovel just broke, and I don't have the right ingredients to make a new one. [NPC] Rick: If you could get me a couple iron ingots, I could make one for us both!|
|Before bringing 2 Iron Ingots ([[Iron Ingot]])|[NPC] Rick: Come back with the right amount of items!|
|Bringing 2 Iron Ingots ([[Iron Ingot]])|[NPC] Rick: Thanks a lot. Here's your Promising Shovel.|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None|[NPC] Rick: Have you met Pat? He's my brother. We're the Flint Bros!|
|None|[NPC] Rick: Mining gravel is hard work, but the flint is worth it.|
|None|[NPC] Rick: Careful when it rains around here, it gets dangerous!|
## Trivia
- His brother is [[Pat]], who is located at the [[Graveyard]]. They are referred to as the "Flint Brothers" or "Flint Bros".
  - According to [[Pat]]'s dialogue, Rick mines the [[Gravel]], and [[Pat]] then sells it.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Rick.|

Romero is an NPC related to the [[Romero and Juliette]] quest, where the player assists him in getting presents for [[Juliette]].
## Quest
To make Romero appear, you must first talk to [[Juliette]]. Every step of the quest Romero's will change location.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|After giving the player an item to give to Juliette|[NPC] Romero: Bring the item I gave you to Juliette!|
|After giving the player an item to give to Juliette without the item in your inventory:|[NPC] Romero: Bring the item I gave you to Juliette! [NPC] Romero: If you lost the item, we can fix that! Although, you will have to cover my expenses, considering the sentimental value... [CLICK]|
[Quest Step 1 (Savanna Woodland)]
|Before giving item|[NPC] Romero: I'm... trying to find gold... [NPC] Romero: but I can only find clay and granite here! [NPC] Romero: I heard of a gold mine in the Savanna Woodland. [NPC] Romero: I'd like to craft a gift for my beloved Juliette. [NPC] Romero: Could you find some gold for me, please?|
|Upon giving the Yellow Rock ([[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]])|[NPC] Romero: Thank you so much! [NPC] Romero: Here, could you do me a favor and bring this gift back to Juliette?|
[Quest Step 2 (Village)]
|Before giving item|[NPC] Romero: Groceries? [NPC] Romero: Haha! [NPC] Romero: I'm here to get a flower bouquet for Juliette. [NPC] Romero: Could you help out and get some flowers?|
|Wrong type of flower|[NPC] Romero: I was thinking of a bouquet of roses.|
|While holding 1 Poppy ([[Flowers]])|[NPC] Romero: Just one? That's not much of a bouquet!|
|While holding 2–7 Poppies ([[Flowers]])|[NPC] Romero: Going to need a few more than that!|
|While holding 8–11 Poppies ([[Flowers]])|[NPC] Romero: I love roses! Can you get more?|
|While holding 12–14 Poppies ([[Flowers]])|[NPC] Romero: That's a lot of flowers! Maybe a few more...|
|Upon giving 15 Poppies ([[Flowers]])|[NPC] Romero: Wow, They're beautiful! [NPC] Romero: Please bring the bouquet to Juliette!|
[Quest Step 3 (Graveyard)]
|Before giving item|[NPC] Romero: I love emeralds! [NPC] Romero: There's emerald on everything I like! [NPC] Romero: This one can't be dislodged! [NPC] Romero: Could you bring me some emerald?|
|Insufficient material quantity|[NPC] Romero: I'm going to need a full stack of those!|
|While holding [[Enchanted Emerald]]|[NPC] Romero: Very cool, but regular emeralds will do the trick.|
|While holding [[Enchanted Emerald Block]]|[NPC] Romero: Although I adore this pristine piece of emerald, I don't need it yet.|
|While holding [[Block of Emerald]]|[NPC] Romero: I don't need a whole block!|
|Upon giving 64 Emeralds ([[Emerald]])|[NPC] Romero: Am [NPC] Romero: -az- [NPC] Romero: -ing! [NPC] Romero: Now bring this gift to Juliette!|
[Quest Step 4 (Crimson Isle)]
|Before giving item|[NPC] Romero: Hey, you again! [NPC] Romero: I wanted mushrooms to concoct a stew, [NPC] Romero: but these big red ones won't cut it. [NPC] Romero: I need some decent mushrooms.|
|While holding Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|[NPC] Romero: I can't cook a gourmet stew with tiny mushrooms! [NPC] Romero: I need bigger ones!|
|While holding Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|[NPC] Romero: That's not the color of mushroom I'm looking for.|
|While holding [[Red Mushroom Block]]|[NPC] Romero: Sweet! Just what I need! [NPC] Romero: EXCEPT... Could you enchant it? [NPC] Romero: I need the perfect stew for my beloved Juliette.|
|While holding [[Brown Mushroom Block]]|[NPC] Romero: That's a big mushroom that I don't need. [NPC] Romero: It's well known that good stews aren't made from brown mushrooms.|
|While holding [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]]|[NPC] Romero: I need red mushrooms.|
|Upon giving [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]]|[NPC] Romero: Fantastic! [NPC] Romero: There's some stew for Juliette! [NPC] Romero: Mmh! Tastes great!|
[Quest Step 5 (Mountain)]
|Before giving item|[NPC] Romero: So cold! I wish I had coffee right now... [NPC] Romero: I want to reach the moon for Juliette. [NPC] Romero: Literally! [NPC] Romero: I wish I could jump like a Rabbit and grab a chunk of it.|
|While holding a Potion ([[Potions]]) that is not a [[Rabbit Potion]]|[NPC] Romero: A potion might do the trick, but not quite that one.|
|While holding a [[Rabbit Potion]] that is not tier 6|[NPC] Romero: Rabbit potion? Sounds great! [NPC] Romero: Although... could you bring a more potent one? [NPC] Romero: I don't want to take any chances!|
|While holding a [[Rabbit Potion]] that is tier 6 but does not have a coffee base|[NPC] Romero: Awesome potion! [NPC] Romero: Any chance you could have it taste like coffee? [NPC] Romero: Come back with a caffeinated beverage, thanks!|
|Upon giving item [[Rabbit Potion]] tier 6 brewed from cheap coffee|[NPC] Romero: Thanks! [NPC] Romero: See ya later! [NPC] Romero: Bring the chunk to Juliette!|
[Quest Step 6 (Gold Mine)]
|Before giving item|[NPC] Romero: Hi friend! [NPC] Romero: I want to make a secret gift for Juliette. [NPC] Romero: I collected tons of gold... [NPC] Romero: Now I just need enough heat to melt it!|
|While holding [[Flint and Steel]]|[NPC] Romero: That's not even close to hot!|
|While holding a [[Lava Bucket]]|[NPC] Romero: *tastes it* [NPC] Romero: This is the kind of lava I need... [NPC] Romero: Except I need hotter.|
|While holding a [[Magical Lava Bucket]]|[NPC] Romero: This is too magical for my taste.|
|Upon giving [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]]|[NPC] Romero: Perfect! [NPC] Romero: Thank you so much for helping with all my gift ideas. [NPC] Romero: Can you bring this one to Juliette?|
[Quest Step 7 (Wilderness)]
|Before giving item|[NPC] Romero: I'm trying to solve this Rubix Prism. [NPC] Romero: Going to need someone wicked smart to solve it.|
|While having 250-499 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Romero: You're pretty smart. [NPC] Romero: I bet you wish you were smarter, though!|
|While having 500-749 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Romero: While very astute. [NPC] Romero: you don't have quite what it takes for the Prism!|
|While having 750-899 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Romero: Can you somehow get even more intelligence?|
|While having 900-1149 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Romero: I can definitely see it, your brain barely fits your head. [NPC] Romero: Just not quite genius enough to solve the Prism!|
|While having 1150-1290 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Romero: If I was to quantify it, I'd say you need 1,291 Intelligence.|
|While having over 1290 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Romero: Incredible! [NPC] Romero: You solved the Prism! [NPC] Romero: Please, bring it to Juliette and show her how smart I am.|
[Quest Step 8 (Colosseum)]
|Before giving item|[NPC] Romero: Juliette keeps talking to me about this other person. [NPC] Romero: I don't know what's going, they meet over and over! [NPC] Romero: Our love is stronger than theirs! [NPC] Romero: I'm waiting for this other person to show up. [NPC] Romero: I'll need my favorite weapon, can you fetch it for me?|
|While holding [[Aspect of the Jerry]]|[NPC] Romero: Wow.|
|While holding [[Emerald]]|[NPC] Romero: That's not even a weapon! [NPC] Romero: I do like it though!|
|While holding [[Pigman Sword]] or [[Aspect of the Dragons]]|[NPC] Romero: That sword is weak! [NPC] Romero: Besides, would you really give it to me?|
|Upon giving [[Emerald Blade]]|[NPC] Romero: This is exactly what I need! [NPC] Romero: Now I just have to wait for the pretendent. [NPC] Romero: Please bring this poem to Juliette. [NPC] Romero: I hope she'll see the reason.|
[Quest Step 9 (Mushroom Desert)]
|Before giving item|[NPC] Romero: It's you, <player>! [NPC] Romero: I need your help to win Juliette back. [NPC] Romero: My plan is to assemble the greatest bouquet ever! [NPC] Romero: I need a ridiculous amount of flowers.|
|Upon giving [[Flower Minion]]|[NPC] Romero: That's exquisite!! [NPC] Romero: With this, I can get enough flowers to win Juliette back!|
[Quest Step 10 (Jungle Island)]
|Before wearing [[Tuxedo]]|[NPC] Romero: <Player>! You're the best person to turn the day into something memorable! [NPC] Romero: But first, you should wear something nice!|
|After wearing [[Tuxedo]]|[NPC] Romero: Looking slick! [NPC] Romero: You really look stunning! [NPC] Romero: Thank you so much for reuniting Juliette and I. [NPC] Romero: Please, one last time, give this to Juliette.|
[After Quest]
|No condition (Romero emits heart particles during this dialogue)|[NPC] Romero: I love Juliette!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Romero: Fancy seeing you here, friend! Today is a special day for me, as I am seeing Juliette! Think you can help me get her some flowers?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Romero: You're a lifesaver! Today's going to be the best day ever! Well, after my wedding day, that is.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Romero: Just because I see Juliette every day doesn't mean it's not a reason to celebrate.|
## Trivia
- Both Romero and Juliette are a reference to the main characters in the play Romeo and Juliet by William Shakespeare.
- His outfit is likely a reference to Where's Wally/Waldo? (who wears a red and white striped hat and shirt, glasses, and blue pants), and the fact you have to find Romero after every stage of his quest is also likely a reference (even if the method you find him is different than in the puzzle books).

[NPC] No Name: I mean of course it does, because I am a genius! [NPC] No Name: Anyway, aren't you getting tired of this? [NPC] No Name: Talking to a guy in a game? [NPC] No Name: Inside a palace in a cave on a floating island? [NPC] No Name: If you asked me, that makes no sense... [NPC] No Name: How does that even work? [NPC] No Name: Anyway... [NPC] No Name: I think I am gonna get some rest soon. [NPC] No Name: It's getting kind of late. [NPC] No Name: I have a big day tomorrow. [NPC] No Name: Maybe you should go too? [NPC] No Name: I mean it's pretty dark outside. [NPC] No Name: It's better you also go to sleep. [NPC] No Name: Here, take this cake! [NPC] No Name: 1 [NPC] No Name: 0 [NPC] No Name: Wow, you did it! [NPC] No Name: You actually waited this long? [NPC] No Name: Man, you are very dedicated [NPC] No Name: You did this for hours?? |[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Royal Resident: I need one more ingredient to make The Cake for the next person I can get to listen to me.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Royal Resident: This should do it! I've got some counting to do.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Royal Resident: What cake? There is no cake.|
## Tips
- You can hold right click instead of continually clicking the NPC to keep the dialogue going.
  - Once he starts counting down, there is no need to click or hold mouse buttons anymore.
  - The countdown will continue when you rejoin the Dwarven mines.
## Trivia
- The Royal Resident is one of the few NPCs that break the fourth wall, a term used to describe a character separated from its audience, due to it being in a "different world".
  - This is because it is aware that it's on a server and that servers restart.
- Its countdown takes roughly 25,000 seconds, or around 7 hours to complete.
- Despite the reference to server restarts, the player can leave the lobby anytime after the Royal Resident starts counting down from 5,000 and will continue the countdown when the player returns.
- At line 79 it transforms into a cow, and at line 94 into a sheep, followed by revealing that he has no name at line 130.
- 21 lines after becoming "No Name", it will give the player [[The Cake]].
- [[The Cake]] is a reference to the game Portal.
  - It takes around 15–20 minutes to obtain [[The Cake]].
- "Because of you and those meddling kids..." is a reference to a popular children's show, Scooby Doo.
- At the end of the conversation, the [[Achievements|"Royal Conversation" Achievement]] is rewarded to the player.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added Royal Resident.|
|Unknown Date|Added achievement "Royal Conversation" to the end of the dialogue.|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|Royal Resident is now unlocked as a visitor on [[The Garden]] after obtaining [[The Cake]] rather than at the end of the countdown.|

Description: Introduces the player to the [[Trials of Fire]].
Location: [[Trials of Fire]]
Coordinates: -364.5 102.5 -90.5

ID: FIRE_GUY
Ryan is an [[NPC]] located by the [[Trials of Fire]] within the [[Dark Thicket]]. They task the player with completing the [[Trials of Fire]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|[[Dark Thicket]] Failing to sneak up|[NPC] Ryan: Hey, you're not part of our Cult! Get out of here!|
|[[Dark Thicket]] First Interaction|[NPC] Ryan: AAAHHHHH! Don't startle me like that bro! [NPC] Ryan: Y'know, someone as stealthy as you could be a useful member of the Campfire Cult. [NPC] Ryan: What do you say to that? Select an option: [Sure, I guess?]|
|[[Dark Thicket]] After selecting [Sure, I guess?]|[NPC] Ryan: Cool! Anyways, there's just one thing you have to do before you can join. [NPC] Ryan: Stand in the CAMPFIRE! Select an option: [WHAT?]|
|[[Dark Thicket]] After selecting [WHAT?]|[NPC] Ryan: Yeah don't worry, it's not as hot as it looks. [NPC] Ryan: If you can last for 10 seconds while standing in the campfire, I'll give you a reward!|
|[[Dark Thicket]] Before completing the First Trial ([[Trials of Fire]])|[NPC] Ryan: If you can last for 10 seconds while standing in the campfire, I'll give you a reward!|
|[[Dark Thicket]] Completed First Trial ([[Trials of Fire]])|[NPC] Ryan: Wow, you did it! [NPC] Ryan: Nice job, hotshot! I'd give you the Campfire Initiate Badge I right now, but I'm out of wood to make one with... [NPC] Ryan: Sorry about that... [NPC] Ryan: I need to keep watch and make sure nobody blows out the Campfire, but if you can grab me 256 Dark Oak Logs, I'll make one for ya!|
|[[Dark Thicket]] After giving 256x [[Dark Oak Log]]|[NPC] Ryan: Thanks for the wood! Here's your badge, as promised! [NPC] Ryan: A Campfire Initiate Badge I, for our latest initiate, <username>! [NPC] Ryan: I can upgrade that badge for you as you survive more Campfire Trials, you know? [NPC] Ryan: Simply stand in the campfire whenever you think you're ready! [NPC] Ryan: But be careful, though, as each trial burns hotter than the last!|
|[[Dark Thicket]] After claiming the badge ([[Campfire Badge]])|[NPC] Ryan: Wow! You survived the campfire! [NPC] Ryan: The campfire deals true damage, which means it ignores your ❈ Defense! [NPC] Ryan: Try to survive the Trial of Fire II and I'll give you the Campfire Initiate Badge!|
|[[Dark Thicket]] Completed Second Trial ([[Trials of Fire]])|[NPC] Ryan: Incredible! Seems like you got the spark for this! [NPC] Ryan: Here's your Campfire Initiate Badge! [NPC] Ryan: Complete the Trial of Fire III and I'll upgrade your badge!|
|[[Dark Thicket]] If you have another trial to do|[NPC] Ryan: Next step is the Trial of Fire <tier>! (<amount> damage) [NPC] Ryan: Step into the campfire to start the trial and survive for 10s!|
|[[Dark Thicket]] Trying to upgrade the badge ([[Campfire Badge]]) without holding it|[NPC] Ryan: I need you to hold your <badge item name> so I can upgrade it!|
|[[Dark Thicket]] Upgrading the badge ([[Campfire Badge]])|[NPC] Ryan: Congrats! Your <old badge item name> was upgraded to a <new badge item name>! [NPC] Ryan: Try the Trial of Fire Trial <tier> for a greater challenge!|
|[[Dark Thicket]] When you can upgrade the badge ([[Campfire Badge]]) to Adept ([[Campfire Badge]])|[NPC] Ryan: Congrats on completing tier V! [NPC] Ryan: You are ready for the next badge rarity! [NPC] Ryan: To receive it, we require Dark Oak Log x512!|
|[[Dark Thicket]] When you can upgrade the badge ([[Campfire Badge]]) to Cultist ([[Campfire Badge]])|[NPC] Ryan: Congrats on completing tier IX! [NPC] Ryan: You are ready for the next badge rarity! [NPC] Ryan: To receive it, we require Spruce Log x1,064!|
|[[Dark Thicket]] When you can upgrade the badge ([[Campfire Badge]]) to Scion ([[Campfire Badge]])|[NPC] Ryan: Congrats on completing tier XIV! [NPC] Ryan: You are ready for the next badge rarity! [NPC] Ryan: To receive it, we require Enchanted Acacia Log x100!|
|[[Dark Thicket]] When you can upgrade the badge ([[Campfire Badge]]) to God ([[Campfire Badge]])|[NPC] Ryan: Congrats on completing tier XXII! [NPC] Ryan: You are ready for the next badge rarity! [NPC] Ryan: To receive it, we require Enchanted Jungle Log x1,500!|
|[[Dark Thicket]] If you've maxed out the Trial of Fire ([[Trials of Fire]])|[NPC] Ryan: You've completed each Trial of Fire! Congratulations!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Ryan: Welcome, fellow member of the Campfire Cult. It is time to embrace the Campfire within and sacrifice something. What that something should be is an interesting question. I am thinking potato. Yes. Yes, a good baked potato does sound very good right now.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Ryan: Come, my brethren, and let us rejoice!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Ryan: sigh|
## Quest
Ryan is the leader of the campfire cult, where in order to join their cult, you must pass the [[Trials of Fire]]. In the [[Trials of Fire]], the player must survive burning in a fire lasts 10 Seconds, doing varying amounts of [[True Damage]] depending on the level.
The player gets the [[Campfire Badge|Campfire Initiate Badge]] and becomes a member of the campfire cult after passing Level II. Every time the player survives the 10 seconds burning in the campfire, Ryan will upgrade the badge by right-clicking him with the badge in the hand.
## Trivia
- Ryan seems to be a reference to the NBC sitcom "The Office" named after Ryan Howard, a main character on the show. The [[Trials of Fire]] accessory has a tag on the bottom that says "Fire guy!" in quotes. Ryan Howard once started a fire in The Office and was nicknamed Fire Guy.
- When Ryan says the total damage of the upcoming challenge, his number is 10x higher than the actual number. This is due to players having 90% damage reduction .

# Safari Manager
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Safari Manager]
Type: [[NPC]]
[Locations]
Description: Allows the player to access the [[Critter Safari]].
Location: [[Critter Safari Entrance]]
Coordinates: -732.5 99.0625 185.5
Description: Meets the player at the spawn of the [[Critter Safari]].
Location: [[Critter Safari]]
Coordinates: -51.5 68 21.5
Safari Manager is an [[NPC]] located within the [[Critter Safari Entrance]] of the [[Torrhus Canyon]] and the [[Critter Safari]].
## Purpose
The Safari Manager allows entry to the [[Critter Safari]] with the use of [[Safari Tickets]]. They also allow the player to exchange Safari Tickets for higher tiers with [[Forest Essence]].
The [[Attributes|Ticket Discount]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], obtained from syphoning the [[June Bug Shard]], reduces the [[Forest Essence]] cost of exchanging Safari Tickets by 1-10%.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|[[Critter Safari Entrance]] First Interaction (without a [[Safari Belt]])|[NPC] Safari Manager: Welcome to the Critter Safari, amigo! [NPC] Safari Manager: First things first, you'll need a Safari Belt - it's the only item you can bring into the Critter Safari with you. [NPC] Safari Manager: Here, take this one. It's on me!|
|[[Critter Safari Entrance]] First Interaction (with a [[Safari Belt]])|[NPC] Safari Manager: Welcome to the Critter Safari, amigo! [NPC] Safari Manager: Looks like you've got yourself a Safari Belt already, so we can get straight down to business! [NPC] Safari Manager: Give me any tier of Safari Ticket and I'll get you right on into the Critter Safari. [NPC] Safari Manager: Here, why don't you take a look at the experiences we currently have on offer?|
|[[Critter Safari]] First Interaction|[NPC] Safari Manager: I already saw your ticket, so you're free to go.|
|[[Critter Safari]] First Interaction in a Party|[NPC] Safari Manager: I already saw your ticket, so you're free to go. [NPC] Safari Manager: Your friends will need to show me their tickets, though.|
|[[Critter Safari]] Choosing a ticket when warped in|[NPC] Safari Manager: Looks good to me. Have fun out there!|
|[[Critter Safari]] None|[NPC] Safari Manager: You already gave me your ticket, bud. [NPC] Safari Manager: You're free to explore the Critter Safari 'til your heart's content!|
|[[Critter Safari]] Not choosing a ticket for 30 seconds|[NPC] Safari Manager: Dude, if you're not gonna pay, you're not gonna play. [NPC] Safari Manager: You're just standing around doing nothing - I'm sending you back to the Critter Safari Entrance. [NPC] Safari Manager: Come and see me again if you decide you actually want to play next time.|
|[[Critter Safari]] Not choosing a ticket for 30 seconds|The Player will be warped back to the [[Critter Safari Entrance]], and their ticket will be refunded.|
|[[Critter Safari]] Using all the Critter Capsules ([[Critter Capsule]])|[NPC] Safari Manager: Looks like you used up your last Critter Capsule! [NPC] Safari Manager: Would you like to leave the Critter Safari? Select an option: [LEAVE]|

[NPC] Safari Receptionist: He's right over there, and he'll sort out the rest for you. [NPC] Safari Receptionist: You can hear all about her Contests from Miria herself - she lives just down the Spring Path. # Salvage Old Attributes
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Salvage Old Attributes]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Allows the player to salvage [[Attributes]] placed on items.
Location: [[Tangleburg]]
Coordinates: -594.5 114 -17.5
Salvage Old Attributes is an [[NPC]] located in [[Tangleburg]]. They allow the player to salvage [[Attributes]] from [[Armor]], [[Equipment]], and [[Weapons]].
## Salvaging Attributes
Interacting with Salvage Old Attributes opens up a menu to remove [[Attributes]] from items and turn them into [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]]. If the [[Attributes]] salvaged are considered a god roll, then an [[Ananke Feather]] is rewarded as well.
## Dialogue

|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Salvage Old Attributes: Beep boop. [NPC] Salvage Old Attributes: This is a Bio-Anvil. Beep. [NPC] Salvage Old Attributes: If you still have attributes on items, you can use it to transfer them into your Hunting Box. Boop.|

# Scholar Alluin
Aliases: Alluin
Categories: Non-player characters
[Scholar Alluin]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Hints about a bookshelf in the [[Cathedral]] that can be read, which ultimately leads to obtaining the [[Kuudra Relic]].
Location: [[Cathedral]]
Coordinates: -15.5 114 -915.5
Scholar Alluin is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Cathedral]].
## Purpose
Scholar Alluin will hint that there is a book that can be taken out in the [[Cathedral]]. This bookshelf can be found at [[Coordinates|-10, 127, -938]], and will give numbers which will help to find the [[Kuudra Relic]].
